## Summary

The Perception Filter is the presentation-side renderer of hallucination: it answers "what does this surface show the local player" (`perceived(ref, viewer)`) from Sanity's per-player `sanity.perception` output and draws every lie as a gleam, glide, tick or stale-hold, sound request and, at higher stages, a breathing fringe, on the corruptible module-body channel only. It owns the truth-channel tag contract (default TRUTH; the consequence channel, character sprites, beams and the HUD frame it may neither remove, alter nor add to), the pixel geometry and flash safety of every leak at every zoom rung, and the Debug Overlay's true-versus-perceived contrast, while owning no instrument, HUD node, light, block, hook or sound asset. Read it if you build Instruments, the HUD, Audio, Lighting decoys, the Debug Overlay or Accessibility Settings, or if you need to know why no sanity meter, screen effect or clean frame exists; its MVP scope is the class-(b) false-reading lie, with classes (a), (c) and (d), decoys and the container pass designed to depth and stubbed.

> **Quick reference** — Layer: `Feature` · Priority: `MVP` · Key deps: `Command & Simulation Layer (designed), Pixel-Perfect Render Pipeline & Camera (designed), 2D Lighting & Occlusion (designed), Sanity & Hallucination Rules (designed), Life-Support Graph (designed), Suit & Equipment (designed)`

## Overview

The Perception Filter is the only place in the game where the picture is allowed to be wrong, and it is built so that it can never be wrong cleanly. In one sentence: it is a presentation-only service, with no simulation state, hook, block or mutator of its own, that takes what Sanity says a seat is lied to about (`sanity.perception`), wraps the true value of exactly the surfaces the world has named lie-capable, and hands back what that seat *displays*; around that it draws a small closed set of **leaks** (a violet gleam on a lying glass, a breathing band on the lamp's rim, an outline on a crewmate) that make every lie falsifiable at a glance, and it enforces, by default and by lint, that everything it is not named to touch is pixel-for-pixel the sane world. It owns no instrument node, no HUD node, no light, no sound asset and no camera: Instruments and the HUD *ask* it for the displayed value of a thing and draw the result (`perceived(ref, viewer)`); Lighting draws the decoy overlays from a list it publishes; Adaptive Audio plays the sounds whose ids it names; the Debug Overlay reads the true value beside its answer.

**The routed thread and the reading adopted (stated on page one).** The routing for this system reads: *"A PERCEPTION_OUT class block (kernel CR-12: its output never feeds the simulation; never persisted)."* Read against the kernel, that is satisfied exactly and only as follows. The kernel's own definition of the class (CR-12) is "Sanity's per-player 'what lies to show' output", the kernel's Perception Filter row (C.3 row 11) grants the Filter "Subscriptions only ... Neither gets submit or mutators", and a `PRESENTATION` subscriber cannot write a block (H3, H4). So **the Filter registers no kernel block, no step hook and no mutator.** The `PERCEPTION_OUT` block of the thread is `sanity.perception`, which the Filter *consumes*; what the Filter *publishes* (the `Perceived` records, the lie trajectories, the per-viewer decoy list, the rim and outline state) is a presentation-side **perceived channel** that inherits every `PERCEPTION_OUT` rule by lint: never read by any simulation system, never persisted, never in `state_hash` (KERN D.12). Stated in the language of the kernel: the Filter is the second consumer channel of CR-10, "the Perception Filter reads true state and publishes what a client perceives; presentation nodes read the filtered channel", and nothing more. (orchestrator ruling R1; ADR candidate: Filter channel architecture and the "no block" ruling.)

**The data layer.** Per local seat, keyed by PlayerId (never a transport peer ID), the Filter holds only *presentation-local* state, rebuilt from the formula on a hard start and never saved: one slewing envelope per stage-bound effect (the own-cone rim fringe, the stage-3 hue bleed, the gleam floor *(Amended 2026-10-27 — user rulings)*), one small per-lie record (a return-follower value, a breath cycle counter, a tick phase), a per-lie hash draw, and a tag registry mapping presentation scene roots to a grade. It reads: `sanity.perception` (local owner only, `OWNER_PRIVATE`), the SIM view `sanity_view(player_id) -> {stage, tell_on, harm_eligible}`, Graph `true_value(kind, id)` and Suit `true_value(key, player_id)` (it is the only non-diagnostic caller of either), the kernel tick, `tick_alpha` and the Pipeline's `present_time`. It never reads `sanity.state` or `sanity.ledger`. It writes `CanvasItem` members, `Material` and shader parameters, particle members and audio *requests*, and nothing else: no real light's member, no camera or viewport property, no simulation state. Its API is one pull query, `perceived(ref, viewer) -> Perceived`, whose cheap honest path (no lie on `ref`) returns the true value with leak amplitude 0, so even honest readings pass through the one wrap point and the Debug Overlay has one place to hook.

**The tag contract (the load-bearing idea).** Every presentation node under the world layer carries a grade, and the **default is TRUTH**: **TRUTH_PX** (bit-identical pixels with the Filter on or off), **TRUTH_ST** (state, shape, position and existence never moved, added or removed), **LEAK** (truth by construction: the leaks themselves, which no other lie can fake or suppress) or **CORRUPTIBLE** (a named module-body surface that carries a `lie_surface_id` present in Sanity's `lie_targets.json`). The **consequence channel** is a closed list (trail VFX, the Graph's `acoustic` classes at descriptor anchors, Suit body cues, lamp brightness from `served_pm`, the `AIRFLOW` draught, the `SEAL_STRAIN` strain) that the Filter "may neither remove, alter nor add"; crewmate beams, character sprites and the HUD frame are immune; the **module-body channel** (sprites, gauges, inspect cards; WORN descriptors from VS-A) is the corruptible set *(Amended 2026-10-27 — re-review, delegated ruling G2)*. The failure mode of omission is therefore "a lie vanishes", never "the truth breaks" (design test, section Player Fantasy).

**The player-facing effect.** The player never sees a number, a bar, a ring, a vignette or a word for any of this. They meet it as a gauge they have read a hundred times whose needle will not rest and whose glass edge carries a faint violet gleam; as a quiet sound under that needle; later as a lamp-housing glow that says lit over a floor the lamp is not lighting, and, while such a lie runs, a thin violet band riding the rim of their own beam (lie-bound by default, user ruling X11); later still as the whole machine sprite showing a state the room contradicts (violet at the edge of a pipe silhouette is a default-off, playtest-gated opt-in) *(Amended 2026-10-27 — user rulings)*. Each of the three leak modes (the gleam, the glide that never rests, the sound) is sufficient on its own (HR-5), so a player who cannot tell the hue, cannot hear, or cannot read a slow needle still finds the liar. Nothing else on screen changes: not the light the lamp throws, not the frost on the duct, not the hiss, not a friend's beam or voice, not the walls, not the camera. Violet appears only at an edge and only means the player's own mind.

**What the game loses without it.** Sanity's MVP class (b) has nothing to draw: "no MVP-live criterion can render a lie" until this GDD exists (Sanity's own words). Pillar 4's secondary design test ("-10% move speed vs. a gauge showing a false value -> the false gauge") would have no gauge; the Graph's falsifiability table and Suit's chargeless scanner would have nothing to be a witness *against*; and Pillar 1's closed lists would have no enforcement point, only a convention each renderer could forget. The core hypothesis's fifth requirement (a single hallucination class, false system state, falsifiable by the physical world) cannot be tested without it. The index also rates it a Technical high risk ("Per-client divergence is indistinguishable from desync without tooling"); the mitigation is part of this mandate: the perceived channel is presentation-only, and the Debug Overlay (True Values) is built against the contract in section Detailed Design.

**MVP scope.** The MVP draws class (b) only (`CLASSES_ENABLED` MVP value {b}): the lying needle, digital readout, inspect card, state glyph, lamp-housing glow sprite and whole-machine sprite frame, each tri-modal; plus the first-RUNNING-frame guarantee, the tag contract and its lints. Conditional (ruled 2026-10-27 *(Amended 2026-10-27 — user rulings)*): the stage-2 own-cone rim fringe, shipped lie-bound by default (OQ-SN-21 answered, OQ-PF-1) and the stage-3 edge hue bleed, shipped default-off (OQ-SN-22 answered, OQ-PF-2; cut first). Designed to the same depth and exercised with stubs: classes (a), (c) and (d), the decoy list (`GLOW_EXTENSION`, `PHANTOM_SHADOW`), suit-reading lies (`READING_LIE_STAGE` defaults to never; all five suit keys honest, SE-29), scanner lies, the class-(c) victim tell and monsterised view, the whole-image container pass. **Stage count confirmed: four stages, 0 to 3, there is no stage 4**; stage 3 is the Filter's maximum (answers PIPE:1048 and PC:1092).

**Index wording reconciled.** The index rationale (IDX bullet 20) is honoured with the corrections below, each recorded as a refresh owed (Cross-References) and never as an edit to another document.

| The index says | This GDD does | Why | Refresh owed |

**Scope guard.** The Filter never decides *whether*, *when* or *for whom* something lies (Sanity), never draws a decoy node, a light or a shadow (Lighting), never plays a sound or states whether audio can lie (Adaptive Audio), never owns a needle, digit mechanism, card, plate, strip or prompt (Instruments, HUD, Interaction), never poses a real horror (Horror poses its own rigs, with `leak` 0; the Filter, per HE-40, "may feed phantom or crewmate-driven poses": pose streams only for phantoms (VS-A) and for class-(c) substitutions driven by the crewmate's true velocity, plus the whole-body substitution or omission), never draws or reuses an Attention tell, and never moves a camera, a chest point or a beam.

## Player Fantasy

*Relation to Sanity's fantasy.* Sanity & Hallucination Rules owns the fantasy of the verification economy: "Your eyes are an instrument you cannot recalibrate." The Filter must neither restate nor compete with it. It owns the other half of the same moment, at a finer grain: **what a lie looks like at the pixel, and what never moves.** Sanity decides *that* a gauge lies and *when*; the Filter decides that the lie has a seam, where the seam sits (the edge), and that everything outside the seam is bit-identical to the sane world. Sanity's player learns *when to doubt*; the Filter's player learns *how to see the doubt*. The Filter is not a second sanity system. (Framing choice, Category Gameplay: direct framing A below, with B as its corollary and C as its register; orchestrator ruling, logged.)

**Every lie shows its seam.** The picture can lie; it cannot lie *cleanly*. A lying needle glides and never rests; a violet gleam sits on its glass edge; a quiet sound runs under it. Each of the three is enough on its own (ART HR-5). Everything the Filter is not named to touch (the light your lamp throws, the frost on the duct, the hiss, your friend's beam and voice, the walls, the camera) is pixel-for-pixel the sane world. The player's power is not resistance to the lie. It is **literacy**: learning that violet only ever appears at an edge, that it only ever means their own mind, and that the edge is always there.

*Corollary (framing B, "the frame you can trust"): and the frame never moves.* The player learns the anchors (the beam and the pool it makes, the trails, the sounds the ship makes about itself, the crew, the HUD frame, the camera) and reads everything else as suspect.
**Named pillar tension: P1 (the seam must exist) against P4 (a lie must cost something to disprove).** *(Amended 2026-10-27 — design review)* The gleam is mandatory, always on and never below the stage-indexed floor `floor_eff` (`AMP_FLOOR_PM` 400 at stages 0 to 1, 250 at stage 3). On a binary surface (FALSE_OK, FALSE_FAIL, a housing, a glyph, a SPRITE_SWAP) a player who has learned what violet means can read both *where* the lie is and *what the truth is* (invert the reading), which turns P4's "costly falsification" into a search for violet pixels and makes the room witness redundant. This is a real tension and the review's most important design-level finding. **Resolved 2026-10-27 — user ruling (design-review decisions, D74):** lever (a) is adopted, the gleam floor falls toward 250 with stage (400, 325 and 250 per mille at stages 0 to 1, 2 and 3; derived in D.5), so a learned violet is a weaker inversion key as the stage rises. Lever (b) (`MODE_DROP_PM` for SOUND at stage 3) is not turned on and lever (c) (accepting the tension openly) is not the ruling; (b) and (c) remain the fallback if playtest signal V7 (at least 60 % of players who notice violet still verify) fails. *(Amended 2026-10-27 — user rulings)*

*Register (framing C, "the ship dreams warmer than it is"):* lie *flavour* is comfort first (a needle resting in OK, a lamp housing lit over a cold floor), following Sanity's `KIND_W`; the tell lives in the almost-right. It is the register, not the spine: the "cone seems to reach further" lie is now Lighting's `GLOW_EXTENSION` decoy overlay (VS-A), and the true reveal radius never changes.

**Emotion target, in order across a session:** *familiarity* (the gauge read a hundred times) -> *the snag* (something about the edge is off) -> *suspicion with a direction* (the seam says *where*, not *what*) -> *quiet certainty* (the room answers) -> *trained eye* (next time the snag is faster). The Filter never contributes fear on its own; fear belongs to Attention (P2, P3) and Horror. The Filter contributes **doubt with a seam** (ART "Doubt, not horror").

**Power fantasy, stated precisely.** The player cannot stop being lied to (their sanity is not theirs to protect), but they can become *the kind of person who is not fooled*. Competence is measured in seconds-to-snag, not in a number. This is the self-determination Competence lever for the whole of Pillar 4. **MDA:** Discovery (finding the seam) and Challenge (reading it under load) are the Filter's aesthetics; Fantasy ("the only ones awake") is supported by the anchors never moving. **The Filter adds no Sensation of its own:** no lie is pleasurable to look at for its own sake (no shimmer showcase, no screen-wide treatment), and the sane world's Sensation (light as a resource, flow-coded loops glowing in the dark) is untouched, because every light member, pipe emission and chevron is pixel-identical to the sane world (TRUTH_PX). The concept's Sensation row is therefore served by the world the Filter leaves alone, not by the Filter. **Fellowship** (concept priority 3) is not served at MVP; it is served at VS-B through the class-(c) victim tell, where the saner viewer becomes the instrument for a friend ("I am the saner instrument now", Deferred anchor below).

### Anchor moment (MVP, solo, class b, stage 1): "The needle with a violet rim"

The Filter's half of Sanity's anchor "The needle that won't hold still". Sanity owns *that* it lies and *when*; this table owns *what is drawn*.

| t | What the Filter draws | What stays bit-identical (TRUTH_PX) | Player read |
|---|---|---|---|
| 0 s | the lie is PENDING (`ONSET_DELAY_RUNS` 10 to 25, Sanity's): **nothing** is drawn; the gauge is honest with its normal settle and tremor (Instruments, provisional) | everything | none |
| about 2 to 5 s later | ACTIVE. `perceived(GRAPH_NODE(...), viewer)` returns `motion_mode` GLIDE; the needle leaves its honest rest on the glide path (0.5 to 2 deg/s on the dial arc, micro-wander below 0.5 Hz, never rests, ART 7.3); a violet `#B07BE6` gleam on the glass edge only (violet at an edge, never a fill); the sound signature begins **no later than** the glide. A first lie carries all three modes (SN-15 L-L) | the duct sprite's pipe emission and chevrons, the HISS at the descriptor anchor, mist at the pinhole, the lamp's floor pool, the breath step and visor mist (Suit body cues) | "That needle's not right." |
| the player walks 8 tiles (about 3.2 s at `V_WALK` 2 px per tick, `TICK_RATE` 60, `TILE_PX` 48; Sanity's figure) | the lie continues unchanged; its trajectory is a function of the lie's sim-time age, so it reads the same wherever the player is | the HISS gets louder by distance (Audio, truth), the draught | "The room says leak." |
| on repair or end | the lie ends on the **glide path** back to truth at `GLIDE_RETURN_RATE`; the gleam follows the remaining displayed-minus-true discrepancy; identical for EXPIRED, INVALIDATED and DEBUNKED (SN-16). No flash, no relief cue, no mark on the checked gauge | everything | nothing is announced; the player simply knows |

**What this moment proves.** In the frame where the needle lies, the only non-sane pixels on screen are the needle's position, the gleam band on the glass rim, and (as audio) one sound class from the Filter's id namespace. A designer can count them. If a fourth thing changed, the Filter has failed.

### Second anchor (MVP, solo, stage 2): "The warm housing over a cold floor"

The stage where "the light lies" (SN stage 2). The Filter's stage-2 reach is the lamp-housing glow sprite and the state glyph, plus the own-cone rim fringe (MVP-conditional; lie-bound by default, drawn only while a reach lie is ACTIVE, user ruling X11) *(Amended 2026-10-27 — user rulings)*.

| What the player sees | What is true | Why it is fair |
|---|---|---|
| a lamp housing's glow sprite shows its lit frame, warm, with a violet gleam at the housing edge (FALSE_OK on the housing appearance frame, SN-16) | the fixture is starved: `served_pm` is low, so the **floor light pool is dim** (lamp brightness from `served_pm`, a consequence-channel item, TRUTH_PX). The housing is a sprite; the pool is light | two things in one frame disagree, and the one that is light wins. "The physical radius their light actually reveals" is the anchor that never shifts |
| while a stage-2 reach lie (the housing or glyph above) is ACTIVE, a thin breathing violet band rides the rim of the player's own beam (`RIM_FRINGE_PX` 2 art px `[PLACEHOLDER]`; breathes 4 to 6 s; never locked to `tell_beat`) *(Amended 2026-10-27 — user rulings)* | the beam's colour `#FFE2A8`, shape, reach and true reveal radius are unchanged | the band is the diegetic self-sign: "my own light has a violet edge" = "my eyes are the instrument now". It sits *beside* the beam, never on it |

**Player read:** "The housing says on. The floor says no." This is the moment the player learns that a *glow sprite* is module-body and a *light pool* is world. It is the most important literacy lesson the Filter teaches, and it is why the true reveal radius and every light member are untouchable. If the rim fringe is cut (second in the cut order), this anchor still works through the housing gleam alone.

### Deferred anchor (VS-B, class c, saner viewer): "The friend with the violet edge"

From the saner viewer's side, a crewmate's silhouette carries a 2-art-px violet fringe outside the Void Steel contour and along both beam-cone edges, breathing 4 to 6 s, unlit, silent, casting no light. Fantasy line: **"I am the saner instrument now."** The viewer becomes rung 3 of Sanity's ladder for a friend. From the monsterised actor's side (the viewer's own `harm_eligible` set, read from SIM, no copy): the friend is a whole-body horror rig that **walks like a technician** at true position and velocity, carrying their **true Tungsten beam from the true chest point** and speaking in their **true voice** (SN-20). The seam on a monsterised crewmate is not a gleam but the three things a horror cannot have: their beam, their voice, their walk. This deepens framing A rather than replacing it: the seam grows from a 2-px glass edge to a whole human signature. The tell is rendered for every saner viewer **including a viewer who is themself `tell_on`**: its absence would be a self-sign.

### Pillar text served (verbatim)

- **P1 The World Never Lies:** "Three channels are **inviolable** — no hallucination may ever alter them: Physical consequences. ... The sane palette. Flesh tones and violet never appear in the sane world, and every hallucination leaks them somewhere — a fringe, a flicker, a tint at the edge of the light. Subtle is allowed; absent is not. ... The human channel. Voice/proximity chat and a crewmate's own light beam (colour and shape) are never faked. A monster that speaks in your friend's voice, or carries their torch, is your friend." Design test: "a hidden stat vs. a visibly leaking pipe → the pipe."
- **P4 Sanity Rewrites Reality:** "Low sanity changes what the player perceives, not just their stats." Class (b): "Broken reads as fixed, fixed as broken; the lie extends from the gauge to the sprite. The physical consequence (P1) stays honest." "**No lie is unfalsifiable** ... At least one channel of truth always remains." Design test: "a hallucination that can never be disproved vs. one that costs time, risk or trust to disprove → the costly one." Secondary: "−10% move speed vs. a gauge showing a false value → the false gauge."
- **Intended tension (concept):** "Pillar 1 (legible) vs. Pillar 4 (lying). Resolved inside the game world: the physical ship is ground truth; instruments and perception are what can be wrong. The tension is the game." The Filter is **the physical location of that resolution**: P4 writes only inside the corruptible tag; P1 is everything else by default.
- **P2 Dread Before Chaos** (served by abstention): "Every breach is preceded by a tell a careful player could have perceived." The Filter never adds, removes, delays or alters a tell.
- **P3 The Web Watches** (served by abstention): "Attention is shown in the world ... never as a hidden meter." The Filter is never an Attention input and never draws Attention state.

### Design test sentences (this system's own)

**Primary (the design test of this GDD):**

> **When a lie could be made more convincing by hiding its seam vs. more intense by adding another seamed lie -> add a lie; keep the seam.**

This is the user's INTENSE decision (count, kind, reach) reconciled with the art bible's "Doubt, not horror" (per-lie tempo and loudness) at the Filter's grain. It answers every "make the hallucination better" request: intensity is bought with Sanity's dials (`LIES_MAX`, `LIE_GAP_S`, `ONSET_PM`, `STAGE_ENTER`), never with a quieter seam or a louder screen.

**Corollaries:**

1. "When a presentation element could be corrupted vs. left exact -> **exact**, unless it is a named module-body surface in `lie_targets.json`." (default TRUTH)
2. "When a lie could be smoother vs. leak more -> **leak**." (the SN-15 L-L floor: never below two modes at stages 2 and 3, three on the first lie)
3. "When a stage change could announce itself (a flash, a pulse, a relief cue) vs. arrive like weather -> **weather**."

*Worked test.* A future request: "Stage 3 should feel scarier: add a slow red pulse to the whole screen." The primary test: a pulse is not a seamed lie, it is a mood. Corollary 3: it announces the stage. Guard rails: no full-frame effect (PIPE PR-10, PR-13), no Alarm Red in a lie (ART 4.4), no sanity surface. **Rejected.** Counter-offer within the pillars: raise `LIES_MAX` at stage 3 (Sanity's knob), or ship the cuttable stage-3 edge hue bleed.

### How the fantasy deepens, not breaks, under hallucination

The **seam grammar is constant across every stage**; only **reach** and **count** grow. A player who learned the edge at stage 1 is never betrayed by stage 3. That constancy keeps competence intact while the picture gets worse.

| Stage (0 to 3 confirmed; no stage 4) | What the Filter may reach | The seam the player reads | What stays the anchor | Literacy taught |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| 0 SANE | nothing | none; violet appears nowhere | everything | the baseline: what the ship looks like when it is honest |
| 1 INSTRUMENTS *(Amended 2026-10-27 — re-review, delegated ruling G2)* | needle, digital readout, inspect card (same instrument as its wall gauge, IA-38, HR-14); WORN descriptors are VS-A | gleam on glass + glide + sound (all three on the first lie) | the room (trails, acoustic classes, draught), Suit body cues | "look at the edge, then at the room" |
| 2 LIGHT | + lamp-housing glow sprite, state glyph | + own-cone rim fringe on the player's beam rim, while a reach lie is ACTIVE (MVP-conditional, lie-bound default) *(Amended 2026-10-27 — user rulings)* | the light pool, the true reveal radius, the beam itself | "a glow sprite is not light" |
| 3 WORLD (the Filter's maximum) | + whole machine sprite state frames (SPRITE_SWAP; both honest frames required) | + edge hue bleed on pipe silhouettes (cuttable; default-off opt-in) *(Amended 2026-10-27 — user rulings)*; every lie still carries at least two modes | pipe emission, chevrons and shape; wall and seam geometry (grade ST); trails; beams; crewmates; the camera | "the whole machine can lie; the room cannot" |

**No screen-level event at stage 1** (SNV-1). No tint, vignette, shake, blur, chromatic aberration, desaturation, grade, scan-line or glitch at any stage. A stage change arrives over `STAGE_RAMP_S` (8 s `[PLACEHOLDER]`), amplitude-monotone, never a pulse.

**INTENSE (user decision, binding) survives this restraint.** At stage 3 with `LIES_MAX` 4, four lies can run at once across gauges, glyphs and whole machine sprites; the player's ship can look half-broken or half-healed while the room tells a different story. That is intense in the user's sense (count, kind, reach). It is never intense in tempo. A player who calls stage 3 "not scary enough" is routed to Sanity's dials, not to a louder Filter. Classes (a), (c) and (d) carry the phantom-monster, crewmates-as-monsters and phantom-loot intensity at the Vertical Slice; the MVP intensity is deliberately the single-class core hypothesis (concept, MVP Definition item 5). This is an identity-level expectation gap against the user's binding INTENSE decision, which names phantom monsters, teammates as monsters and phantom loot: at MVP **only class (b) ships**, and intensity comes only from count, kind and reach. It is therefore not left as a playtest question: it goes to the user as an explicit briefing and acceptance, **a user decision before MVP sprint planning** (risk CD-F5; OQ-PF-31), with the counter-offer attached (raise `LIES_MAX` at stage 3, or enable the stage-3 edge hue bleed opt-in, PF-24). *(Amended 2026-10-27 — design review)* The briefing also states how intense a *single* lie is: a lie departs over up to `GLIDE_REACH_FRAC` of its life and only the time with displayed-minus-true at or above `D_VIS_MIN_DEG` deceives, measured by the dev metric `deceptive_dwell_frac` (V9). **Resolved 2026-10-27 — user ruling (design-review decisions, D77): the user accepts that only class (b) ships at MVP (intensity from count, kind and reach, and a single lie's intensity as V9 measures it); the stub ACs of PF-27 to PF-30 stay `[MVP-stub]` and become `[MVP-live]` only if a later playtest verdict declines the MVP intensity (OQ-PF-31).** *(Amended 2026-10-27 — user rulings)*

**Death inside the fantasy.** Death is lethal and, by user decision, handled in the MVP as restore from the last autosave (owned by Save/Load: "This is like project zomboid, death is a bitch. It happens."). The Filter's contribution is the **no clean frame** rule: the first RUNNING frame after LOADING is already filtered, with ramps initialised steady. One unfiltered frame would be a free oracle and would turn death into a sanity peek. Glide and breath *phase* may differ after restore; the displayed *value* does not.

### Tone and language

*Register:* the art bible's, concrete, physical, sparse. The Filter is written as **a set of named seams and a list of things that never move**, not as an effect stack. *Preferred nouns:* seam, edge, gleam, glide, rim, fringe, leak, mode, reach, anchor, witness, surface, frame, the glass, the room, the pool (of light), weather. *Preferred verbs:* draw, leave exact, leak, glide, breathe, reach, return (on the glide path), hold. *Banned in prose describing the player experience and in any player-facing string* (extends Sanity's list, which applies verbatim): meter, bar, sanity gauge, debuff, insanity effect, jump scare, reveal, "you were hallucinating", "the truth is revealed", cure, and the Filter-specific glitch, distortion, screen effect, post-process effect, "insanity filter", vignette, tunnel vision, VHS, CRT, static, "the screen warps", "reality bends" (these name screen-wide treatments the pillars forbid; using them in prose primes an implementer to build one). Permitted technical vocabulary in rule text only: `ShaderMaterial`, `CanvasItem`, mask bit, per-element material, container slot. **Naming:** "the Perception Filter" or "the Filter" in documents; there is no player-facing name for it and characters never acknowledge it.

### Reference techniques

| Reference | ADOPT (exact technique) | REJECT (exact technique) and why |
|---|---|---|
| *Beyond the Aquila Rift* | lie flavour is comfort: warm, soft, domestic over cold steel; the tell lives in the almost-right | the single reveal shot that strips the illusion at once; the Filter has no reveal register (lies end on the glide path) |
| *Pandorum* | the hands-free chest lamp as the player's one truth tool: its pool and reveal radius are anchors the Filter cannot touch | delusion as plot twist or authored reveal (anti-pillar: no authored story) |
| *Project Zomboid* | death as story material; restore from autosave with no ceremony; the zoom ladder, where leak geometry is defined in art px and verified at every rung | any level-of-detail that culls a leak at far zoom ("a culled leak is an unleaked lie") |
| *I'm on Observation Duty* / *Exit 8* (anomaly spotting) | baseline literacy: the player learns the honest look of a ship (stage 0) so precisely that a 2-px violet edge snags; stage 0 is the Filter's teacher | the report-the-anomaly verdict (a button, a confirmation, a score): a verdict icon in disguise |
| *Papers, Please* | discrepancy between two documents as the core read: gauge vs room, housing vs pool | rule-book UI that names the discrepancy for the player |
| *Return of the Obra Dinn* | truth is deduced from what the world shows, never stated by an interface | confirmation-in-threes UI (a verdict system) |
| *Darkwood* | the view cone as the honest extent of knowledge (our true reveal radius) | darkening vignette and screen-edge treatments |
| *Amnesia*, *Eternal Darkness*, *Don't Starve*, *Sanitarium*-type sanity post-processing | the fear of a state you cannot see | screen wobble, blur, chromatic aberration, vignette, desaturation, fake-crash or fake-save-deletion fourth-wall effects (save and pause surfaces are never a hallucination class; the HUD frame is an anchor) |
| *Hyper Light Drifter* / modern HD pixel art | hard 1-art-px-multiple edges; leaks drawn at world resolution so they stay pixel-true | screen-resolution post bloom or aberration over the upscaled image (softens the band) |
| *Alien: Isolation* | tells precede threats (P2) | phantom tells or false calm (AT-17: permanently rejected) |
| Apollo console / *Satisfactory* | state lives in the sprite (frame, needle, numeral): exactly the corruptible module-body channel | icon-only state; any verdict glyph on a checked gauge |

### Anti-pillar and guard-rail statements this system carries

| # | Guard rail | Binds |
|---|---|---|
| PF-G1 | **Not a combat game.** A phantom never harms, collides, blocks, casts light, raises Attention or appears in `emitter.state`; a Filter lie is never a reason a tool works or fails | PF-27, PF-30 |
| PF-G2 | **Not intentional PvP.** Class (c) is a render substitution gated by the viewer's own SIM bit `harm_eligible` (no copy); it is never an option, setting or input another player can trigger. The Filter adds no attribution to the player; web attribution lives in Sanity's `incidents` ring and Scars | PF-28, PF-29 |
| PF-G3 | **Not an authored story.** The Filter draws no scripted image, no authored hallucination, no staged sequence; every drawn lie is a pure function (modulo presentation-local state) of a `sanity.perception` entry, the true value, sim time, `present_time` and the viewer | PF-11, PF-13 |
| PF-G4 | **Not a game you win.** No mastery surface for seam-spotting: no counter of lies spotted, no achievement, no "perception" skill | PF-38 |
| PF-G5 | **No sanity surface.** No meter, bar, ring, number, icon, colour, vignette anywhere; the only violet in screen space is a gleam on a lying reading's glass (HUD, provisional), plus two *named conditional exceptions*, ruled 2026-10-27: the own-cone rim fringe, which ships lie-bound (drawn only while a reach lie is ACTIVE, so it is a leak of a real lie and not a standing stage indicator) and the stage-3 pipe-edge bleed, which ships default-off as a playtest-gated opt-in (OQ-PF-1, OQ-PF-2, PF-23, PF-24) *(Amended 2026-10-27 — user rulings)*; no self-tell on the player's body. The own-cone rim fringe sits on the beam rim, not the body (its tension with IA-39 is answered by the lie-bound default, OQ-SN-21) | PF-23, PF-38 |
| PF-G6 | **The consequence channel is closed.** The Filter may "neither remove, alter nor ADD" (no lie adds scorch, mist, drip, frost, puddle, sparks or a hiss) | PF-5 |
| PF-G7 | **The human channel and the frame never move.** Crewmate beams (colour, shape, chest origin), character sprites (whole-body substitution only, never pixel edits), voice, chat, speaker indicator, pings, HUD frame, camera framing | PF-5, PF-36 |
| PF-G8 | **Every lie leaks, and always in violet.** Non-empty `leak_modes` that always contains GLEAM (the palette mode: P1's "absent is not" allowed) and the motion mode; only SOUND is ever droppable; never fewer than two at stages 2 and 3, three on the first lie; level-of-detail may reduce amplitude detail, never remove a mode | PF-15, PF-34 |
| PF-G9 | **No verdict, no relief.** No debunk flash, banner, icon, Alarm Red, "safe" colour, mark on a checked surface or relief cue on a stage drop | PF-14, PF-21 |
| PF-G10 | **No clean frame.** The first RUNNING frame after LOADING and after any `force_stage` or `force_level` is already filtered | PF-33 |
| PF-G11 | **Inside the flash bound pair.** `FLASH_MAX_PER_S` 3 per rolling 1 s per region (a ninth of the displayed picture at the current field, LO-22) **and** HR-13 (no HUD luminance change above 3 Hz) | PF-32 |
| PF-G12 | **The Filter decides how, never whether.** It never chooses what lies, when, or for whom (Sanity), never draws a decoy node (Lighting), never plays a sound (Audio), owns no instrument or HUD node | PF-1, PF-3 |

### We will know this was right if

All thresholds are `[PLACEHOLDER]` signals for the playtest plan (owner: creative-director), not acceptance criteria.

| # | Signal | Target | Failure reading |
|---|---|---|---|
| V1 | seconds from the first ACTIVE frame of a stage-1 lie to the player looking at or walking toward the subject's room witness, among players who notice | median at most 30 s by the third lie of a session | seam too quiet (raise gleam amplitude within ART bounds; never add a mode outside the three) |
| V2 | post-session phrasing, unprompted: "I should have looked at the edge" vs "it wasn't really broken / the game cheated" | at least 3:1 | seam not legible, or a witness missing (Sanity L-W) |
| V3 | players who describe a **screen** effect ("it went weird / blurry / glitchy") | 0 | something outside the corruptible set changed: a P1 breach, investigate as a defect |
| V4 | players who can say what violet means after one session without being told | at least 70 % | violet appears somewhere outside a lie (palette leak in the sane world) |
| V5 | at stage 2, players who distinguish the housing glow from the floor pool on first encounter | at least 50 %, rising to at least 80 % by the second | pool and housing contrast too low; art-director ruling |
| V6 | stage onset noticed as "a moment" (players can name when it changed) | under 20 % | the ramp announces itself; lengthen `STAGE_RAMP_S` |
| V7 *(Amended 2026-10-27 — design review)* *(Amended 2026-10-27 — re-review)* | among players who notice violet, the share who still verify against the room (a second instrument, a hiss, a draught, a scan) before acting on a binary-surface reading, reported against the trough-to-peak dwell of the stage where the lie was seen (0.268, 0.341, 0.392 of a breath cycle below half amplitude; the peak is 1000 at every stage on binary surfaces, D.5) | at least 60 % | violet has become an inversion key and the room witness redundant: the OQ-PF-37 ruling (the stage-indexed gleam floor, D.5) is in force; if V7 still fails, apply levers (b) and (c) of OQ-PF-37 *(Amended 2026-10-27 — user rulings)* |
| V8 *(Amended 2026-10-27 — design review)* | testers who mute or lower the lie sound within the first 30 minutes of play | at most 20 % | the sound masks consequence sounds or habituates into irritation: tighten the PF-20 level contract before cutting the mode |
| V9 *(Amended 2026-10-27 — design review)* | `deceptive_dwell_frac`, a dev counter: the share of a lie's life with displayed-minus-true at or above `D_VIS_MIN_DEG` | median at least 0.60, reported at each playtest review (OQ-PF-31 accepted 2026-10-27) *(Amended 2026-10-27 — user rulings)* | lies spend too little of their life deceiving; MVP intensity is carried by count alone |

## Detailed Design

### Core Rules

*Conventions.* Rules are named `PF-n`; each states a **Rule**, a **Why** and **Edge notes**. Formulas are `D.n` (Formulas section, written in part 2 of this document; the rules below fix *what* each formula must compute and its binding constants, and name it). Every pixel measure is in **art px of the 640x360 base picture**; on screen it is `k` times larger at integer zoom `k` in `[1, k_auto + 2]` (`k_auto = floor(min(W/640, H/360))`, PIPE PR-1, C6). Registry constants are cited by **name and registry value**; a number marked `[PLACEHOLDER]` is invented here, carries a range and a proposed registered name, and is final nowhere (reflexion: a placeholder never becomes a fact). Upstream facts are cited by rule id first (`SN-15 L-L`, `HE-40`). Anything about Accessibility Settings or Co-op Session is **(provisional — undesigned)**; HUD & Suit Layer, In-World Instruments & Inspect View, Debug Overlay (True Values) and Adaptive Audio & Tells have since been designed and their answers are recorded in Interactions I-B and OQ-PF-29 (2026-10-27 consistency pass). For the undesigned neighbours: this GDD places a requirement on the neighbour and describes what the Filter *returns*, never the neighbour's internals. There is no `scripting.md` or `core.md` in `docs/engine-reference/godot/`: GDScript integer-overflow, `Dictionary` ordering, `RefCounted` lifetime and per-call cost are **not sourceable** and are marked `[UNV]` wherever a rule depends on them; the reference also carries nothing on `canvas_item` shader instance parameters, container materials or viewport masking, so every shading-language mechanism is a `[UNV]` spike item, never the basis of a rule.

#### A. Architecture, channels and the tag contract

**PF-1. Channel architecture: consume, do not own.**
*Rule.* The Filter registers no kernel block, no step hook and no mutator (KERN CR-3, CR-8, CR-9). The `PERCEPTION_OUT` block of the routed thread is `sanity.perception`, which the Filter consumes. The Filter's **perceived channel** (the `Perceived` records, the lie trajectories, the per-viewer decoy list, the rim and outline state) is a presentation-side surface that inherits every `PERCEPTION_OUT` rule: no simulation system reads it (kernel H5), it is never persisted, it is never part of `state_hash` (KERN D.12). KERN C.3 row 11 grants the Filter "Subscriptions only ... Neither gets submit or mutators"; it subscribes as `PRESENTATION`.
*Why.* A `PRESENTATION` subscriber cannot write a block (H3, H4), and the kernel defines the class as Sanity's output. Stating the reading on page one keeps a reviewer from reading the thread literally.
*Edge notes.* Lighting's row says "a per-client `PERCEPTION_OUT` decoy list"; under this rule that list is a Filter-published presentation list (refresh row 20). -> ADR candidate: Filter channel architecture and the "no block" ruling. (orchestrator ruling R1.)

**PF-2. Write set.**
*Rule.* The Filter writes `CanvasItem` members, `Material` and shader parameters (including per-instance parameters), particle members and audio *requests*. It writes **no member of any real light** (LO-25 (ii): "the Filter writes no `Light2D` member"), no `Camera2D`, `Viewport`, `SubViewport` or `SubViewportContainer` property (PIPE C1, C9; the container's `material` slot is assigned and nulled by the pipeline root, PF-25), no simulation state and no block. A grep-able lint enforces every clause (AC-PF-8).
*Why.* The true reveal radius is an anchor (EM-29); a Filter-written light member would be a path to move it. The six upstream clauses that still say "CanvasItem/Material/Light2D" are stale tokens (Cross-References, refresh table), not authority.
*Edge notes.* `Light2D` appears in this GDD only in the negative or inside a quotation of a stale clause. (orchestrator ruling R2.)

**PF-3. Read set and handles.**
*Rule.* The Filter reads: `sanity.perception` (local owner only), the SIM view `sanity_view(player_id) -> {stage, tell_on, harm_eligible}`, Graph `true_value(kind, id)` and Suit `true_value(key, player_id)` (it is the **only non-diagnostic caller** of either, LS-19, LS-22, SE-29), the kernel read views of true positions (`horror.view`, Body and Controller views, for placement only), the tick count, `tick_alpha` and the Pipeline's `present_time`. It **holds no handle on `sanity.state` or `sanity.ledger`** ("no Sanity symbol reads a Filter value, and the Filter holds no handle on `sanity.state` or `sanity.ledger`", SN-23). It imports no simulation module, never registers or moves an Interaction subject (IA-13) and never submits a command.
*Why.* One wrap point, one place the Debug Overlay hooks, and a one-way edge: Sanity writes, the Filter draws, nothing flows back.
*Edge notes.* The HUD never calls Suit's `true_value`; it calls `perceived(SUIT_KEY(k), viewer)`, which wraps it (refresh row 11, X10). The Filter never reads `served_pm`, `lit` or any Attention value to *draw* anything.

**PF-4. Default TRUTH, four grades.**
*Rule.* Every presentation node under the world layer is TRUTH unless it carries a `PerceptionTag` of another grade. Grades: **TRUTH_PX** (bit-identical pixels with the Filter on or off); **TRUTH_ST** (state, shape, position and existence never moved, added or removed; a colour-only edge tint inside ART 4.4 is allowed); **LEAK** (truth by construction: the leak elements themselves, which "cannot be faked or suppressed by any other lie"); **CORRUPTIBLE** (a named module-body surface carrying a `lie_surface_id` present in `lie_targets.json`). An untagged node is TRUTH (counted in `tag_defaults_applied`, never an error).
*Why.* A forgotten node that lies about the consequence channel is the one unrecoverable P1 failure; a forgotten instrument that never lies is a content bug caught by a coverage lint (AC-PF-6). The consequence of default-TRUTH is that the failure mode of omission is "the lie vanishes", never "the truth breaks".
*Edge notes.* Layout's request that walls be truth is granted in full at grade TRUTH_ST (colour-only tint allowed, geometry never touched); Layout's narrower fallback (ordinary interior door leaves at the Filter's discretion) is recorded, not adopted (OQ-PF-14). (orchestrator ruling R11.)

**PF-5. The truth-channel union (table of record)** (registry: `truth_channel_union`).
*Rule.* The **CONSEQUENCE CHANNEL** is the closed list of LS-14: trail VFX (mist, drip, frost, scorch, debris, puddle), the `acoustic` classes at a descriptor's anchor, Suit body cues, lamp brightness from `served_pm`, the `AIRFLOW` draught and the `SEAL_STRAIN` strain; "Perception may neither remove, alter nor add". No lie adds scorch, mist, drip, frost, puddle, sparks or a hiss. The **module-body channel** (a module's sprite, gauge and inspect card, with WORN descriptors from VS-A, LS:864 and LS-14) is the corruptible set *(Amended 2026-10-27 — re-review, delegated ruling G2)*. The union below is assembled once from every neighbour's ask; each row names its grade and the rule that owns the item.

| # | Item | Grade | Owner rule (source) |
|---|---|---|---|
| 1 | Trail VFX (mist, drip, frost, scorch, debris, puddle, hairlines) and the stamps' emissive layers; the mask is per trail element, not per layer (a pipe edge may bleed beside a frost fan; the fan never) | TRUTH_PX | LS-14; FT-2; FT AC-4, AC-5; FT E22 |
| 2 | The `acoustic` classes at descriptor anchors (HISS, DRIP, HUM_DROP, CLANK, WIND, TICK, SIZZLE, CREAK); relay clunk; cavitation TICK | TRUTH (audio) | LS-14; SN-25 |
| 3 | Suit body cues (breath step, visor fog, rime, shiver, reserve valve click, load layer, seam jet, HEAVY back-frame, stagger, walk-only); the 5.3 vital overlays; the warning mark; rail counts (anchors) | TRUTH_PX | SE-25, SE-27, SE-29; SN-19 |
| 4 | Lamp brightness from `served_pm`; the floor light pool; `lux`; fault-glow light; every real light's reach, edge, colour and shadow; the beam, local and remote; the **true reveal radius** | TRUTH_PX | EM-29; LO-25, LO-28; `modifier_stack` |
| 5 | `AIRFLOW` draught; `SEAL_STRAIN` creak and strain | TRUTH | LS-14 |
| 6 | Attention outputs: `web_strand_*`, `vfx_tell_*`, band slide, filaments, tell audio, dust-direction change | TRUTH_PX; exclusive to Attention's presenter; **"its anchor list includes the tell cues"** | AT-17; AT AV-17, AV-18; AC-AT-41, AC-AT-113; AT E-55 |
| 7 | Real horror marks, disturbances, positions, footprints (`vfx_footprint_*`), M17 writes, `apply_suit_damage`; a HIDE removes the **body** only ("hides the body, never the trail") | TRUTH_PX (marks); body omitted or substituted only | HE-32, HE-40; HE E-HE-48; AC-HE-63; SN-22 |
| 8 | Character sprites, hand tools, the true crewmate beam (colour, shape, chest origin), voice, chat, speaker indicator, crew-strip tile, pings | TRUTH_PX; the Filter may substitute the WHOLE body (class c), never edit pixels, never half-transform | ART Corollary, ART 5.0, HR-6; PC-11 and PC row 16; BDY-30 |
| 9 | HUD frame, menu, pause, settings, chart table, text chat, Debug Overlay | TRUTH (separate viewport; the pass sits below every `CanvasLayer`; the frame is fed nothing) | PIPE PR-3, PR-10; HR-6, HR-13; SN-14 |
| 10 | Camera framing: position, zoom, rotation, offset (`Camera2D`, any Viewport property) | TRUTH (zero-write lint) | PIPE C1, C9 and its acceptance lint |
| 11 | Wall faces, door leaves, socket and seam geometry, cap plate, bead, `CUT_BLOCKED`, install, weld and cut prompts (an install prompt is never faked or suppressed), `welds[]` (ordinary doors and hatches alike) | TRUTH_ST (Layout's ask granted in full) | LY-34, LY OQ-13; MSA-35, MSA F1; MSA E-40, E-41 |
| 12 | The seam lane's own PIPE and DUCT valve sprites and CABLE breaker sprite | TRUTH until Graph ASK G8 and F&T ASK FT1 publish a zero-gradient lane witness; **the wrapped-sprite registry must contain none of their ids** | MSA-23 fallback; AC-MSA-126; SN-15 L-C |
| 13 | World block, `web.view`, `pod_at`, the leg exterior, `visible_unknown`, the residual lamp point (exactly one residual lamp point per wreck exterior, drawn lit iff its lamp node is OK or DEGRADED and its persisted battery stock is above 0, otherwise honestly dark; neither fabricated nor erased, never dimmed or flickered), the pod console's refusal reasons (`FUEL_LOW`, `ARRIVAL_BLOCKED`), the dock prop and the empty cradle, the home `FUEL_TANK` "pod reserve" band (no move, no erase, no relocate) | TRUTH | WM-35, WM ASK-F1, WM ASK-AD-8, WM E-55, E-57; DA residual-light ban, AC-DA-38; `visible_unknown` |
| 14 | Interaction prompts, durations, rejection reason codes; save and pause surfaces | TRUTH | IA-10 to IA-13; AC-IA-85; SL H6, SL-24; SN-27 |
| 15 | Authored structure and props (pod props, pedestals, web intrusion) | TRUTH_ST *(provisional — Derelict Authoring re-check when this GDD lands)*; the Filter may add phantom copies, never edit | DA V-11 |
| 16 | Scanner reading (MVP) | TRUTH (anchor); VS-A corruptible at stage 3 only, behind `SCANNER_LIE_STAGE` (default never), tri-modal | SN-19; SNV-12 |
| 17a | Pipe emission, flow chevrons, pipe pattern, pipe diameter (a running pipe is a world-state witness) | TRUTH_PX | SN-15 table; OQ-SN-29; ART Principle 2 (**CONFLICT with LO-14, X5; PF-24**) |
| 17b | The pipe's fitting silhouette **edge** (the colour-blind shape backup keeps its shape; only a colour-only edge tint is allowed, stage 3, cuttable) | TRUTH_ST (the same grade, and for the same reason, as Layout's walls, X9) | SN-15 table; ART 4.4; PF-24 |
| 18 | Gouged WORN footprints of a real horror, while that entity is registered | TRUTH | SN-15 L-C |
| 19 | **The leaks**: gleam, own-cone rim fringe, crewmate-outline fringe, stage-3 hue bleed | LEAK | SN visual table; ART Corollary |
| 20 | **Corruptible set, MVP-live**: gauge needle (dial arc), digital readout, state glyph, inspect card (the same instrument as its wall gauge, from stage 1), lamp-housing glow **sprite** (appearance frame only), whole machine sprite state frames (stage 3; both honest frames required) | CORRUPTIBLE, each with a tri-modal leak | SN-16; SNV-7 to SNV-9; LS:864 |
| 21 | **Corruptible set, VS-A and VS-B** (designed, tested with stubs) *(Amended 2026-10-27 — re-review, delegated ruling G2)*: WORN descriptors (demoted from MVP-live: no `PerceptionRef` exists, PF-9; promoted back only if Sanity answers OQ-PF-38 with a `WORN_DESC` ref before epics), `suit.*` readings behind `READING_LIE_STAGE`, scanner at stage 3, decoy overlays, phantom rigs, HIDE of a real body, lure dressing, the class-(c) horror rig on a crewmate (beam and voice stay TRUTH) | CORRUPTIBLE | SN-19 to SN-22 |

*Why.* P1: "Subtle is allowed; absent is not" applies to the leaks; the closed lists apply to everything else. Rows 17a and 17b follow Sanity's art-director classification over LO-14's "pipe flow" (X5): a running pipe is a world-state witness and its shape is the colour-blind backup. Radiator glow sprite, lamp-housing glow sprite and breaker-panel lamp stay corruptible (LS:213).
*Edge notes.* The lamp-housing lie swaps an **appearance frame** only: the real lamp light, pool and `lux` never change (AC-SN-122). **Must repeat (MSA-35, MSA F1):** Assembly reads no `PERCEPTION_OUT` block and writes no perception state; the Filter never wraps seam geometry, bead, cap plate, socket state or `CUT_BLOCKED`, never fakes or suppresses an install prompt, and, until Graph ASK G8 and F&T ASK FT1 are answered, never wraps the sprites of the seam's own PIPE and DUCT valves and CABLE breaker (MSA-23 fallback). MSA-35's allowance of a phantom seam or a false "welded" state in the Filter's corruptible layer is not used: no seam surface is in `lie_targets.json`, and rows 11 and 12 bar one while F1 stands. (orchestrator ruling R11, R13.)

**PF-6. Tag contract and lints.**
*Rule.* A `PerceptionTag` resource on each presentation scene root carries `{class: TRUTH_PX | TRUTH_ST | LEAK | CORRUPTIBLE, lie_surface_id}`; a CORRUPTIBLE dial or readout surface also carries the calibration record of PF-45 (supplied by the owning scene). The decision logic lives in a headless-testable resolver. Build-failing lints: (i) CORRUPTIBLE requires a `lie_surface_id` present in `lie_targets.json`; (ii) **no TRUTH_PX node has any Filter leak parameter at a non-rest value in any frame**, and on a TRUTH_ST node only `edge_bleed_pm` may be non-rest, and only where the sheet's edge channel is set and its emission channel is clear (PF-24); the lint is at parameter level because the lit leak family lives as a default-off block inside the shared HullSurface and Emissive materials (PF-43), so a node may carry the shared material and still be bit-identical; (iii) the wrapped-sprite registry holds no seam-lane valve or breaker id (row 12); (iv) the Filter source imports no simulation module and writes no block, `Camera2D`, `Viewport`, `SubViewport`, `SubViewportContainer` or real-light member; (v) no Filter code path calls `register_subject`, moves a subject or submits a command (IA-13, AC-IA-85); (vi) coverage: every module kind in `lie_targets.json` has at least one tagged CORRUPTIBLE surface in the MVP content (a VS-A WORN row is not counted and not required, PF-9) *(Amended 2026-10-27 — re-review)*; (vii) no Filter code path calls `set_shader_parameter` on a material referenced by more than one node *(Amended 2026-10-27 — re-review)*.
*Why.* Behaviour plus lint, not a class layout. -> ADR candidate: tag mechanism and default-deny.
*Edge notes.* Lint (ii) is reworded from the rulings' "no Filter material on any TRUTH-tagged node" to parameter level because the stage-3 pipe-edge bleed puts a Filter-written parameter on a shared pipe material whose silhouette edge is graded TRUTH_ST (row 17b) while its emission, chevrons, pattern and diameter stay TRUTH_PX (row 17a); the parameter-level reading keeps the lint testable (CONFLICT C-2 below). (orchestrator ruling R12.)

**PF-7. The query.**
*Rule.* `perceived(ref: PerceptionRef, viewer: PlayerId) -> Perceived` is the only way a corruptible node obtains a displayed value. It is a pull, called from the render callback, deterministic in `(the sanity.perception entry for ref, the true value, the lie's sim-time age, present_time-driven phases, viewer)` modulo the presentation-local state of PF-11. **Cheap honest path:** with no entry for `ref`, `displayed` equals the true value, `leak_amplitude_pm` is 0, `motion_mode` is HONEST, nothing is allocated. Instruments and the HUD never call `true_value`; the Debug Overlay (DIAGNOSTIC) calls both `true_value` and `perceived` for the same ref (PF-41). Results are memoised per `(ref, viewer, frame)`; an honest ref is further memoised per sanity run and invalidated by the notification that dirtied it (so an honest gauge never lags its own settle).
*Why.* One call keeps one wrap point and one debug hook. It mirrors Suit's `true_value(key, player_id)`. The wall gauge, its inspect card and the Debug Overlay's "perceived" column cost one evaluation, and card-gauge parity (HR-14, IA-38) holds by construction.
*Edge notes.* Instruments apply their honest settle and tremor **only when `motion_mode` is HONEST**. (orchestrator ruling R8.)

**PF-8. The `Perceived` record.**

| Field | Type and range | Meaning |
|---|---|---|
| `displayed_pm` | int 0 to 1000 | the value the needle, readout or glyph shows, as per mille of the instrument's range; wander included; equals the true reading on the honest path |
| `displayed_value` | number in the unit of `true_value` for the ref | the same value in the ref's own unit, for numerals and suit keys (derived through the PF-45 calibration); equals `true_value` on the honest path |
| `frame_override` | StringName or NONE | the honest state frame to show (SPRITE_SWAP, glow sprite, glyph); NONE on the honest path |
| `motion_mode` | HONEST, GLIDE, TICK, STALE, BREATH, RETURN | HONEST: Instruments apply settle and tremor. GLIDE: Filter-owned trajectory, no settle. TICK: the readout ticks continuously. STALE: the readout is held. The "readout" is the card numeral (and any digit readout), i.e. the `displayed_value` of the ref's record: TICK and STALE govern that numeral only, and the needle of the same ref draws `displayed_pm`, always with the never-rest wander, in every non-HONEST mode (Instruments C2, OQ-IW-19). BREATH *(Amended 2026-10-27 — design review)*: a static-pair frame (housing, glyph, machine state) is showing its false state and its gleam breathes between `floor_eff` (D.5) and `AMP_MAX_PM` over 4 to 6 s, the hue-independent mode of that surface class (PF-15 table, D.8) *(Amended 2026-10-27 — user rulings)*. RETURN: the lie ended and the follower closes the gap; the mode stays RETURN until CLEAR (PF-12, D.5) |
| `tick_phase_ms` | int | ms since the last digit change (TICK only) |
| `leak_amplitude_pm` | int 0 to 1000 | the gleam amplitude at this instant (PF-15, D.5) |
| `leak_modes` | bitmask {GLEAM = 1, GLIDE = 2, SOUND = 4} | modes in force after the drop rule; always contains GLEAM and the motion mode (PF-15); bit 2 (named GLIDE, Sanity's "motion bit") stands for the surface class's hue-independent motion mode: needle drift and wander, TICK or STALE, BREATH, or rotor drift *(Amended 2026-10-27 — design review)* |
| `tremor_weight_pm` *(Amended 2026-10-27 — design review)* | int 0 to 1000 | the share of an honest tremor already in progress that Instruments may still apply: 1000 on the honest path; on a lie's first ACTIVE frame it falls linearly to 0 over `TREMOR_BLEND_S` (:= `WANDER_ONSET_S`) and stays 0 until CLEAR, so a gauge that was trembling at onset blends out instead of stopping dead (the onset rule of PF-17). It never starts a tremor, and a lie never borrows one |
| `lie_id` *(Amended 2026-10-27 — design review)* | int, 0 = none | the lie handle, for logs and the Debug Overlay: the Filter-derived `lie_key` of D.14 (Sanity's `start_run` masked to 32 bits and forced non-zero), so it is non-zero for exactly as long as a lie is ACTIVE or in RETURN and 0 on the honest path. It is kept (with `lie_kind`) through RETURN and becomes 0 (`lie_kind` NONE) only at CLEAR (S3 `NONE`) (OQ-DO-2 (v)). Sanity's entry carries no `lie_id` (OQ-PF-35), so this key is the interim handle and the Debug Overlay's `lie_id != 0` discriminator (DO-21) keeps working unchanged on it; a real minted id is requested of Sanity (OQ-PF-38) |
| `lie_kind` | FALSE_OK, FALSE_FAIL, GLIDE, SPRITE_SWAP, NONE | echo of Sanity's kind |

*Rule.* The record **never contains the true value** when a lie is active; it is what the seat *perceives*. A remote `viewer` returns the honest record (PF-10). *Why.* A record that carried the truth beside the lie would hand the HUD a way to leak it. The record is a request to Instruments and the HUD, not their design.

**PF-9. `PerceptionRef`.**
*Rule.* The ref is a tagged union: `GRAPH_NODE(kind, id)`, `GRAPH_COMPARTMENT(cid, quantity)` (the `quantity` term, one of the Instruments IW-9 quantities, keeps two compartment instruments in one room distinct in the PF-7 memo and selects the `true_value` kind; K-5, OQ-IW-26), `SUIT_KEY(key)` (the five keys `suit.o2`, `suit.reserve`, `suit.integrity`, `suit.warmth`, `suit.charge`), `SCANNER`, `HOUSING_GLOW(ref)`, `STATE_GLYPH(ref)`, `MACHINE_FRAME(ref)`. The inspect card uses the **same ref** as the wall gauge it mirrors: there is no separate card ref and no separate lie. A ref resolves to Sanity's `subject_ref` through the tag's `lie_surface_id` plus the Graph id *(provisional — the Graph-node-to-subject mapping is the one named assumption of SN-16's lit-entry rule, routed to Interaction and Derelict Authoring)*. A stable integer `ref_key` (kind, id and, for a compartment, `quantity`; never an instance id) feeds the hash (PF-37).
*Why.* One union, one wrap point (orchestrator ruling D11). Suit rail counts are not keys (SE OQ-6) and stay anchors.
**The join: Sanity's `subject_ref` to a `PerceptionRef`** *(Amended 2026-10-27 — design review)* *(provisional until Sanity publishes the `subject_ref` encoding, OQ-PF-38)*. Each row of the Sanity-owned `lie_targets.json` is a pair (module kind, surface kind) and each ledger `subject_ref` is a (module `stable_id`, surface kind, quantity) triple, `quantity` being NONE except for a compartment gauge, so two gauges in one room never collide *(Amended 2026-10-27 — re-review)*; the Filter resolves it with exactly one constructor, so a lie can never attach to two refs and a ref can never receive two lies from one entry (AC-PF-112 tests the bijection on every row).

| Surface kind in `lie_targets.json` | `subject_ref` (module `stable_id`, surface kind, quantity) *(Amended 2026-10-27 — re-review)* | `PerceptionRef` | True source (read only by the Filter) | Note |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| gauge needle, digital readout, inspect card of a Graph node | (node id, GAUGE) | `GRAPH_NODE(kind, id)` | Graph `true_value(kind, id)` | wall gauge and card share this ref (HR-14) |
| gauge of a compartment quantity | (compartment id, GAUGE, quantity) | `GRAPH_COMPARTMENT(cid, quantity)` | Graph `true_value` for the quantity's kind | `quantity` keeps two instruments in one room distinct |
| lamp-housing glow sprite | (lamp node id, HOUSING) | `HOUSING_GLOW(GRAPH_NODE of the lamp)` | the lamp node's state frame (appearance only) | the light and `lux` stay TRUTH |
| state glyph | (node id, GLYPH) | `STATE_GLYPH(GRAPH_NODE)` | the node's state | |
| whole machine sprite | (node id, MACHINE) | `MACHINE_FRAME(GRAPH_NODE)` | the node's state | both honest frames required |
| suit reading (VS-A, honest at MVP) | (PlayerId, SUIT_KEY) | `SUIT_KEY(key)` | Suit `true_value(key, player_id)` | |
| scanner (VS-A, honest at MVP) | none | `SCANNER` | none published (OQ-PF-36) | `UNAVAILABLE` in the Overlay |
| WORN descriptor *(Amended 2026-10-27 — re-review, delegated ruling G2)* | (item id, WORN) | **no ref type at MVP** | n/a | VS-A (PF-5 row 21), demoted from MVP-live and out of the MVP coverage lint (PF-6 vi); it has no `PerceptionRef`; AC-PF-112 fails closed if Sanity lists a WORN surface before a `WORN_DESC` ref exists (OQ-PF-38) |

**PF-10. Per-viewer scope.**
*Rule.* The Filter answers for the **local seat only**. A remote PlayerId passed to `perceived` returns the honest record (counter `remote_queries`); it is never an error. Addressing is by PlayerId, never `peer_id`. `sanity.perception` is `OWNER_PRIVATE`; the replicated `SHARED_SUMMARY` `{stage, tell_on, harm_eligible}` is what lets viewer A draw seat B's tell fringe (VS-B).
*Why.* Answering a remote PlayerId from replicated data would let a client compute another's lies (SN-26). (orchestrator ruling R9.)

#### B. The lie: clock, trajectory, leak, end

**PF-11. Lie clock and purity.**
*Rule.* A lie's trajectory runs on **sim time**, clocked from the lie's **activation run**: `lie_tau = (T_c - SANITY_DRIFT_DIVISOR * act_run + tick_alpha) / TICK_RATE`, with `SANITY_DRIFT_DIVISOR` 12 (registered; equal to the kernel hook divisor by relation, not a knob), `TICK_RATE` 60, `act_run` the run on which the entry became ACTIVE, clamped at 0 (D.1). Pause freezes the tick and therefore the lie. Presentation phases that carry no information (wander phase, breath phase, tick cadence) run on `present_time` (PIPE PR-9). The displayed value is a **pure function of the entry, the live true value, `lie_tau` and per-lie constants, modulo PRESENTATION-local state**: the single slew state of PF-13 and PF-14, which is never persisted and is rebuilt from the formula on `hard_start` (PF-33). While a lie is ACTIVE its displayed value is the formula's value, so a restore reproduces it **up to the slew gap that was still closing at the save** (D.3, PF-33) *(Amended 2026-10-27 — design review)*; the per-lie constants (rate, magnitude, direction, wander frequency and phase, tick mode, sound-breath period) are restore-exact because their hash key is `start_run`, which lives in the saved ledger, and `epoch` is 0 for every per-lie draw (D.14).
*Why.* A trajectory on `present_time` would restart from phase 0 after a restore and show a different displayed value (breaking PF-33); a trajectory on sim time is restore-exact and pause-consistent. A lie is a moving value of the world, so it is interpolated with `tick_alpha` (PIPE PR-8 rule 5), not an animation (rule 6): the world moved, so the lie must have advanced. The clock starts at **activation**, not at the accept: Sanity stamps `start_run` at the accept (SN-16, EC-SN-29) and activates `ONSET_DELAY_RUNS` (10 to 25 runs, 2 to 5 s) later, so a clock from `start_run` would put the first ACTIVE frame 2 to 5 s into the glide, a first-frame jump of up to 10 deg that PF-13 and SNV-13 forbid.
Sanity publishes `active_run` in the perception entry (registry `sanity.perception`); `act_run` is that field.
*Edge notes.* This is the wording the systems-designer proposed for the "pure function" ruling (C-SD2): the lie never snaps (SNV-11), so a step in the true value needs a slew state, and R8's purity holds up to that state. (orchestrator ruling: R8 amended in the live text.)

**PF-12. Lie presentation lifecycle** (Sanity's PENDING, ACTIVE, EXPIRED, DEBUNKED, INVALIDATED mapped to pixels).

| Sanity state | Filter presentation | Notes |
|---|---|---|
| PENDING | nothing drawn; `perceived` honest | invisible by SN-16; no pre-onset hint |
| ACTIVE, onset | ONSET: the displayed value departs the true value on the glide path from displayed-minus-true = 0; the gleam is already at `floor_eff` (D.5) *(Amended 2026-10-27 — user rulings)*; the sound request is already on | no first-frame jump; the leak is born with the lie |
| ACTIVE, arrived | HOLD: the displayed value sits at its false region with wander (PF-17); the leak is steady | never rests |
| EXPIRED, DEBUNKED or INVALIDATED (the entry has left `sanity.perception`) | RETURN: a rate-limited follower closes the gap to the live true value at `GLIDE_RETURN_RATE`; the leak follows the remaining discrepancy through a downward-rate-limited follower (D.5); `lie_id`, `lie_kind` and `leak_modes` stay as they were until CLEAR *(Amended 2026-10-27 — design review)*; the sound level follows the leak, then off at CLEAR | **presentation-identical for all three end types**; no verdict flash, no relief cue |
| **CLEAR** *(Amended 2026-10-27 — design review)* = the *later* of three conditions: displayed-minus-true discrepancy under `LEAK_EPS_DEG` (`[PLACEHOLDER]` 0.25 deg; range 0.1 to 0.5; registered); gleam amplitude at 0 (D.5); wander faded to 0 (`WANDER_BLEND_S` after removal, D.6) | CLEAR: `motion_mode` returns to HONEST without an event, `lie_id` and `lie_kind` become 0 and NONE on that frame, the sound request goes OFF; honest settle is allowed again | the mode returns without a signal; until CLEAR the record is a RETURN record, so a gleam is never reported on an honest record and a wander is never faded under an honest label |

*Rule.* The entry's disappearance from `sanity.perception` is the **only end signal**; the Filter cannot distinguish the three end types and does not try. A stage change never ends a lie (SN-16).
*Edge notes.* The return track is presentation-local: a restore during RETURN shows the true value from the first frame, behind the black hold; no lie is active then, so it is not a clean-frame oracle (E-PF-5). Sanity does not retain ended entries for `LIE_TAIL_S` (OQ-PF-9 answered: ended entries are not retained, loading clears the tail; registry `sanity.perception`), so the tail record and its restore edge are accepted.

**PF-13. Glide laws: GLIDE tracks a discrepancy, FALSE_OK and FALSE_FAIL target a zone.**
*Rule.* Two laws, each a history-free formula of `(lie_tau, live true value, per-lie constants)` (D.3). **GLIDE** (SN-16 kind) is *delta-tracking*: the displayed value is the live true value plus a signed offset that grows along the glide path at the per-lie rate and then holds, so the discrepancy can neither collapse nor grow unseen between Sanity's 5 Hz re-checks. **FALSE_OK and FALSE_FAIL** are *zone-targeting*: the displayed value departs toward an aim point inside the instrument's OK (or alarm) zone interval at the per-lie rate (the nearest zone point to the true value, pushed `ZONE_MARGIN_PM` inside; success is **the displayed interval, the held value plus the wander, intersecting the zone interval** *(Amended 2026-10-27 — design review)*) and then holds the zone, because "OK" is a place on the dial, not an offset; a FALSE_OK that kept tracking a degrading truth would stop hiding it. In both laws the displayed-minus-true discrepancy is 0 at `lie_tau` 0, so there is **no first-frame jump**, and the needle never snaps and never settles. The per-lie **rate** is drawn from the hash (PF-37) inside `[rate_floor, GLIDE_RATE_MAX_DEG]`, where `GLIDE_RATE_MIN_DEG` 0.5 and `GLIDE_RATE_MAX_DEG` 2.0 are names introduced here, values from ART 7.3 (they are not registry constants), and `rate_floor = max(GLIDE_RATE_MIN_DEG, 28.6 / needle_len_px)`, where 28.6 is `0.5 x 180 / pi` (the rate that moves the needle tip 0.5 art px per second; PF-17; the per-lie rate and magnitude draw is D.2); the **magnitude** is drawn from the hash and then reduced so the departure completes within `GLIDE_REACH_FRAC` `[PLACEHOLDER]` (0.6; range 0.4 to 0.8) of the lie's life; it is capped at `D_MAX_FRAC_ARC` `[PLACEHOLDER]` (0.4; range 0.25 to 0.5) of the dial arc; the dial stop is handled live by clamping the target (D.3), never by shrinking the per-lie magnitude. For FALSE_OK and FALSE_FAIL the magnitude is the cap itself (the needle stops at the zone, D.3), so a zone inside the cap is reachable whenever the life allows (D.2) *(Amended 2026-10-27 — design review)*. Sanity picks the *kind*; the side follows from the kind (FALSE_OK and FALSE_FAIL imply it; the direction of a GLIDE is the Filter's, by hash, comfort-biased per `KIND_W`); the Filter picks the rate, magnitude and direction (SN-23: WHAT and WHEN, never HOW). Both laws feed **one slew state per ref**, rate-bounded by `GLIDE_RATE_MAX_DEG`, which keeps the needle from snapping after a step in the true value and closes any residual gap (including the in-flight value of a return, PF-14); wander is added outside the slew (D.3, D.4). *(Amended 2026-10-27 — design review)* The slew is additionally held within `DISC_CAP_PM` of the true value (D.3): a documented, bounded exception to the no-snap rule that stops a truth step or a fast truth ramp (a breach) from leaving the needle a full dial away from the room, which would break the 40 % fairness cap and hide the emergencies `W_MIN_WARNING_S` protects (OQ-PF-40).
*Why.* The absolute-anchor alternative (displayed value independent of truth after an anchor angle) carries a restore conflict, because the anchor is presentation-local and the true value may have moved since; the split above needs no anchor. (orchestrator ruling: the systems-designer's two-law form is adopted; the technical-artist's anchor form is rejected for restore-exactness.)
*Edge notes.* Build assertion *(Amended 2026-10-27 — design review)*: `rm_min * D_DRAW_MIN_DEG >= D_VIS_MIN_DEG` (0.7 x 24 = 16.8 >= 12) guarantees that the smallest drawn departure is visible in time even on the slowest reduced-motion time axis (it is the visible-in-time condition `D_VIS_MIN_DEG / (rm_min * rate_need_min) <= GLIDE_REACH_FRAC * 40 s` with `rate_need_min` 1.0 deg/s, D.2). Reduced motion scales the **time axis only**: `D_eff` is the same at every `rm`, so the setting changes when a lie arrives and never how far it can reach. *(Amended 2026-10-27 — re-review)* The claim is worded as a worst-case aim distance and backed by a second assertion: `GLIDE_REACH_FRAC / rm_min <= RM_ARRIVE_FRAC_MAX` (0.6 / 0.7 = 0.857 <= 0.9), i.e. `t_arrive(rm_min) <= 0.9 x L_s` for every lie (a reach-bound lie arrives at `GLIDE_REACH_FRAC x L_s / rm`; the earlier default `rm` 0.6 put every reach-bound FALSE_* lie at or after its expiry, so the setting acted as an easy mode and PF-13's "must reach its zone" failed), which leaves a lie at least `ZONE_DWELL_MIN_S` (4.0 s at the 40 s life) held in its zone. `RM_GLIDE_SCALE` therefore starts at 0.7. The dial stop is handled live: the target is clamped to the dial range minus the wander peak (D.3), so a lie whose truth sits at a stop shows a smaller displayed-minus-true than usual but is still drawn with its gleam, its sound **and its wander, which is the needle's hue-independent motion mode and is never clamped away** (the needle never pins at a stop; a pinned needle rests); counter `lie_short_reach` (once per lie, when the discrepancy is under `D_VIS_MIN_DEG` after arrival time). Such a lie is weak, not unleaked; Sanity's pre-draw gate should refuse a subject whose truth leaves no room (OQ-PF-38). Sanity supplies `first_lie` as a field of the `sanity.perception` entry (registry `sanity.perception`), not inside `params`; the side is implied by the kind and is the Filter's HOW (`params.side` is not needed). The reach of a 40 s lie to a distant comfort zone is a risk owned by Sanity and the art-director (a nearest-edge target or a longer `LIE_LIFE_S` minimum would fix it); counter `lies_partial` measures it (OQ-PF-10). *(Amended 2026-10-27 — design review)* A FALSE_OK or FALSE_FAIL lie must reach its zone, not merely leave the truth: the Filter's D_eff for these kinds is the cap itself and the aim point sits inside the zone (D.2, D.3); a lie whose displayed interval still misses the zone interval at its arrival time counts `lies_zone_missed`, and the eligibility test belongs in Sanity's pre-draw gate, where the true value and the life `L_s` are known at accept (OQ-PF-38) *(Amended 2026-10-27 — re-review)*: the distance to the zone edge plus `m_z` must fit inside the reach the Filter can deliver, `min(D_MAX_FRAC_ARC x arc_deg, GLIDE_REACH_FRAC x GLIDE_RATE_MAX_DEG x L_s)` (96 deg at 240 deg arc for `L_s` of 80 s or more, 48 deg at 40 s, 200 pm), independent of `rm`, not `D_MAX_FRAC_ARC` alone.

**PF-14. Lie end: the glide-return.**
*Rule.* On entry removal the Filter starts a return track and moves the displayed value toward the **live** true value at `GLIDE_RETURN_RATE` (`[PLACEHOLDER]`, default 3.0 deg/s; range 2.0 to 8.0; registered; D.6), never overshooting, never settling, never snapping, and tracking the true value at the same bounded rate if it moves meanwhile. It is identical for EXPIRED, INVALIDATED and DEBUNKED (SN-16). The leak amplitude follows the remaining displayed-minus-true discrepancy (PF-15), so there is no separate "lie over" event; the record stays a RETURN record, with `lie_id` and `lie_kind` non-zero, until CLEAR (PF-12) *(Amended 2026-10-27 — design review)*. Build assertion: `GLIDE_RETURN_RATE >= GLIDE_RATE_MAX_DEG`.
*Why.* SN-16, SNV-11 and P4 ("no verdict"): the end type must never be an oracle. A return slower than the top glide rate would let a debunked needle outlast a live lie, making DEBUNKED a punishment; a much faster return is a legible "the lie just ended".
*Edge notes.* Under reduced motion the glide is scaled by `rm` and the return by `rm_ret = max(rm, RM_RETURN_FLOOR)` (`RM_RETURN_FLOOR` `[PLACEHOLDER]` 0.83; range 0.8 to 1.0), which caps the reduced-motion return at about 1.2 times the normal return duration (an unbounded slowdown, 1.43 times at `rm` 0.7, was a hidden difficulty cost and a longer tail that marks an ending); the assertion `rm_ret * GLIDE_RETURN_RATE >= rm * GLIDE_RATE_MAX_DEG` holds (2.5 >= 1.4 at defaults) (PF-39) *(Amended 2026-10-27 — design review)*. A new lie on a ref mid-return **starts from its own formula** (discrepancy against the true value is 0 at `lie_tau` 0), never from the return's value, so an ACTIVE lie's displayed value never depends on presentation-local return state. The slew of PF-13 closes the residual gap from the in-flight return value at a rate of at most `GLIDE_RATE_MAX_DEG`: no snap, a history-free law, and after a restore the displayed value equals the formula from the first frame (E-PF-3).

**PF-15. The leak triple, the ACTIVE floor and the drop rule.**
*Rule.* Each lie renders its entry's `leak_modes`: GLEAM (the violet gleam), GLIDE (the motion signature) and SOUND (the sound signature), each sufficient on its own (HR-5). While a lie is ACTIVE its gleam amplitude is never below the stage-indexed floor `floor_eff` (D.5: `AMP_FLOOR_PM` `[PLACEHOLDER]` 400 per mille at stages 0 to 1, falling to `AMP_FLOOR_S3_PM` 250 at stage 3; range 250 to 600) *(Amended 2026-10-27 — user rulings)* even at zero discrepancy, and above that it follows the discrepancy (its rises are instantaneous, its falls are limited to `R_AMP_ACT`, D.5) *(Amended 2026-10-27 — re-review)*; after the entry leaves it follows the *remaining* discrepancy from the amplitude at removal, with no step (D.5). The sound request is emitted on the first ACTIVE frame, not when the needle has visibly moved. **Mandatory modes and the drop rule (SN-15 L-L made computable; P1 sane-palette rule).** Two modes are **mandatory on every lie and never droppable**: **GLEAM**, the *palette mode*, because P1 requires that "every hallucination leaks [flesh and violet] somewhere ... Subtle is allowed; absent is not", and a glide or a sound is not a palette leak (a lie drawn with motion and sound but no violet anywhere would be a palette-silent lie); and the **motion mode** (the one mode that survives both hue removal and mute). A lie whose violet is carried by a draw other than the host gleam (a SPRITE_SWAP frame whose gleam leads the cut, a VS-A lure whose flesh or violet content is its own leak, a class-(c) or phantom rig contour with `leak` above 0, E-PF-37) satisfies the palette mode through that draw, and its entry still carries the GLEAM bit. **Only SOUND may be dropped:** at stage 2 or 3, only when the entry carries all three, only when it is not the first lie, chosen by a PRESENTATION-only hash test against `MODE_DROP_PM` and counted in `leak_modes_dropped`. By construction `leak_modes` therefore always contains GLEAM and the motion mode on every ACTIVE lie (`popcount >= 2`, `leak_modes & GLEAM != 0`), at every stage and every level of detail. This is a lint as well as a runtime rule: `lint_leak_palette.gd` (AC-PF-18) fails the build if any lie-content row, fixture or drop-rule branch can produce an ACTIVE lie with `leak_modes & GLEAM == 0`. `MODE_DROP_PM` is `[PLACEHOLDER]` **0 at MVP** (lint) (shipped value; `first_lie` is now supplied).
**The invariant, restated: GLEAM plus at least one hue-independent motion mode, on every ACTIVE lie, on every fallback path** *(Amended 2026-10-27 — design review)* The motion mode is the one that survives hue removal *and* mute, so SOUND never counts as it. Every corruptible surface class has a named motion mode, in the table below; a lie on a surface for which no motion mode can be drawn, or whose gleam cannot be drawn within the budget (PF-42), is **not drawn at all** (the honest path, counter `lie_unrenderable`). The Filter never draws a lie with only part of the pair, so a fallback can reduce how many lies are drawn and never how a drawn lie leaks. `lint_leak_palette.gd` (AC-PF-18) and the fallback lint (AC-PF-108) fail the build for any branch that can.

| Surface class | Lie kinds | Hue-independent motion mode (`motion_mode`) | Notes |
|---|---|---|---|
| Dial gauge needle (GRAPH_NODE, GRAPH_COMPARTMENT, SUIT_KEY dial) | GLIDE, FALSE_OK, FALSE_FAIL | needle drift plus the never-rest wander (record mode GLIDE, or STALE for FALSE_*); the card numeral adds TICK or STALE | the wander runs in every needle lie, including one whose truth sits at a dial stop (D.3 live clamp, E-PF-10); a needle shorter than `NEEDLE_LEN_MIN_PX` is not lied on unless the ref has a visible digit mechanism (E-PF-11) |
| Linear sight-glass | same | meniscus drift plus the wander (PF-45 linear branch) | below `TRAVEL_MIN_PX` not lied on |
