| Numeral-only readout (digit mechanism, no needle) | GLIDE; FALSE_OK, FALSE_FAIL | TICK for GLIDE; for FALSE_* STALE **plus the gleam breath** (a held digit alone is not motion, so the record carries BREATH's gleam modulation) | D.9 |
| State glyph, lamp-housing glow, machine state (static pair) | SPRITE_SWAP; FALSE_OK, FALSE_FAIL on a housing appearance frame | BREATH: the gleam breathes between `floor_eff` (D.5) and `AMP_MAX_PM` over 4 to 6 s (D.8; HR-7 already treats the breath as a fringe's hue-independent channel) | the false frame itself is static; E-PF-12 |
| Machine sprite, continuous frame (rotor on a stopped machine) | SPRITE_SWAP | the rotor cadence drift (record mode GLIDE, D.17) | |

*Why.* "Subtle is allowed; absent is not" (ART:73). The earlier draft let the hash choose *which* of the three modes to drop, so a stage-2 or stage-3 lie could have been drawn as glide plus sound with no violet anywhere, breaking the pillar's sane-palette clause (creative-director concern, CD-GDD-ALIGN); the palette mode is now mandatory exactly as the motion mode already was. The stage-2 rim fringe and the stage-3 hue bleed are not relied on to cover a gap, because both are conditional and cuttable. A leak that is a pure function of discrepancy is 0 on the first frame, which would break SN-15 L-L ("the first lie carries all three") and SNV-13 ("full leak from frame one"); the floor closes that onset hole. The `AMP_FLOOR_PM` visibility floor is a sibling of `DECOY_LEAK_ALPHA_PM` 300 and `BREATH_FLOOR_PM` 400 at stages 0 to 2 (the gleam floor is 400 and 325); at stage 3 the user-ruled floor of 250 (D74) deliberately sits below the decoy's 300 (a VS-A overlay, absent at MVP), and the alignment is routed to Lighting and the art-director. *(Amended 2026-10-27 — user rulings)*
*Edge notes.* A malformed entry with empty `leak_modes`, or with a non-empty `leak_modes` that lacks the GLEAM bit, is a debug-build assertion; release draws GLEAM and GLIDE for the empty entry, **adds GLEAM** to the other, and counts `leak_repaired`, so a palette-silent lie is never drawn (E-PF-49). If audio is muted or unavailable the lie keeps two modes; the player's mute is not a Filter event.

**PF-16. The gleam.**
*Rule.* Violet `#B07BE6` (never the core `#8A3FD1`), at an **edge only** (glass edge, needle leading edge, housing edge, sprite contour), never a fill or a plane, `GLEAM_EDGE_PX` `[PLACEHOLDER]` (range 1 to 2), set per rung by Instruments' `gleam_edge_px`: 2 art px at k = 1 and 1 art px at k >= 2, so the gleam is at least 2 screen px at every rung (`GLEAM_EDGE_SCREEN_MIN` 2, OQ-IW-4 accepted inside this range). It is drawn on the corruptible element itself, **lit with its host** (it appears only where the instrument is visible; no lit-gate query is needed and none is made, IA-13), and its amplitude is the PF-15 amplitude (breathing on the static-pair classes, D.5). Its transport is the ladder of PF-42 (instance parameters, then a MultiMesh overlay, else the surface is not lied on); a MultiMesh path must keep the gleam lit with its host and sample the host's `_msk` edge slice, which is spike S-1c *(Amended 2026-10-27 — design review)* *(Amended 2026-10-27 — re-review)*. Its peak channel is capped at `LEAK_MAX_CH`, defined **by relation** as `LEAK_MAX_CH := DECOY_MAX_CH` (0.85, itself at most 0.9 x `GLOW_HDR_THRESHOLD`; `DECOY_MAX_CH` is registered and owned by Lighting; `LEAK_MAX_CH` is a name introduced here for the registry stage, not a knob and not a second value), so a leak never blooms (glow is processed before tonemapping since 4.6) and never reads as a light.
*Why.* A gleam on an unlit instrument would be a location oracle and would put a phantom in the dark (SN:1267). The cost of the lit choice is that the on-screen colour shifts under the Tungsten beam (the blue channel is attenuated about a third), which the art-director must accept (OQ-PF-6); the hue-removed test (AC-SN-154) is unaffected.
*Edge notes.* On a HUD-layer reading (VS-A) the HUD draws the gleam on its own glass, in HUD px, from `leak_amplitude_pm` and `leak_modes`; the inspect card's glass is drawn by its owning node the same way (Instruments IW-16, with the HUD's gleam helper). The Filter supplies parameters and owns no HUD node (C-3 and OQ-PF-11 closed from the HUD side, OQ-HS-16). `LEAK_MAX_CH` is a **linear** channel value, compared in the same space as `GLOW_HDR_THRESHOLD` (pre-tonemap), and applies on every surface the Filter parameterises, world layer and HUD-layer glass alike; if HDR output is enabled on the root viewport the cap is re-tested (OQ-HS-13; `[UNV]` until the Lighting spike confirms the colour space of `DECOY_MAX_CH`).

**PF-17. Motion signatures and the never-rest rule.**
*Rule.* Exactly three motion signatures exist and only one belongs to a lie: **settle** (damped spring, ease-out 250 to 400 ms, overshoot at most 5 %, then still), **tremor** (+-1 art px (+-2 art px at k = 1, so it stays at least 2 screen px; Instruments' `tremor_offset`), irregular, 6 to 10 Hz, around a *true* value) and **glide** (the lie). A lie never borrows settle or tremor; an honest gauge never glides; the returned `motion_mode` is the only switch. The leak's own HR-7 breath (D.8) is a modulation of the gleam and not a fourth gait: it is the motion mode of the static-pair classes (PF-15 table) *(Amended 2026-10-27 — design review)*. **Never-rest:** the 0.5 to 2 deg/s band of ART 7.3 bounds the *drift* component only; a separate **wander**, one sinusoid per lie, of `WANDER_AMP_PX` `[PLACEHOLDER]` (2.2 art px at the needle tip, along the needle's tangent; range 2.1 to 2.6 at `WANDER_F_MIN` 0.40, the lower bound being derived in D.4 for the worst needle angle; the art-director's floor of 1 px is met with margin) at a hashed frequency in `WANDER_F_MIN` to `WANDER_F_MAX` `[PLACEHOLDER]` (0.40 to 0.45 Hz; below the 0.5 Hz cap, and disjoint from the 6 to 10 Hz tremor) carries "never rests" for a held lie. (orchestrator ruling: the systems-designer's two-sine sum is not adopted, because its beat envelope falls to 0.2 of the peak and the rounded tip would then rest; D.4.) A held lie's tip changes its quantised pixel position at least every `NEVER_REST_MAX_S`, a **cited ceiling of 1.0 s** (ART 7.3 and SNV-25: "the needle is always a pixel from where it was a second ago"; not tunable above 1.0 s), proven in D.4 **on two axes** *(Amended 2026-10-27 — design review)*: the tip moves along the needle's tangent, so at tangent angle `phi` the rounded tip position is a pair of rounded axis positions, and the worst-case dwell on one pixel is `acos(1 - 1/(A m)) / (pi f)` with `m = max(abs(cos phi), abs(sin phi))`, at least `1/sqrt(2)`: the worst needle angle is 45 degrees, where each axis sees only `A / sqrt(2)`. The single-axis figure of the earlier draft (0.952 s at 2.0 px and 0.35 Hz) hid a **1.16 s rest at 45 degrees**, over the art bible's 1.0 s ceiling, which no amplitude inside the old range could fix; so `WANDER_F_MIN` is **0.40 Hz** (range 0.40 to 0.45) and `WANDER_AMP_PX` 2.2 (worst case 0.96 s at 45 degrees, 0.79 s at 0 and 90 degrees). Lie-capable in-world gauges ship a needle of at least 16 art px (recommended 24); a needle shorter than `NEEDLE_LEN_MIN_PX` (`[PLACEHOLDER]` 16; range 14.3 to 32; eligibility floor 14.3 = 28.6 / `GLIDE_RATE_MAX_DEG`; owner In-World Instruments, provisional) is ineligible for a needle glide **and the surface is not lied on at all** (E-PF-11: a gleam with a sound has no hue-independent motion mode), unless the same ref has a visible digit mechanism whose TICK, or STALE with the gleam breath, is then its motion mode *(Amended 2026-10-27 — re-review)*.
*Why.* At 0.5 to 2 deg/s a 12 to 32 px needle's tip moves 0.1 to 1.1 art px per second, one pixel step every 1 to 10 s, so "always a pixel from where it was a second ago" (ART 7.3, HR-8, SNV-25) is unattainable by rotation alone. The wander also exceeds 2 deg/s instantaneous angular speed on a short needle (14.9 deg/s at 24 px, 2.2 px and 0.45 Hz), which is why the band is read as drift only. (CONFLICT C-1: the art bible's glide band and its own never-rest sentence; the bible refresh is owed by the art-director, Cross-References.)
*Edge notes.* The per-lie drift rate is raised to `rate_floor = max(0.5, 28.6 / needle_len_px)` so tip speed is at least 0.5 art px per second where the band allows (at 24 px, 1.2 deg/s). **HUD dials (VS-A; OQ-HS-12).** The glide-eligibility rule is the same for a HUD-layer dial: `needle_len_px` and `NEEDLE_LEN_MIN_PX` are measured in the pixel grid the dial is drawn in (HUD px), so the rate floor keeps the tip at least 0.5 px of that grid per second; a HUD dial whose needle is shorter is not lied on (E-PF-11), unless it has a visible digit mechanism whose TICK or STALE is its motion mode *(Amended 2026-10-27 — re-review)*. The charge cell's motion mode is not ruled here (OQ-PF-33). Reduced motion does not scale the wander amplitude or frequency (PF-39), so the 1.0 s ceiling is untouched by `RM_GLIDE_SCALE`. The wander is added outside the slew of PF-13, which would otherwise clip its instantaneous speed. **Onset rule and maximum onset rest** *(Amended 2026-10-27 — design review)*: at a lie's first ACTIVE frame the gauge is usually still (a settled honest needle), so the wander fades in *linearly over `WANDER_ONSET_S`* (`[PLACEHOLDER]` 1.0 s; range 0.5 to 3.0; linear, because the smoothstep of the earlier draft left the tip on one pixel for over 2 s) from a zero offset on frame one (no first-frame jump, D.1), and any honest tremor already running is blended out, not stopped, by `tremor_weight_pm` (PF-8) over `TREMOR_BLEND_S` := `WANDER_ONSET_S`. The maximum onset rest is `ONSET_REST_MAX_S` := `WANDER_ONSET_S + NEVER_REST_MAX_S` = 2.0 s (analytic bound: the fade-in, then the held-state ceiling); the worst case measured over needle angle, phase and fractional pixel is 1.15 s at 0.40 Hz (D.4, AC-PF-46). The gleam and the sound are on from frame one, so an onset rest is never a moment without a leak.

**PF-18. Lying readouts: tick, stale-hold and card parity.**
*Rule.* A lying digital readout either **ticks continuously** (kind GLIDE: the digit tracks the glide) or **holds stale** (kinds FALSE_OK and FALSE_FAIL: the digit holds the false target while the ship changes), chosen by hash per lie. The readout is the card numeral (the `displayed_value` of the needle ref's `Perceived`); TICK and STALE govern that numeral only, while the needle draws `displayed_pm` with the never-rest wander in every non-HONEST mode (PF-8; Instruments C2, OQ-IW-19). Honest numerals update on the shared hold grid `HONEST_TICK_MS` (500 ms, HUD-owned; at most one step per grid boundary, then-current value, never queued) and never tween (registry `honest_numeral`; the grid amends art bible 7.3's "one step per real change", ratified 2026-10-27, user ruling X9/D91 *(Amended 2026-10-27 — user rulings)*). The tick interval is `LIE_TICK_MS` `[PLACEHOLDER]` (registered; default 400 ms; range 334 to 1000): the floor is **334 ms**, because a 333 ms interval is 3.003 transitions per second against AC-SN-149 "no more than 3 per second" and the registered intent of `FLASH_MAX_PER_S` 3. Lying readouts that share a region (a ninth of the displayed picture, counting HUD-layer readouts) share one scheduler: `T_tick = max(LIE_TICK_MS, N_r * 334)` with phases staggered, so the sum of transitions in any rolling second stays at most 3 (D.9); a tick is a digit-glyph substitution, never an inversion of a plate. The inspect card calls the same `perceived(ref)` as its wall gauge, so it lies exactly when the gauge lies, from stage 1 (HR-14, IA-38); the card frame is an anchor and violet appears only on the reading's glass (ART 4.6).
**Return, hand-off and the region gap governor** *(Amended 2026-10-27 — design review)*: during RETURN the lying numeral keeps stepping on the same scheduler to `round(slew_value / lsb)` (it follows the returning needle and never jumps to the honest value) and switches to the honest grid only at CLEAR, at the next `HONEST_TICK_MS` boundary. A STALE numeral is derived from the *displayed* needle value: it follows the needle by ticks during ONSET and holds from arrival, never jumping to the zone centre while the needle is still short of it, and every numeral (TICK step included) is clamped to `[value_lo, value_hi]`. A **region-level gap governor** guarantees at least 334 ms between any two Filter-owned lying ticks in a region, across a change of `N_r` when a lie starts or ends: a lying tick due sooner is deferred to the earliest slot 334 ms after the previous one and never queued twice, so the spacing guarantee survives an `N_r` change mid-run (D.9) *(Amended 2026-10-27 — re-review)*. The governor defers **only Filter-owned lying ticks** and never an honest swap: the lying-to-honest switch at CLEAR lands on the **next HUD grid boundary** (the `HONEST_TICK_MS` grid is HUD-owned, tick-anchored and skip-only, so the HUD arbiter admits the switch at that boundary or skips it to the following one, and never delays it; the numeral keeps its last lying value until then). **Honest-swap accounting** (registry `hud_flash_budget`): in WEIGHTED mode honest digit swaps are exempt and take no slot in `N_r`; in STRICT (reduce-flashing on, the first-launch default) they are not exempt, each counts as one transition in the region window, and the HUD arbiter never defers a Filter lying tick, so when `N_r` lying ticks already fill the window (`N_r` 3 is 2.994 per second) the arbiter skips the honest swap to its next boundary.
*Why.* One readout at the 333 ms floor already equals the transition ceiling; `LIES_MAX` 4 would exceed it unscheduled (four readouts at 400 ms are 10 transitions per second). The trade-off at `N_r` 4 is a weaker continuous cadence (about 0.75 Hz): still never stopped, still distinguishable from an honest numeral. (R35 said "at least 333 ms"; the arithmetic puts the first compliant value at 334 and the registry entry is written from this text.)
*Edge notes.* Reduced motion never lowers the interval (PF-39). The area model (a digit substitution changes well under 1 % of a region's luminance) would permit more, and the conservative reading binds for HUD-layer readouts, where HR-13 is region-agnostic (OQ-PF-12).

**PF-19. State glyph, glow sprite and machine sprite.**
*Rule.* Frames come from the module's **own sheet**: a SPRITE_SWAP shows one of the machine's own honest state frames (a rotor spinning on a stopped machine, a housing cracked on a whole one) and is eligible only where both honest frames exist (the content gate is Sanity's SN-16 plus Instruments and Derelict Authoring; a missing frame draws nothing at all, no frame, no gleam and no sound, and counts `lie_unrenderable`, E-PF-12) *(Amended 2026-10-27 — design review)*. "Cracked" is a `mach_*` state frame, never a `trail_*` hairline. The lamp-housing glow sprite lie swaps an unlit appearance frame that cannot read as a lantern pool (ART Principle 3). **Onset** is the mirror of return: the gleam rises first over `SWAP_LEAD_S` `[PLACEHOLDER]` (3 s; range 2 to 6) and the frame cut follows (for the rotor the "cut" is the start of the cadence wind-up over `ROTOR_WINDUP_S`, D.17, never a jump to running cadence on frame one) *(Amended 2026-10-27 — re-review)*; from the cut the gleam breathes (`motion_mode` BREATH, the static-pair hue-independent mode, PF-15 table, D.5) *(Amended 2026-10-27 — design review)*. **Return** (art-director ruling, one rule by lie type): a *continuous-variable* frame (rotor cadence on a stopped machine) winds its cadence toward the honest cadence at a constant rate that includes `rm_ret` and snaps to it at a minimum cadence (`ROTOR_SNAP_FRAC`, D.17), with the gleam outliving the snap, so the return is invisible and a false state is never shown without its leak *(Amended 2026-10-27 — re-review)*; a *static-pair* frame (cracked or whole, lit or unlit) cuts back to the honest frame and the gleam decays on the glide-return schedule after the cut, so the leak outlives the false frame and a false frame is never shown without its leak. Identical for the three end types. The rotor cadence of a SPRITE_SWAP lie drifts and never settles to the honest rate (D.17). **Route** *(Amended 2026-10-27 — re-review)*: a SPRITE_SWAP, static pair and rotor alike, is applied by writing the lying node's own frame or region (one write at the cut and one at the cut-back for a pair, the per-frame cadence for the rotor), which is inside PF-2's write set, is per instance and a hard cut by construction, and so has no dependency on spike S-1 and no place in the PF-43 shared-shader block.
*Why.* HD pixel art has no blended in-between sprite and a half-cracked housing is a machine state that does not exist; a frame cut is the native vocabulary of machine state change, but an *uncaused* cut with no leak is an unleaked lie.
*Edge notes.* The afterglow after a static-pair cut tells the player something changed back, a mild oracle accepted because it is identical for all end types and never says why. Two alternatives were proposed and are carried, not adopted: an ordered-dither dissolve (the shader-specialist: every output texel is exactly one of the two honest frames' texels, so the palette stays exact) and a crossfade (the technical-artist; rejected, it produces off-palette blended pixels). OQ-PF-3 owes the art-director and creative-director confirmation. (orchestrator ruling: art-director's split rule adopted; R16 left the choice open.)

**PF-20. The sound signature and `FILTER_AUDIO_CLASSES`.**
*Rule.* The Filter owns the **id namespace** `FILTER_AUDIO_CLASSES` and the request semantics `(viewer, ref or position, intent in {ON, LEVEL(pm), OFF}, class)`; Adaptive Audio owns assets, mix, bus and ducking, and states whether audio can lie *(Adaptive Audio & Tells, designed, honours this rule, answering IDX:41 with "audio can lie only through the hallucination family the Perception Filter requests for one viewer, every member of which carries an audible leak")*. MVP ids: `FA_LIE_SIG_GAUGE` (needle, readout, card, glyph) and `FA_LIE_SIG_SPRITE` (housing, machine); VS-A: `FA_PHANTOM_MOVE`, `FA_PHANTOM_PRESENCE`. The request goes ON on the first ACTIVE frame (so it is requested no later than the first visible frame, HR-5; D.15) *(Amended 2026-10-27 — re-review)*, LEVEL carries the lie's SOUND amplitude `amp_snd` (the unbreathed leak amplitude, per mille of `AMP_MAX_PM`, 0 to 1000; D.5, D.15), and OFF at CLEAR *(Amended 2026-10-27 — re-review, delegated ruling R8)*. The ids are **lint-disjoint** from the Graph's witness `acoustic` classes, Attention's tell family and Horror's sub-tell. Never altered, faked or added: the human voice and chat, the P1 proximity cue (physically real, played on every client), consequence-channel sounds, tell audio. Silent by requirement: the outline fringe, a stage transition, a recovery, a debunk, an expiry.
**Level contract: a quiet, slow breath, never a constant tone** *(Amended 2026-10-27 — design review)* *(Amended 2026-10-27 — re-review, delegated ruling R8)*. SOUND stays **independent of visibility** (not gated on on-screen or lit): Audio owns distance attenuation, so an off-screen lie attenuates naturally; each leak mode must be sufficient alone (HR-5), which a visibility-gated sound would break for exactly the player who looks away; and the location-oracle risk (a sound that says "something over there lies") is accepted because it points at a surface to doubt, never at the truth, and Audio's attenuation bounds it (the review ruled against gating on on-screen and lit). The request contract *(Amended 2026-10-27 — re-review, delegated ruling R8)* (the Filter is the producer of the request, so it defines the unit and Audio converts, ruling R8; the 2026-10-27 design-review text that scaled LEVEL against a "quietest consequence-channel class" reference Audio does not define is withdrawn): (1) **unit**: `LEVEL(pm)`, and the level an ON carries, is `amp_snd`, an integer per mille of `AMP_MAX_PM` (0 to 1000), the lie's discrepancy envelope of D.5 with the gleam's own breath factor removed; it is the value Adaptive Audio D.15 receives as `leak_amplitude_pm` (0 to 1000) and maps linearly onto `HAL_FLOOR_DB` to `HAL_CEIL_DB`. The Filter states no dB value, no reference class and no cap; (2) **ceiling, floor and combined level are Audio's**: the ceiling (`HAL_CEIL_DB <= WIT_CEIL_DB - HAL_BUS_REL_DB`, so a lie never out-shouts a true witness or masks a P1 consequence sound), the audible floor (`HAL_ACTIVE_MIN_DB`) and any combined cap are applied on Audio's `hal` bus over the voices that survive its `R_hal` cull, which the Filter cannot see (a Filter-side cap would leave a lone audible lie near the floor); the Filter sends the raw per-lie `amp_snd` of every lying instrument and never scales one lie against another; (3) **envelope, one authority**: ON carries `(LEVEL, T_s, SOUND_BREATH_FLOOR_PM)`, with `T_s` drawn once per lie in 4000 to 6000 ms from the hash (tag 11, dead-zoned like the fringe breath, `SOUND_BREATH_FLOOR_PM` `[PLACEHOLDER]` 500) and run locally by Audio, so there is no constant tone and no per-breath request traffic; Audio's `HAL_BREATH_PERIOD_TICKS` and `HAL_BREATH_DEPTH_PM` are fallbacks used only for an ON that carries no envelope (OQ-PF-39), and `LEVEL` never carries `breath01`, so the sound is modulated exactly once; (4) the gleam breath (D.8 cycle draw) and the sound breath (`T_s`) are separate draws and are not claimed to be phase- or period-paired; both are dead-zoned and the sound envelope is covered by the lock test of AC-PF-27. Habituation is a playtest signal (V8, the share of testers who mute the sound within 30 minutes).
*Why.* Only if the Filter names the ids can a lint prove disjointness from the witness classes (LS:976). "Phantom-entity audio uses the Filter's own classes" and "owned and mixed by Adaptive Audio" (SN-25) are reconciled as ids versus content. (orchestrator ruling R5.)
*Edge notes.* The art-director's mood: doubt, not horror; no heartbeat, no tempo change, no sting at a stage boundary or a debunk. No audio-only phantom in the MVP.

#### C. Stage, fringes and the container

**PF-21. Stage presentation: weather.**
*Rule.* The SIM stage steps instantly; the picture does not. Each stage-bound effect has a 0-to-1 **envelope** that slews linearly toward its target (`GAIN_e[stage] / 1000`) so that **every transition takes exactly `STAGE_RAMP_S`** of `present_time`: the rate is latched at each target change as the remaining distance over `STAGE_RAMP_S`, never above `1 / STAGE_RAMP_S` per second (D.7): `STAGE_RAMP_S` `[PLACEHOLDER]` default 8 s, range 6.5 to 20, registered, with the build assertion `STAGE_RAMP_S >= the breath period upper bound (6000 ms, ART 5.5) + a 0.5 s margin` (so 6.5 s; D.7, D.11). Amplitude-monotone, never a pulse, never announced, never an overshoot. A reversal (stage flapping at a threshold, hysteresis release) continues from the current value, re-latches its rate so the return also takes `STAGE_RAMP_S`, and never restarts at 0; a stage drop is silent (no relief cue). Lies are not ramped by stage; they have their own onset and run on to expiry.
*Why.* `STAGE_DWELL_RUNS` 25 (5 s) is shorter than the ramp, so the stage can change while a ramp is mid-flight. The ramp must exceed one breath so the onset cannot be read as the first breath, and must not announce itself "through tempo" (ART 2.9).
*Edge notes.* (orchestrator ruling R16 gave a range of 6 to 20; the floor is raised to 6.5 by the breath rule: the ramp must exceed the 6000 ms upper bound of a breath period by a 0.5 s margin, so an onset cannot be read as a first breath. The flash model (D.11) is slack and does not bind: the conservative patch-level minimum is 3.91 s with the computed violet luminance and the adopted breath depth 0.6. An earlier draft figure of 6.41 s used a breath depth of 0.3 and a placeholder luminance of 0.5 and is superseded.)

**PF-22. The stage effect matrix.** (Stage count confirmed 0 to 3; "stage-1/2/3 drift" is honoured as per-stage *reach and leak*, delivered per element; a deliberate narrowing of the index's "screen-wide effects", X8.)

| Element | 0 | 1 | 2 | 3 |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| Screen-level effect of any kind | none | none (SNV-1) | none | none |
| Lies drawn (`LIES_MAX`, cited, Sanity's) | 0 | at most 1 | at most 2 | at most 4 |
| Reach: needle, readout, inspect card | n/a | yes | yes | yes |
| Reach: WORN descriptors (VS-A; no MVP ref, PF-9) *(Amended 2026-10-27 — re-review, delegated ruling G2)* | n/a | no | no | no |
| Reach: state glyph, lamp-housing glow sprite | n/a | no | yes | yes |
| Reach: whole machine sprite (SPRITE_SWAP) | n/a | no | glow sprite only | yes |
| Gleam on lying surfaces *(Amended 2026-10-27 — user rulings)* | n/a | per lie, floor `floor_eff` 400 | per lie, floor 325 | per lie, floor 250 (D.5) |
| Own-cone rim fringe (MVP-conditional, `RIM_MODE` LIE_BOUND by default, OQ-SN-21 answered) *(Amended 2026-10-27 — user rulings)* | n/a | no | envelope target `RIM_STAGE_GAIN_PM[2]` `[PLACEHOLDER]` 600 while a reach lie is ACTIVE for the viewer, else 0 | `RIM_STAGE_GAIN_PM[3]` 1000 while a reach lie is ACTIVE, else 0 |
| Edge hue bleed on pipe silhouettes (cuttable, default-off: `AMBIENT_FRINGES_ENABLED` false, OQ-SN-22 answered) *(Amended 2026-10-27 — user rulings)* | n/a | no | no | `BLEED_STAGE_GAIN_PM[3]` 1000 only when the flag is on; 0 at the shipped default |
| Whole-image warm drift (container pass, VS-A, spike-gated) | n/a | no | non-anchor content only | same |
| Flesh surface tint (grade ST, VS-A; none at MVP) | n/a | no | no | VS-A |
| Phantoms, HIDE (VS-A) | n/a | no | no | stage 3 only |
| Reach is Sanity's eligibility, not a Filter gate: the Filter draws every entry it is handed (E-PF-18) and never decides whether a surface may lie; the Reach rows document what Sanity will hand over at each stage *(confirmed 2026-10-26, QA review C-4)* | | | | |
| Forbidden at every stage | tint, vignette, tunnel, shake, blur, chromatic aberration, scan-line, glitch, desaturation, grade, heartbeat pulse, sanity meter, number, icon or colour, verdict banner, relief cue, Alarm Red or a "safe" colour in a lie | | | |

**PF-23. The own-cone rim fringe (MVP-conditional).**
*Rule.* Shown to the lying seat only, on its own beam. One continuous band **tangent to the true cone rim and outside it**, `RIM_FRINGE_PX` `[PLACEHOLDER]` art px wide (default 2; range 1 to 3; registered), violet `#B07BE6`, edge-only, drawn on the unlit overlay mask bit 2, casting no light, absent from `emitter.state`, no Attention input. It never extends the true reach, never overlaps a beam pixel (`#FFE2A8` pixels stay bit-identical), never roots at a point and never locks to `tell_beat`. It is drawn only along rim segments in lit, unoccluded space: where an occluder cuts the beam the band stops at the cut and never follows the shadow edge, so no violet appears in the dark beyond a wall (that would be a free wall-hack). Its pose is sourced from Lighting's published beam pose, not recomputed, to +-0 art px *(provisional — Lighting ask)*. It breathes (PF-30, D.8). It is anchored to the per-facing chest point and the Filter never moves the chest point.
**Mode** *(Amended 2026-10-27 — user rulings)*: `RIM_MODE` is {LIE_BOUND, AMBIENT}; **LIE_BOUND is the shipped default (user ruling X11, D65/D75)** and AMBIENT, the stage-bound band described above, is a playtest-gated opt-in. LIE_BOUND shows the band only while a *reach lie* exists for the viewer (an ACTIVE lie on a state glyph, lamp-housing glow sprite or machine frame): the envelope target is `RIM_STAGE_GAIN_PM[stage] / 1000` while one exists and 0 otherwise, on the same `STAGE_RAMP_S` slew (D.7), so it never pulses and it appears only as a leak of a real lie. The design review (game-designer) found that AMBIENT contradicts PF-G5 ("the only violet is a gleam on a lying reading"), dilutes "violet marks the liar" and acts as a stage meter; the user accepted its recommendation on 2026-10-27 (OQ-PF-1, answering OQ-SN-21), so LIE_BOUND ships. AMBIENT may be enabled only after a playtest shows that the standing band is not read as a stage meter; both modes stay specified, so the change is one value.
*Why.* The art bible's 4.4 puts a cone-rim fringe in the stage-2 row as a standing leak; it is the diegetic self-sign and the "look at edges" lesson. In AMBIENT mode its cost is that it behaves as a de facto stage indicator (tension with IA-39 "no sanity meter or number exists", OQ-SN-21). Ruled 2026-10-27 (OQ-PF-1): kept, shipped lie-bound (shown only while a reach lie is ACTIVE, so the standing-stage-indicator cost is removed and IA-39 needs no tolerance for it); second in the cut order (R30, R31). *(Amended 2026-10-27 — user rulings)*
*Edge notes.* How the occlusion clip is realised is a technical-artist and lighting-spike item `[UNV]`. -> ADR candidate: rim-band occlusion clip realisation.

**PF-24. Stage-3 hue bleed (MVP-conditional, cut first).**
*Rule.* An edge-only violet mix on pipe silhouette sheets, controlled by one shared per-sheet parameter `edge_bleed_pm` (`EDGE_BLEED_MAX_PM` `[PLACEHOLDER]` 500; range 200 to 800) scaled by the stage-3 envelope. It mixes toward violet only where the sheet's silhouette-edge channel is set **and** its emission channel is clear: it never replaces the loop hue, never alters chevrons, pattern, speed, direction, diameter or fitting silhouette (TRUTH_PX, row 17a), and never paints a trail or consequence VFX. It requires the `pipe_shared_msk` edge channel; if that channel is not authored, this rule is cut and stage 3 still leaks through the gleam and the whole-sprite lies. No flesh tint at MVP (PF-22; ART 4.4 reserves flesh for stage 3 and for surfaces, graded TRUTH_ST, VS-A). *(Amended 2026-10-27 — design review)* The bleed is ambient by nature (it is bound to no lie), so it ships **default-off** (`AMBIENT_FRINGES_ENABLED` false) as a playtest-gated opt-in (user ruling X11, D76/OQ-PF-2), because ambient violet on pipes contradicts PF-G5 and works as a stage meter. With the flag off its envelope target is 0 at every stage, no non-rest `edge_bleed_pm` is written, and the edge channel is built only if `pipe_shared_msk` is authored anyway (cut first). *(Amended 2026-10-27 — user rulings)*
*Why.* Stage 3 is reachable in the MVP only by sustained BREACH or horror contact (SN-29), so it is a late payoff that must not delay the MVP; one shared per-sheet parameter makes the render cost zero extra draws at any zoom, so the real cost is the art channel (OQ-SN-22, user-carried; R29).
*Edge notes.* Flagged: this puts a Filter-written parameter on a shared TRUTH-graded pipe material; lint PF-6 (ii) is worded to allow exactly the edge-tint variant.

**PF-25. Mask route and the container slot.**
*Rule.* **Route (b) at MVP:** every effect is a per-element material inside the world layer; a TRUTH node never receives one, so truth is exact *by construction*. The container `SubViewportContainer.material` slot is **null** and is assigned and nulled only by the pipeline root; the Filter hands over a configured material when one exists and writes only its parameters. The Filter publishes `filter_route = PER_ELEMENT` and container state `NOT_USED`, which is distinct from `NO_MASK` and is not red in the Debug Overlay. Route (a) (a second viewport of truth silhouettes multiplied into a container pass) is deferred to the Vertical Slice lighting spike for warm drift; if it ships, `D_FT_MAX` goes 38 -> 60 and fallback C (no pass) applies whenever the mask cannot be exact. Any container pass writes colour only, holds hue at or above 36 degrees on non-anchor content, and never touches beam pixels `#FFE2A8` (LO-25 CD-2, AC-LO-36). There is no `Compositor`/`CompositorEffect` here: that is the 3D post-process path and does not apply to the 2D container slot.
*Why.* SNV-29 requires the fringes "be drawn inside the world render at world resolution" (a post-process on the upscaled container would soften the band and break the HD-pixel-art rule); FT OQ 22 requires a per-trail-element mask; the MVP set (gleam, glide, tick, housing, glyph, machine frames, rim, bleed) needs no whole-image pass. PIPE:735 reads a null container as `diag.filter_pass_disabled = NO_MASK`, shown red; under route (b) that would read as a permanent failure (X13, refresh row 19).
*Edge notes.* -> ADR candidate: truth-mask route. A VS-A route (d), a `warm_pm` parameter on the shared hull material instances, is truth-by-construction (an anchor never uses the hull material) but its beam-pixel definition must be settled first (OQ-PF-7). (orchestrator ruling R6, R7.)

**PF-26. The decoy list (VS-A; MVP ships the contract and an empty-list stub).**
*Rule.* The Filter publishes, per viewer, entries `{decoy_id, kind in {GLOW_EXTENSION, PHANTOM_SHADOW}, anchor, shape, tint_leak, start, end}`. Lighting instantiates them on mask bit 3, enforces `decoy_budget` (`DECOY_OVERLAYS_MAX` 4; each entry needs `peak_ch <= DECOY_MAX_CH` 0.85 and a violet rim of at least `DECOY_LEAK_PX` 2 at alpha at least `DECOY_LEAK_ALPHA_PM` 300) and governs decoy alpha ramps under LO-22. Sanity owns which decoy and when, and the invariant `PHANTOMS_MAX` 2 + active decoy lies <= `DECOY_OVERLAYS_MAX`. **Must repeat (verbatim, LO row):** the Filter "may add decoy visuals but never changes the real reveal radius"; a decoy is "a Lighting visual: not an emitter, absent from `emitter.state`, exposes nothing" (EM-29); a decoy never lights geometry; it must deliver "warm drift on non-anchor content only". **Must repeat (Lighting, "What each dependent must repeat"):** "submission is published state Lighting reads and never a call; no submitter touches a beam, the beacon, a fault glow or a glowstick; no submitter changes a real light's radius, position or colour". The Filter writes only `CanvasItem` and `Material` members, never a `Light2D` member (PF-2, LO-25); decoys are overlays on mask bit 3 (LO-21, LO-24); warm temperature drift (ART 2.9, VS-A) never recolours a beam pixel (`#FFE2A8`): it is delivered on non-anchor content only or through an exact truth mask (CD-2, LO-25, PF-25).
*Why.* Content versus drawing is split so the true reveal radius cannot be moved by a Filter write. (orchestrator ruling R4.)

#### D. Class (a), (c), (d): designed, stubbed in the MVP

**PF-27. Phantoms, HIDE and lures (VS-A, stubs).**
*Rule.* A phantom is a rig that exists only in the viewer's perception, inside a decoy overlay slot, in the lit region only, on walkable tiles only; it has no collision, no light, no Attention input, no footprint and no SIM subject, and never uses `web_strand_*` or `vfx_tell_*`. A HIDE omits a real horror's **body only** (footprints and marks stay). A lure dresses a real junk def with a violet edge and a flesh tint, its edge leak breathing at the same period. The Filter animates; Sanity names intent. A phantom never harms, collides, casts light or raises Attention (PF-G1). Exercised with stubs at MVP. Per HE-40 "the Filter (Vertical Slice) may feed phantom or crewmate-driven poses": the Filter feeds the rig presenter pose streams only for phantoms (VS-A) and for class-(c) substitutions driven by the crewmate's true velocity (PF-29); it never poses a real horror, which Horror poses with `leak` 0; Horror owns the rig asset. Every phantom and every class-(c) render has `leak` above 0 (HE-40, HE:1189; a lint fails any rig instance with no Horror ref and `leak` 0). **Must repeat (Horror's Perception Filter row):** the Filter substitutes or suppresses the rig body only, through the rig presenter `(pose_stream, type_id, leak)`, and never edits marks, L3 state, footprints or audio; marks, disturbances and real positions are TRUTH-tagged and immune; "the hallucination hides the body, never the trail" (HE-32). The decoy criterion AC-LO-32 is discharged here by AC-PF-36.
*Why.* SN-22; HE-40. "A shadow with no occluder" remains an unambiguous stage-2 tell because a real horror contributes no occluder (HE-2).

**PF-28. Class (c) victim tell (VS-B live; MVP stub).**
*Rule.* For every other seat B with `tell_on` (the `SHARED_SUMMARY` flag; `SAN_TELL_ON` 30000 mSU, `SAN_TELL_OFF` 35000, no dwell), every viewer A draws: violet `#B07BE6`, **2 art px outside the Void Steel contour** (fixed by ART 5.5; not a knob), on the full outer silhouette and both edges of the beam cone, on the unlit pass (mask bit 2) so it shows when the subject stands in darkness, casting no light, raising no Attention, silent, breathing 4 to 6 s (PF-30), never flickering, never on one's own body. Set and clear are smoothed by a ramp of `TELL_RAMP_S` (`[PLACEHOLDER]` 8 s; range 6.5 to 15; the floor is the same breath rule as `STAGE_RAMP_S`, D.7; a registry candidate beside `STAGE_RAMP_S`) (the flag has no dwell; the picture does). It is rendered for **every** viewer **including a viewer who is themself `tell_on`**: absence would be a self-sign. The P1 proximity cue is physically real and played by Audio on every client; the Filter supplies only the trigger fact (`tell_on` rising). Web attribution lives in Sanity's `incidents` ring and Scars: **the Filter adds none.** Sprite pixels, beam colour and beam shape are untouched.
*Why.* ART 5.5; SN-20; HR-7 (the fringe's breathing motion is the hue-independent channel). (orchestrator ruling R18, R21.)
*Edge notes.* Whether the outline is drawn when its subject is unlit is OQ-SH-4 (the shader-specialist recommends lit-only, the art bible says unlit; this GDD follows the art bible and the ruling, and carries the question, OQ-PF-8). **Creative-director recommendation for the OQ-PF-8 resolution: draw it unlit.** Under P4 class (c) "the victim sees a tell": a lit-only outline would let darkness erase the victim's only visual tell, weakening the guard rail exactly where friendly harm is most likely (a monsterised crewmate in an unpowered corridor). The shader-specialist's lit-only suggestion must not win by default.

**PF-29. Class (c) monsterised view (VS-B live; MVP stub).**
*Rule.* While the **viewer's own** SIM bit `harm_eligible` is set (`SAN_HARM_ENTER` 15000 with `SAN_HARM_DWELL_RUNS` 150; `SAN_HARM_EXIT` 20000; read from SIM, **no PERCEPTION_OUT copy exists**), every other bound seat's body is substituted by a horror rig for that viewer only: a **hard cut within one rendered frame** of the bit setting or clearing (no morph, no half-transformed frame, no pre-eligibility glimpse), the rig at the crewmate's **true position** and driven by the **true velocity**, the **true beam** anchored to the **true chest point** (never re-anchored to the rig), the **true voice**. The substituted rig **always renders with `leak` above 0** (the violet contour, ART 5.5; HE-40 and HE:1189: "any phantom or class (c) render must have `leak` above 0", enforced by a lint that fails any rig instance with no Horror ref and `leak` 0). When `tell_on(B)` also holds for the same crewmate, that contour is the single fringe (SNV-17), never a second one. The Filter sets only Horror's `leak` input and feeds the rig the crewmate's true velocity as its pose stream (HE-40) and the substitution or omission; it never edits pixels, positions, prompts or durations; the prompt key is identical whether the target renders as crewmate or horror (AC-IA-54). Under a body substitution the lightless hand point is free to move for tool placement (PC-11); the chest point never moves (PC row 16).
*Why.* Anti-pillar "no intentional PvP": a render substitution gated by a SIM bit, never an attacker option (PF-G2). A flickering pre-eligibility glimpse would reproduce IA-31's leak. (orchestrator ruling R21.)
*Edge notes.* The monsterised rig does not draw the held tool's body, while the tool's true emission VFX remain (a free truth channel); this is the recommendation for OQ-SN-26, carried to Horror and the Filter in Open Questions. The prompt a viewer sees on a monsterised crewmate is Interaction's and is identical to the crewmate's (AC-IA-54); the Filter never changes a prompt, a duration or a reach. **Must repeat (Ship Body BDY-30):** the class-(c) rig is drawn at the true position (PC:216); Body has no hallucination field and no per-player variant; the Filter reads Body's SIM read view only and never writes `body.state`.

**PF-30. Fringe firewalls and breathing.**
*Rule.* The rim and outline fringes are unlit overlays: absent from `emitter.state` (EM-29 lint: no field named `phantom`, `perceived`, `decoy`, `hallucinated`), no Attention input, never reusing `web_strand_*` or `vfx_tell_*` assets or geometry (tangential, continuous, unrooted, per-viewer, breathing versus radial, discrete, rooted, global, beat-locked: AC-SN-154). The Filter never adds, removes, delays or alters a tell cue; **"its anchor list includes the tell cues"** (AT:378 must-repeat). A fringe **breathes**: a raised-cosine amplitude between `BREATH_FLOOR_PM` `[PLACEHOLDER]` 400 (range 250 to 700; it never reaches absent, else the fringe would blink out and be unleaked) and 1000, with a period drawn **per cycle** from the hash inside 4000 to 6000 ms (ART 5.5, HR-7), cycle boundaries at the cosine minimum so a period change never kinks, never locked to `tell_beat`, the alarm sweep (2 to 3 s) or the voice-activity cue; two simultaneous fringes use different hashed periods (D.8). A dead zone of `BREATH_DEADZONE_FRAC` `[PLACEHOLDER]` 0.06 around `2S` and `3S` of every registered periodic source is excluded from the draw; the alarm sweep is registered as `BEACON_SWEEP_TICKS` (Emitter State; 128 ticks = 2.13 s, range 120 to 180), the single alarm rhythm, so the dead zone has a live input.
*Why.* Every legal breath period equals `2S` for some sweep period `S` in 2 to 3 s, so a fixed period locks 2:1 against a fixed sweep (the harmonic finding); per-cycle jitter makes any accidental lock last about one cycle, and the dead zone closes the rest. The sweep is `BEACON_SWEEP_TICKS`; Audio's duty is `ALARM_DUTY_MAX_PM` (250) and the pulse leading edge is the contract (Audio CX-A20, F6), so no new constant is needed (OQ-PF-13 closed). Audio D.15 and AC-AA-108 cite `BREATH_LOCK_CEIL` (AC-PF-27) for the `hal` breathing against `tell_beat` and the alarm sweep.
*Edge notes.* Breath phase is presentation-local and may differ after restore (SNV-13).

#### E. Geometry, safety, ordering and budget

**PF-31. Leak geometry at every ladder step and both passes.**
*Rule.* All fringes, gleams and bands are defined in **art px** and drawn inside the world render at world resolution, never as a post-process on the upscaled container: `n` art px is `n * k` screen px at ladder step `k` (D.10), and the width is always an exact integer multiple of `k` (no sub-art-pixel softness) at **every** `k in [1, k_auto + 2]` (1 to 5 at 1080p, 1 to 8 at 2160p), under render pass A and pass B (pass B takes `k` as a uniform, PIPE:182). Geometry is sampled in texels of the sprite's own texture where possible, so it is pass-agnostic for free; the rim band is sourced from Lighting's four beam-edge mask sprites. A zoom change is a cut (PIPE C6): leak widths are re-derived on the first frame of the new rung and never interpolated across `k`. The drawn marks are sprites or quads, never `draw_line` (4.7 lines lose their antialias feather); no mark is narrower than 1 art px; nearest sampling only; luminance at or below 0.9 x `GLOW_HDR_THRESHOLD`.
*Why.* SNV-29; reflexion: an invariant written against "the default" zoom must be re-checked at every reachable rung. At `k` 1 on 2160p a 2 px band is 2 screen px on a 3840x2160 field: legibility at the Deck is an Open Question (PIPE OQ 4, OQ-PF-4).
*Edge notes.* Character and horror cells keep at least 2 transparent px outside the contour for the outline (target 4 px; the 2-versus-4 reconciliation is the art-director's, 8.2). An outline never covers a sprite pixel (`show_behind_parent`-class).

**PF-32. Flash and photosensitivity: the bound pair.**
*Rule.* The Filter's luminance ramps (gleam, fringe breath, hue bleed, stage envelopes) are not Lighting-originated and do not enter LO-22's governor; the Filter **self-governs by construction**. The binding bound is always the **pair**: PIPE PR-13 (at most `FLASH_MAX_PER_S` 3 flashes per rolling 1 s, per light, per region of **a ninth of the displayed picture at the current field**, and on the frame mean; a flash is a pair of opposing luminance changes of at least 10 %, red included) **and** HR-13 (no HUD element changes luminance faster than 3 Hz). No full-frame flash on any layer. Every Filter source is amplitude-continuous, **except the rise-only onset step** (0 to `floor_eff` on the first ACTIVE frame, SNV-13: a single-direction change on one gleam element, followed by no fall for at least half a breath period because a lie's gleam breath starts at its trough, D.8) *(Amended 2026-10-27 — re-review)*, and its modelled opposing-pair magnitude inside any rolling second stays under 10 % at the patch level and far under it at the region level (D.11; the ramps are monotone, the breath is a raised cosine whose turning points are at least 2 s apart), and the verification is a 60 s flash scan at stage 3 at the **largest field** (zoom 1x on 2160p, 36 times the 640x360 area).
*Why.* "Region = a ninth of the displayed picture at the current field" (LO-22) supersedes PIPE D.15's "213x120 fixed" (refresh row 10, X11).
*Edge notes.* Reduce-flashing is a separate toggle from reduced motion; Filter ramps are already flash-free, so it changes nothing here (PF-39).

**PF-33. No clean frame.**
*Rule.* The first RUNNING frame after LOADING, and the first frame after any `force_stage` or `force_level`, is presented **already filtered**: envelopes are set to their targets (`hard_start`), lies are drawn at the formula's value for the restored `lie_tau` with full leak from frame one, and `perceived` is answerable before the first RUNNING frame (Sanity rebuilds `sanity.perception` in `post_restore`, SN-23). No fade-in from clean. Glide and breath *phase* and the slew state may differ after restore; **while a lie is ACTIVE** its presence, kind, leak and displayed value (by PF-11) do not: the first frame shows the formula's value, which equals the pre-save value except for any slew gap that was still closing at the save (D.3), and a return in progress at the save is not restored (the entry had already left `sanity.perception`; E-PF-5). Counter `first_frame_filtered`.
*Why.* "One unfiltered frame would be a free oracle" (SN-23); AC-SL-124: "the first `RUNNING` frame after `LOADING` must already be presented through the Perception Filter for the restored sanity level - the black hold covers any rebuild". This answers SL OQ-11 as co-owner with Sanity and the Pipeline. (orchestrator ruling R20.)

**PF-34. Unseen lies and no leak culling.**
*Rule.* A listed lie is drawn whenever its surface is on screen and lit; there is no "seat away" special case; its trajectory is a function of sim time and reappears at the correct point. At far zoom (the large field) level-of-detail may reduce amplitude *detail* (breath sampling rate only; the wander amplitude and frequency are never reduced, D.4), never remove a leak mode. Counts are bounded by `LIES_MAX`, not by the field, so zooming out multiplies nothing.
*Why.* "A culled leak is an unleaked lie" (OQ-SN-17 answered; SN-15 L-L). (orchestrator ruling R17.)

**PF-35. Frame order and latency.**
*Rule.* In the render callback the Filter runs **after** the Avatar Presenter and **before** Lighting reads the decoy list in the same frame. Dirty-mark in the notification, draw in the render callback (PIPE PR-8). A change in SIM reaches pixels after up to one sanity run (`SANITY_DRIFT_DIVISOR` 12 ticks = 0.2 s) plus one frame (D.16): stage steps and entry appearance and removal; lies and ramps then evolve on their own clocks.
*Why.* Accepted and stated in Game Feel. (orchestrator ruling R26.)

**PF-36. Anchors that never shift.**
*Rule.* The chest point, beams, character sprites, hand tools, the HUD frame, the true reveal radius and **camera framing (position, zoom, rotation, offset)** are never written. No lie produces a jolt, zoom or shake (a camera jolt from Horror is Horror's and the Pipeline's, not the Filter's). The camera framing joins the never-shift anchor list (art-bible Corollary refresh, row 13).
*Why.* PIPE C1 and C9; PC row 16 ("may never move the chest point (the beam origin under a body substitution)"). (orchestrator ruling R33.)

**PF-37. Presentation randomness.**
*Rule.* Every draw the Filter needs (glide rate and magnitude, wander frequencies and phases, breath cycle periods, tick jitter and mode, mode-drop choice) is a **pure integer hash** of `(viewer, ref_key, lie_key, epoch)` plus a per-use tag (D.14), with explicit 32-bit masking after every multiply and operand bounds that avoid dependence on GDScript integer overflow `[UNV]`. Never `rng_derive` (sim-only, KERN D.10, H9), never the global RNG, never `RandomNumberGenerator`. *(Amended 2026-10-27 — design review)* `lie_key` is Sanity's `start_run` for the lie, masked to 32 bits and forced non-zero: Sanity's entry carries no `lie_id` (OQ-PF-35), and `start_run` is in the saved ledger, so every per-lie draw is restore-exact. **`epoch` is 0 for every per-lie draw**: the epoch is presentation-local and not saved (AC-PF-70), so a per-lie draw that depended on it would change on a restore. `epoch` is used only by the rim, bleed and outline breath envelopes, where it increments only while that effect's amplitude is 0, so a re-draw is invisible (D.14 table). Statistical quality (avalanche, modulo bias) is a test, not an assumption.
*Why.* Restore-exactness and reproducibility across platforms; precedent LO-19 (the pure integer hash for gutter timing). -> ADR candidate: a PRESENTATION-only stream. (orchestrator ruling R19.)

**PF-38. The Never list.**
*Rule.* *(Amended 2026-10-27 — re-review)* The Filter never draws (numbered for AC-PF-57b): (1) a sanity meter, bar, ring, number, icon or colour; (2) a vignette or tunnel; (3) blur, chromatic aberration, shake, a scan-line or a glitch; (4) global desaturation or a colour grade; (5) a verdict icon, banner or "you were hallucinating" line; (6) Alarm Red or a "safe" colour in a lie or a debunk; (7) a relief cue on recovery; (8) a self-tell on the player's body; (9) a lie that adds scorch, mist, drip, frost, puddle or sparks; (10) any Attention visual; (11) a phantom in the dark; (12) a phantom that harms, collides, casts light or raises Attention. There is no mastery surface for seam-spotting: no counter of lies spotted, no achievement, no "perception" skill.
*Why.* Guard rails PF-G1 to PF-G5, PF-G9. SL H6: save and pause surfaces are never a hallucination class.

**PF-39. Accessibility semantics per effect.**
*Rule.* *(Amended 2026-10-27 — re-review)* **Reduced motion:** the glide slows (the **time axis only** is scaled by `RM_GLIDE_SCALE` `[PLACEHOLDER]` 0.7, range 0.7 to 1.0 (raised from 0.6 and 0.5 to 1.0 by the re-review, so that the lie still arrives with at least 10 % of its life to run: `GLIDE_REACH_FRAC / rm_min <= RM_ARRIVE_FRAC_MAX`, D.2), with the rate floor never below 0.3 deg/s, and `D_eff` is unchanged so no lie gets smaller (the worst-case aim distance is the same at every `rm`); the return is scaled by `rm_ret = max(rm, RM_RETURN_FLOOR)` so it takes at most about 1.2 times the normal one) but never rests *(Amended 2026-10-27 — design review)*; the wander amplitude is never scaled; the lying digit tick survives and is never lowered below `LIE_TICK_MS` (floor 334 ms; it may be raised to 600); the breathing fringe **stays** (it is HR-7's motion channel); honest settle becomes a step and honest tremor may use a held stress mark (Instruments' mechanism). **Reduce flashing:** a separate toggle; Filter ramps are already flash-free. **Colour vision:** the tri-modal redundancy carries it; no global hue remap or daltonization (HR-10). **No sanity-specific setting may remove a leak.** Accessibility Settings exposes only the Filter's published scalars (registry: `filter_accessibility_scalars`: `reduced_motion_glide_pm`, `breath_depth_pm`, `lie_tick_min_ms`) *(provisional — Accessibility Settings, Alpha, undesigned)*; the bounded low-vision hull raise (HR-15) is Lighting's.
*Why.* ART HR-8, HR-10, HR-7; SN-15 L-L. (orchestrator ruling R23.)

**PF-40. Suit readings and the scanner: designed, honest at MVP.**
*Rule.* The HUD asks `perceived(SUIT_KEY(k), viewer)` for the five keys; the Filter returns a wrapped value only when `READING_LIE_STAGE[k]` is at or below the viewer's stage (all "never" in the MVP; designed order `suit.warmth`, then `suit.o2` and `suit.charge`, then `suit.integrity` and `suit.reserve`, SN-19). **Must repeat (verbatim, SE row):** "`true_value` is the only wrap point" on Suit data, and the Filter "may neither remove nor add a body cue, move the chest point, or edit overlay visibility". The scanner is an anchor until `SCANNER_LIE_STAGE` (default never). A lying reading carries the full tri-modal and no suit body cue changes (AC-SE-30).
*Why.* SE-29: "the MVP's single class (b) hallucination lives on ship instruments"; Pillar 2 protects a first-time player's only death clock until the cue ladder is playtested. The flip from honest to wrapped is the Filter's, on Sanity's switch (refresh row 16).

**PF-41. Diagnostics and the Debug Overlay contract.**
*Rule.* The Debug Overlay (DIAGNOSTIC; absent from release builds; never an accessibility feature) calls `true_value` and `perceived` for the **same ref** and shows both with `lie_id`, `lie_kind`, `leak_modes`, `start_run`, `end_run` and `motion_mode`. The Filter publishes counters (registry: `filter_counters`): `lies_drawn`, `leak_modes_dropped`, `leak_repaired`, `lies_partial`, `lie_short_reach`, `lie_unrenderable`, `lie_collisions`, `remote_queries`, `filter_route`, `filter_pass_disabled` (the container state `NOT_USED` is not red), `first_frame_filtered`, `tag_defaults_applied`, `filter_draws_dropped`, `lies_zone_missed`, `render_cpu_us`. `decoy_overflow` is Lighting's. `force_stage` and `force_level` are Sanity's flags (SN-28); the Filter's obligation is to render them with no ramp (PF-33). **Counter severities (confirmed for the Debug Overlay, OQ-DO-2 (iv)):** `lies_partial`, `lie_short_reach`, `lie_unrenderable`, `lies_zone_missed` and `filter_draws_dropped` above 0 are WARN *(Amended 2026-10-27 — design review)*; `leak_repaired` above 0 is DEFECT (a malformed entry, already a debug assertion, E-PF-49); `filter_pass_disabled` = `NO_MASK` is DEFECT (`NOT_USED` is not red); `render_cpu_us` is WARN when its median exceeds `CPU_BUDGET_MS`; every other counter (`lies_drawn`, `leak_modes_dropped`, `lie_collisions`, `remote_queries`, `first_frame_filtered`, `tag_defaults_applied`, `filter_route`) is NOTE. **Dev-channel publication (OQ-DO-2 (i), (ii); dev builds only, never read by a release surface):** per lie, `t_return_max`, now the **time to CLEAR** `= max(WANDER_BLEND_S, amp_end / R_AMP, D_MAX_FRAC_ARC x arc_deg / (rm_ret x GLIDE_RETURN_RATE))` with `rm` latched per lie (E-PF-43): 32 s at `rm` 1.0 and 38.4 s at `rm` 0.7 (`rm_ret` 0.83) for the 96-deg cap, against 53.3 s before the reduced-motion return was capped *(Amended 2026-10-27 — design review)*; per ref, the PF-45 calibration `(value_lo, value_hi, arc_deg)` (Instruments IW-9 supplies `value_lo` / `value_hi` per quantity). `lie_id` (the Filter-derived `lie_key`) is kept through RETURN and is 0 once CLEAR is reached (PF-8, OQ-DO-2 (v)) *(Amended 2026-10-27 — design review)*.
*Why.* The index's high risk row ("Per-client divergence is indistinguishable from desync without tooling") is mitigated by contrasting deliberate corruption with real desync; `state_hash` excludes the perceived channel (KERN D.12). (orchestrator ruling R27.)

**PF-42. Budget.**
*Rule.* MVP: **0 container draws** (`D_filter` 0; 1 only if route (a) ships). Leak overlays are counted inside the effects allowance `D_fx` (fringe + hallucination: at most `D_FX_ADDED_MAX` 10 added draws; cited, art bible 8.6, not a placeholder). **The gleam transport is a ladder, tried in this order** *(Amended 2026-10-27 — design review)* *(Amended 2026-10-27 — re-review)*. **Planning baseline: no transport is proven** (the engine reference carries nothing on per-instance `CanvasItem` shader parameters, and the MultiMesh path has its own limits: one y-sort position, one texture, one material, one light mask and four custom-data floats per `MultiMeshInstance2D`); spike S-1 therefore runs early as a gating spike, on a backend matrix (D3D12, Vulkan and Compatibility, the renderers the Pipeline supports), before any gleam story is written, and the contingency below is recorded: (1) **instance parameters** on the lying instrument's own material (adds no draw), if spike S-1a (API exists) and S-1b (batching within +1 batch of the baseline) pass; (2) a **`MultiMeshInstance2D` gleam overlay**, one instance per (y-sort depth band, atlas page, light mask) group of lying hosts, so 1 to 6 draws and not "1 to 2 whatever the lie count" (D.12 `G_MM`), if spike S-1c confirms that it batches, orders correctly against occluding sprites at several depths, keeps the gleam lit with its host (mixed light masks) and samples the host's `_msk` edge slice; the instance transform is the host's and the four custom-data floats are packed `r` = `amp` / 1000 (gleam alpha), `g` = the resolved gleam edge in art px / 4, `b` = the index of the host's `_msk` edge slice in a per-atlas slice table / 255 (an instance has one texture), `a` = the surface-kind flags (needle edge, glass, sprite contour) / 255 `[UNV]`; (3) **only if neither fits the budget, that surface is not lied on**: the Filter draws the honest path and counts `lie_unrenderable`, or the host is excluded from `lie_targets.json` at stage 2 (a content rule; the exclusion list is a build lint). **Recorded contingency (reduced lie set, not adopted)** *(Amended 2026-10-27 — re-review)*: if neither (1) nor (2) passes on a backend, that backend ships a reduced lie set: only surfaces whose gleam is drawn by their owning node on its own glass (the inspect card, HUD-layer readouts) are lied on, and every world-surface class takes the honest path (`lie_unrenderable`); the fantasy then rests on the card and readout modes, and adopting the reduced set is a decision for the creative-director and the user at the time of the spike result (OQ-PF-43). **A lie is never drawn without its GLEAM** (PF-15). The earlier cut order (drop the housing and glyph gleams, keep the needle's) left palette-silent lies and could not even reach the budget (26 draws less 8 is 18 against 10), and `self_modulate` cannot draw an edge-only gleam, so it is removed from the ladder. The rim fringe adds 1 draw at stage 2 or 3 when it is drawn; the hue bleed is one shared per-sheet parameter (0 draws). The surviving draws per stage on each path are tabulated in D.12. Presentation CPU: at most `CPU_BUDGET_MS` `[PLACEHOLDER]` 0.5 ms median per frame, `CPU_P99_MS` `[PLACEHOLDER]` 1.0 ms at the 99th percentile and `CPU_MAX_MS` `[PLACEHOLDER]` 2.0 ms at the worst frame (D.13), reported by the Debug Overlay *(Amended 2026-10-27 — design review)*. **MVP GPU row** *(Amended 2026-10-27 — design review)*: the leak block in the shared HullSurface and Emissive ubershader adds at most `GPU_LEAK_REST_MS` `[PLACEHOLDER]` 0.05 ms at 1080p and 0.15 ms at 2160p with every leak parameter at rest, and at most `GPU_LEAK_S3_MS` `[PLACEHOLDER]` 0.15 ms and 0.40 ms at stage 3 with four lies (`[UNV]`; measured on the two reference machines of OQ-PF-25). Container GPU cost (VS-A) is the Filter's budget, reported separately: target at most 0.4 ms at 1080p and 1.2 ms at 2160p `[PLACEHOLDER]`, measured at the largest field. `STEP_BUDGET_MS` does not apply (no hook). `D_FT_MAX` stays 38 unless route (a) ships (60). The VS-B class-(c) outlines (outline + two beam edges per tell subject) do not fit the 10-draw allowance at three tell subjects and must be re-budgeted (OQ-PF-15).
*Why.* PIPE D.16; ART 8.6. Instance-parameter batching and caps are `[UNV]` (spike S-1, split in three, the scheme of AC-PF-76 *(Amended 2026-10-27 — re-review)*: S-1a, a yes/no editor check that the per-instance parameter API exists on `CanvasItem` in 4.7.2, which needs no reference hardware and is done before the gleam stories; S-1b, instance-parameter batching; S-1c, the MultiMesh overlay, ordering and light-mask test); the budget is stated conditionally rather than as a given. (orchestrator ruling R25, with the draw arithmetic of the systems-designer adopted over the technical-artist's two-overlays-per-lie count.)

**PF-43. Material accounting: no sixth shader source.**
*Rule.* The Filter introduces **no sixth `ShaderMaterial` source** (LO-13 counts five: HullSurface, Emissive, the fringe, per-segment iridescence and the Filter's container slot). The **unlit** family (outline, rim band, decoy rim, lure edge; selected by a uniform integer, not by a render mode) is the existing **fringe** source; the **lit** family (gleam, hue bleed; a dither variant only if OQ-PF-3 adopts it; SPRITE_SWAP frame addressing is not in the block, it is a node frame or region write, PF-19) *(Amended 2026-10-27 — re-review)* is a default-off include block inside HullSurface and Emissive, driven by a default-zero parameter. A material whose leak parameters are all at their rest values produces output bit-identical to the unmodified material (pass-through exactness). **Never write a material referenced by more than one node** *(Amended 2026-10-27 — re-review)*: a `set_shader_parameter` on the shared HullSurface or Emissive material would put a lie on every instrument that shares it, so the Filter writes only per-instance parameters, the overlay's own material, or a material it created for exactly one node; a unique material per lying host is not a transport (each breaks its batch run, D.12 `B_host`). Build lint PF-6 (vii). Every variant is instantiated once off-screen during LOADING under the black hold, so no first-use compile hitch announces a stage.
*Why.* Whether a shader is lit or unlit is a compile-time property of its source `[UNV]`, so the rulings' "one parametrised leak shader" is read as the fringe source plus an included block (R24 permits "variants of the one parametrised leak shader (or of HullSurface/Emissive)"). This asks HullSurface's owner (Lighting, art) to accept an extra default-off block *(provisional)*; if refused the Filter needs a sixth source and LO-13 reopens (OQ-PF-5).
*Edge notes.* -> ADR candidate: shader-source accounting; material-budget ratification (LO:1344 item 12). Filter shaders are code shaders only (no Visual Shader on a Filter node), read no `TIME` (all phases come from `present_time`), and inherit the sprite's nearest filter.

**PF-44. Fail open to truth.**
*Rule.* If any Filter datum is missing, malformed, unresolvable or throws (a ref with no tag, a calibration record absent, a lie entry for a destroyed subject, an unavailable `true_value`), the affected surface falls back to the **honest path** (displayed equals true, no leak drawn for a lie that cannot be rendered) and the matching counter increments; a Filter fault never propagates to the simulation or to a TRUTH node.
*Why.* The one unrecoverable failure is a lie where the truth belongs; a missing lie is a content bug, a missing truth is a P1 breach. Combined with PF-4 the Filter is fail-safe in both directions of omission.

**PF-45. Dial calibration contract *(designed; PF-45 answered by IW-8, OQ-PF-29)*.**
*Rule.* To draw a lie the Filter needs, per corruptible dial or readout surface, a calibration record supplied by the owning scene through the tag: `arc_deg` (dial sweep), `needle_len_px`, the zone bands as intervals `[z_lo, z_hi]` of the per-mille reading (one for the OK zone, one for the alarm zone) *(Amended 2026-10-27 — design review)*, and `lsb` (the readout's least-significant unit). Without it the surface is honest (PF-44, `lie_unrenderable`), and the coverage lint (PF-6 vi) makes that a build failure for any kind listed in `lie_targets.json`. Instruments also owe: a pivoted needle as a **separate** sprite or slice, a `_msk` slice naming the glass and needle-edge regions the gleam uses, both honest state frames on one sheet page, and the honest settle applied only when `motion_mode` is HONEST. **Linear branch (K-5, OQ-IW-26).** A LINEAR surface (a tank sight-glass meniscus; Instruments IW-8 `gauge_kind` and D.11 `linear_position`) supplies `travel_px` in place of `arc_deg` and `needle_len_px`. The Filter runs D.2 to D.6 in per-mille space with the angular quantities replaced by Instruments' pixel band (cited, never re-valued here): rate floor `r_floor_pm_s = 1000 x LINEAR_GLIDE_RATE_MIN_PXS / travel_px`; rate ceiling `r_floor_pm_s x GLIDE_RATE_MAX_DEG / GLIDE_RATE_MIN_DEG` (the dial band's ratio, 4); return rate `r_floor_pm_s x GLIDE_RETURN_RATE / GLIDE_RATE_MIN_DEG`, so D.6's assertion holds; smallest visible departure `D_vis_pm = 1000 x D_VIS_MIN_PX / travel_px`; magnitude cap `D_MAX_FRAC_ARC` of the travel; eligibility `travel_px >= TRAVEL_MIN_PX`. The wander (D.4) moves the meniscus row by `WANDER_AMP_PX`. An ineligible LINEAR surface is not lied on (the honest path, `lie_unrenderable`) unless the ref also has a visible digit mechanism, whose TICK or STALE is then its motion mode (AC-PF-111) *(Amended 2026-10-27 — design review)*.
**Linear column of D.2 and D.3** *(Amended 2026-10-27 — re-review)*: the four In-World Instruments constants the linear branch uses are `LINEAR_TRAVEL_PX` 50 (one size at MVP), `D_VIS_MIN_PX` 4, `LINEAR_GLIDE_RATE_MIN_PXS` 0.5 and `TRAVEL_MIN_PX` 40 (all Instruments-owned `[PLACEHOLDER]`, cited in the Cited table, never re-valued here). Every dial quantity that is not already a fraction maps through `arc_deg_ref` 240 (the dial reference of Instruments D.11): `x_pm = 1000 x x_deg / arc_deg_ref`.

| Quantity | Dial (240 deg, 24 px needle) | Linear, `travel_px` 40 | Linear, `travel_px` 50 | Linear form |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| rate floor | 1.19 deg/s = 4.96 pm/s | 12.5 pm/s (0.5 px/s) | 10 pm/s | `r_floor_pm_s = 1000 x LINEAR_GLIDE_RATE_MIN_PXS / travel_px` |
| rate ceiling (`R_act` at `rm` 1.0) | 2.0 deg/s = 8.33 pm/s | 50 pm/s | 40 pm/s | `4 x r_floor_pm_s` (the dial band's ratio) |
| return rate | 3.0 deg/s = 12.5 pm/s | 75 pm/s | 60 pm/s | `r_floor_pm_s x GLIDE_RETURN_RATE / GLIDE_RATE_MIN_DEG` (6 x) |
| `D_DRAW_MIN` | 24 deg = 100 pm | 100 pm (4.0 px) | 100 pm (5.0 px) | `1000 x D_DRAW_MIN_DEG / arc_deg_ref` (0.10 of the travel) |
| `D_MAX_FRAC_ARC` cap and `DISC_CAP_PM` | 400 pm | 400 pm (16 px) | 400 pm (20 px) | a fraction, unchanged |
| `D_VIS` | 12 deg = 50 pm | 100 pm (4 px) | 80 pm (4 px) | `1000 x D_VIS_MIN_PX / travel_px` |
| `W_M` (wander peak) | 21.9 pm | 55.0 pm | 44.0 pm | `1000 x WANDER_AMP_PX / travel_px` (single axis; dwell 0.79 s at 0.40 Hz) |
| `LEAK_EPS` | 0.25 deg = 1.04 pm | 1.04 pm (0.04 px) | 1.04 pm (0.05 px) | `1000 x LEAK_EPS_DEG / arc_deg_ref` |
| `LEAK_D_FULL` | 20 deg = 83.3 pm | 83.3 pm (3.3 px) | 83.3 pm (4.2 px) | `1000 x LEAK_D_FULL_DEG / arc_deg_ref` |
| eligibility | `needle_len_px` >= `NEEDLE_LEN_MIN_PX` 16 | `travel_px` >= `TRAVEL_MIN_PX` 40 | same | PF-17, E-PF-11 |

The dial's assertion `rm_min x D_DRAW_MIN >= D_VIS` has the linear form of the time condition it stands for, `D_vis_pm / (rm_min x max(r_floor_pm_s, D_DRAW_MIN_pm / (GLIDE_REACH_FRAC x 40 s))) <= GLIDE_REACH_FRAC x 40 s`: at `travel_px` 40, 100 / (0.7 x 12.5) = 11.4 s <= 24 s (the product form would fail, 0.7 x 100 = 70 < 100, because the rate floor, not the draw, sets the slowest rate); the arrival bound `GLIDE_REACH_FRAC / rm_min <= RM_ARRIVE_FRAC_MAX` is unit-free and unchanged. **Worked vector** (`travel_px` 40, FALSE_OK, `L_s` 40 s, hash draw 61): `rate_draw` = 12.5 + 37.5 x 0.061 = 14.79 pm/s, `rate_need` = 400 / (0.6 x 40) = 16.67, `rate_nom` = clamp(16.67, 12.5, 50) = 16.67 pm/s, `D_eff` = min(400, 0.6 x 16.67 x 40 = 400) = 400 pm (16 px), `t_arrive` 24 s at `rm` 1.0 and 34.3 s at `rm` 0.7; the target clamp is [55, 945] pm.
*Why.* The Filter works in per-mille dial space and cannot invent geometry; Sanity's `lie_targets.json` names module surfaces, not dials. Instruments (designed) answers it with IW-8 (`gauge_kind`, the calibration record) and IW-9 (`value_lo` / `value_hi` per quantity).

**PF-46. One live lie per surface.**
*Rule.* If `sanity.perception` holds two entries with the same `subject_ref` for the viewer, the Filter draws the one with the lowest `start_run` only, ties broken by the lowest position in the entry order of `sanity.perception` (Sanity rebuilds the lies list in ascending ledger `slot` order, requested in OQ-PF-38) *(Amended 2026-10-27 — design review)*; the other contributes no pixels and no sound; counter `lie_collisions`. No leak is lost because the winner carries its own.
*Why.* Sanity's pre-draw gates check immunity, the cap and the gap, not "already lying on this subject". Sanity enforces the invariant "at most one live (PENDING or ACTIVE) lie per (seat, subject_ref)" at its pre-draw gate (SN-16; registry `sanity.ledger`); this tie-break remains as a defensive fallback.

### States and Transitions

The Filter has no simulation state machine. These are *presentation-local* machines, none persisted, none in `state_hash`; each is rebuilt from the formula on `hard_start` (PF-33). "Who may cause" names the only actors that can move a machine; the Filter itself never causes a SIM transition.

#### S1. Filter service (per client)

| State | Entry condition | Exit condition | Behaviour | Who may cause the transition |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| `COLD` | boot | LOADING begins | no effects; every `perceived` call returns the honest record; the container slot is null | process start |
| `HARD_START` | the first RUNNING frame after LOADING; any `force_stage` or `force_level` | one rendered frame later | envelopes set to their targets; every ACTIVE lie drawn at the formula's value for the restored `lie_tau` with full leak; anchors and phases initialised; counter `first_frame_filtered` | Save/Load (LOADING to RUNNING, kernel state); Sanity's QA flags (`force_stage`, `force_level`, SN-28) |
| `LIVE` | after `HARD_START` | PAUSED or LOADING | ramps, trajectories, breaths and counters run on their clocks | automatic |
| `PAUSED` | kernel pause | resume, or LOADING | `present_time` and the tick are frozen, so every lie clock, ramp, breath, wander and tick freezes; the paused frame still contains every active leak (hue and edge presence do not depend on motion) | the player (pause), the kernel control plane |
| `CLEARED` | LOADING, session teardown | `COLD`, then `HARD_START` | presentation-local state (return tracks, slew limiters, envelopes) dropped, never saved | Save/Load, session teardown |

#### S2. Stage-bound effect envelope (per viewer, per effect `e` in {rim, bleed, floor}; the class-(c) outline uses `TELL_RAMP_S` in the same form)

| State | Condition | Transition | Who may cause |
|---|---|---|---|
| `OFF` | target 0 and envelope 0 | target > 0 -> `RAMP_UP` | `sanity_view.stage` crossing `STAGE_ENTER` (SIM, instant); for the rim in LIE_BOUND mode (the default) the first reach lie going ACTIVE at stage 2 or higher; the bleed never leaves `OFF` while `AMBIENT_FRINGES_ENABLED` is false *(Amended 2026-10-27 — user rulings)* |
| `RAMP_UP` | envelope below target | slews at the rate latched at the target change (`rate_s`, D.7; at most `1/STAGE_RAMP_S`), so every transition lasts `STAGE_RAMP_S`; reaching target -> `STEADY`; target drops -> `RAMP_DOWN` | the stage, then `present_time` |
| `STEADY` | envelope equals target | target changes -> `RAMP_UP` or `RAMP_DOWN` | the stage |
| `RAMP_DOWN` | envelope above target | slews at a rate re-latched at the target change (D.7), so it too lasts `STAGE_RAMP_S`; reaching 0 -> `OFF` | the stage (hysteresis exit above `STAGE_ENTER` + `STAGE_HYST` 2000 mSU, after `STAGE_DWELL_RUNS` 25) |

The SIM stage steps instantly; the picture arrives like weather. A reversal mid-ramp continues from the current value with its rate re-latched, so the return takes `STAGE_RAMP_S` from any value (D.7). Entering `HARD_START` sets the envelope to its target. The hash `epoch` of the effect increments only in `OFF`.

#### S3. Lie presentation (per viewer, per `lie_id`)

| State | Condition | Transition | Who may cause |
|---|---|---|---|
| `NONE` | no entry for the ref and no RETURN in progress, or CLEAR reached after a return (PF-12) *(Amended 2026-10-27 — design review)* | entry appears ACTIVE -> `ONSET` | Sanity (entry appears in `sanity.perception`, SN-16) |
| `ONSET` | entry ACTIVE and the departure not complete (`lie_tau` below arrival time) | departure complete -> `HOLD`; entry removed early -> `RETURN` (the follower starts from the in-flight discrepancy) | Sanity (removal: expiry, a falsification, an invalidating re-check, a class disable); sim time (arrival) |
| `HOLD` | entry ACTIVE and arrived | entry removed -> `RETURN` | Sanity |
| `RETURN` | entry absent and CLEAR not yet reached (CLEAR is the later of discrepancy under `LEAK_EPS_DEG`, gleam amplitude 0 and wander faded to 0; `lie_id` and `lie_kind` stay non-zero throughout; the return is the slew state of PF-13 with the live true value as its target, rate `GLIDE_RETURN_RATE`) *(Amended 2026-10-27 — design review)* | CLEAR -> `NONE`; a new entry on the same ref -> `ONSET` **from its own formula** (discrepancy 0 at `lie_tau` 0); the slew closes the gap from the in-flight value at most `GLIDE_RATE_MAX_DEG`, no snap (E-PF-3) | the follower at `GLIDE_RETURN_RATE` (presentation); Sanity (a new entry) |

`ONSET` and `HOLD` are both `motion_mode` GLIDE (or TICK or STALE for a readout, or BREATH for a static-pair frame *(Amended 2026-10-27 — design review)*); `RETURN` is `motion_mode` RETURN until CLEAR. SPRITE_SWAP uses the same four states with the gleam-leads-cut onset and the type-split return of PF-19. The leak amplitude follows PF-15 in every state; a stage change never moves the machine.

#### S4. Class (c) per subject seat B as seen by viewer A (VS-B; MVP stub)

| State | Condition | Draw | Who may cause |
|---|---|---|---|
| `HUMAN` | `harm_eligible(A)` clear and `tell_on(B)` clear | untouched | SIM (Sanity's flags) |
| `TELL` | `tell_on(B)` set (shown to A whatever A's own `tell_on`) | outline fringe ramped in by `TELL_RAMP_S`, breathing | Sanity (`tell_on` of B, `SAN_TELL_ON`/`SAN_TELL_OFF` hysteresis), replicated as `SHARED_SUMMARY` |
| `RIG` | `harm_eligible(A)` set | horror rig substituted, hard cut, true position, velocity, beam and voice; the fringe, if `tell_on(B)`, is the single leak fringe | Sanity (`harm_eligible` of A: the actor's own SIM bit, `SAN_HARM_DWELL_RUNS` 150 to set, immediate clear) |
| `TELL_AND_RIG` | both | one fringe (SNV-17), never double | both of the above |

A is never shown a fringe on A's own body in any state (ART 5.5 read as "never on one's own body"; the own-cone rim fringe sits on the beam rim).

#### S5. Mask route

| State | Meaning | Transition | Who may cause |
|---|---|---|---|
| `PER_ELEMENT` (container `NOT_USED`) | MVP: every effect is a per-element material; the container slot is null; not a fault | `CONTAINER_MASKED` after the Vertical Slice spike and its ADR | a project decision (ADR), the pipeline root (slot assignment) |
| `CONTAINER_MASKED` | VS-A: a whole-image pass for warm drift on non-anchor content, truth-masked | the mask cannot be exact or allocation fails -> `NONE_C` | the pipeline root; mask availability |
| `NONE_C` | fallback: no whole-image pass; per-element only | n/a | mask failure |

A null container never reads `NO_MASK` while `filter_route` is `PER_ELEMENT` (PF-25).

### Interactions with Other Systems

**Reading the tables.** "Names fixed elsewhere" lists the exact hook, mutator, read view, dirty key or block name the counterpart already fixed; the Filter registers **no hook, no mutator and no block** (PF-1), so the hook column is "none" in every row. Each row quotes the counterpart's own Interactions row so bidirectional consistency can be checked; where the counterpart still carries a stale token the row says so and the correction is a *refresh owed* in Cross-References, never an edit made here. "(provisional)" marks every neighbour with no GDD on disk; no GDD file existed when this GDD was written, so everything stated about them is a request this GDD places, to be re-checked when they land.

#### I-A. Upstream systems (the Filter depends on them)

| System (status) | Data in (to the Filter) | Data out (from the Filter) | Interface owner | Names fixed elsewhere | Counterpart's own row (quoted) and consistency |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| **Command & Simulation Layer** (designed) | `read_view` of `SIM` and `PERCEPTION_OUT` blocks; the tick count, `tick_alpha`, kernel state (RUNNING, PAUSED, LOADING); subscriber class `PRESENTATION` | nothing | Kernel: views and diagnostics surface. Filter: drawing | CR-3 (no hook), CR-8 (no block), CR-9 (no mutator), CR-10 (two consumer channels), CR-12 (`PERCEPTION_OUT` isolation), H4, H5; D.12 `state_hash` excludes `PERCEPTION_OUT`; the sim read of a `PERCEPTION_OUT` block asserts in debug and returns an empty view in release (`PERCEPTION_READ_BLOCKED`) | Row 11: "Subscriptions only: Filter as `PRESENTATION`, Overlay as `DIAGNOSTIC` ... Read views (`SIM` + `PERCEPTION_OUT`) ... Neither gets submit or mutators. Overlay absent from release builds (never an accessibility feature). Filter writes only CanvasItem/Material/Light2D". **Consistent** except the last clause: it is a **stale token** (the Filter writes no real-light member, LO-25 (ii)); refresh row 1. Row 10 (Sanity) says "perception output (`PERCEPTION_OUT`, not persisted)" and KERN:741 maps the tag contract onto the two consumer channels: consistent with PF-1 |
| **Pixel-Perfect Render Pipeline & Camera** (designed) | the world layer (layer 0) and its stack position; `k`, `k_auto` and the field size; the pass A/B flag; `present_time`; the assignment of the container slot; the flash invariants (PR-13) | configured leak `ShaderMaterial` variants and their parameters; `filter_route`; the container material when one exists (handed to the root, which assigns it) | Pipeline: viewports, slot, camera, flash invariants. *The counterpart's own row (Adaptive Audio & Tells, Interactions, quoted).* "The audio request `(viewer, ref or position, intent in {ON, LEVEL(pm), OFF}, class)` (PF-20) ... the request goes ON on the first ACTIVE frame and OFF at CLEAR (PF-12) ... **Filter owns the id namespace `FILTER_AUDIO_CLASSES`** (MVP `FA_LIE_SIG_GAUGE`, `FA_LIE_SIG_SPRITE`; VS-A `FA_PHANTOM_MOVE`, `FA_PHANTOM_PRESENCE`) and the request semantics; **Audio owns assets, mix, bus, ducking, the master identity table and the lint**". Its Overview answers IDX:41: "Audio can lie only through the hallucination family the Perception Filter requests for one viewer, every member of which carries an audible leak". **Consistent**; IDX:41 is answered. Mismatches recorded, not resolved here: Audio files the Filter edge as **hard for the lie family** (a lie without its sound mode leaves HR-5 unmet), which this GDD adopts in its Dependencies table; Audio's run ruling "exactly one class" versus PF-20's two MVP ids is resolved by Audio (OQ-AA-10 closed, CX-F2): one class-(b) signature realised under the two Filter ids `FA_LIE_SIG_GAUGE` and `FA_LIE_SIG_SPRITE` (AA-35).

**Frame-order contract (summary of PF-35).** Per render frame: Avatar Presenter, then the Filter (re-evaluates dirty `perceived` refs, advances breath and wander on `present_time`, updates envelopes, emits audio requests), then Lighting reads the decoy list and draws overlays. The Filter never reads another presenter's output.

**Ownership summary (who owns what in a lie).**
