| `RUN_FRAMES` | int | about 12 at 60 fps, about 29 at 144 fps | derived | frames per sanity run |
| `c_slot`, `c_honest`, `c_eval`, `c_write` | float ms | 0.002, 0.005, 0.006, 0.004 `[PLACEHOLDER]` `[UNV]` (`c_eval + c_write` = the old `c_lie` 0.010) | none sourceable | GDScript per-call costs are not in the engine reference; these are budget shapes, measured by the Debug Overlay counter `render_cpu_us`; the evaluation cost and the write cost are separate so a lie that has not changed costs only its evaluation |
| `CPU_P99_MS`, `CPU_MAX_MS` | float ms | 1.0, 2.0 `[PLACEHOLDER]` | Filter | the 99th-percentile and worst-frame bounds; a median alone hides the restore and stage-boundary frames |
| `CPU_BUDGET_MS` | float ms | 0.5 `[PLACEHOLDER]`, range 0.3 to 1.0 | Filter | median presentation cost per frame |

**Output Range:** `C_pf` in about 0.05 to 0.32 ms on a median frame and at most 0.69 ms on a restore or `hard_start` frame (all 80 refs dirty). **Example:** median frame `24 x 0.002 + 6.7 x 0.005 + 24 x (0.006 + 0.004) = 0.048 + 0.033 + 0.240 = 0.32 ms`, inside 0.5 with a 36 % margin; worst frame `0.048 + 80 x 0.005 + 0.240 = 0.69 ms`, inside `CPU_P99_MS` 1.0 and `CPU_MAX_MS` 2.0; the lie term dominates, which is why `LIES_MAX x S_gleam` and write-on-change are the levers. The old model polled all 80 refs every frame (`80 x 0.002 + 24 x 0.010 + (80 / 12) x 0.005 = 0.43 ms`) and bounded only the median *(Amended 2026-10-27 — design review)*. `STEP_BUDGET_MS` does not apply (the Filter registers no hook). The constants are unmeasured placeholders (`[UNV]`): the limits bind the measurement on the two reference machines (OQ-PF-25), not these figures.

### D.14 Presentation hash

The presentation hash formula (registry: `presentation_hash`) is defined as:

`mix(a) = ((a XOR (a >> 16)) * 0x45D9F3B) AND 0xFFFFFFFF`, applied twice, then `a XOR (a >> 16)` (every intermediate masked to 32 bits)

`H(viewer, ref_key, lie_key, epoch)`: `k = 0`; for each part `p` in order: `k = mix((k * 31 + p) AND 0xFFFFFFFF)` *(Amended 2026-10-27 — design review)*

`lie_key = start_run AND 0xFFFFFFFF`, replaced by `0xFFFFFFFF` when that is 0, so a lie's key is never 0 (0 means no lie, PF-8). A fringe has no lie, so its `ref_key` is a reserved effect id (RIM `0xFFFF0001`, OUTLINE `0xFFFF0002`, BLEED `0xFFFF0003`; PF-9 ref keys never use `0xFFFF0000` and above) and its `lie_key` slot carries 1 (rim, bleed) or the subject seat's PlayerId + 1 (outline)

`sub(H, tag) = mix((H + tag * 0x9E3779B1) AND 0xFFFFFFFF)`; `cycle(H, n) = mix((sub(H, 1) + n * 0x85EBCA6B) AND 0xFFFFFFFF)`

**Variables:**

| Variable | Type | Range | Source | Description |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| `viewer` | uint32 | PlayerId | PF-10 | never a `peer_id` |
| `ref_key` | uint32 | 0 to `0xFFFEFFFF` for refs; `0xFFFF0000` and above reserved for effects | PF-9: kind and id, never an instance id | stable ref identity |
| `lie_key` *(Amended 2026-10-27 — design review)* | uint32 | 1 to 2^32 - 1 | the entry's `start_run` (it is in the saved ledger, and Sanity allows one live lie per subject, so it is unique per ref at any time) | masked to 32 bits first (E-PF-46) and forced non-zero; Sanity's entry carries no `lie_id` (OQ-PF-35). It is also the `lie_id` of the `Perceived` record. A real minted id would replace it without changing any draw's structure |
| `epoch` *(Amended 2026-10-27 — design review)* | uint32 | 0 to 2^32 - 1 | PF-37: increments only while the effect's amplitude is 0 | **0 for every per-lie draw** (tags 2 to 11 and a lie's own gleam breath): the epoch is presentation-local and not saved (AC-PF-70), a restore resets it, and a per-lie draw that read it would change on a restore, which is what made the earlier D.14 untestable across a restore. It is used only by the rim, bleed and outline breath envelopes (tag 1), where a re-draw is invisible (amplitude 0) and a restored sequence may differ (breath phase may differ after a restore, PF-33) |
| `tag` | int | 1 breath, 2 glide rate, 3 glide magnitude, 4 wander frequency, 5 wander phase, 6 tick stagger and sign, 7 mode-drop choice, 8 glide direction, 9 rotor modulation period, 10 rotor modulation phase, 11 sound-envelope period | this GDD | draw identity |

**Output Range:** uint32. **Operand bounds:** the largest product is a 32-bit value times the constant `0x45D9F3B` (< 2^27), below 2^59, and `k * 31 + p` is below 2^37, so no step depends on GDScript integer overflow or shift semantics `[UNV]` (the masks are explicit, so the result is identical under 32-bit wrap or 64-bit arithmetic). Statistical quality (avalanche, modulo bias of `mod 2001`, about 5e-7) is a test, not an assumption (AC-PF-30). It is never `rng_derive` (sim-only, KERN D.10) and never the global RNG.

**Example (test vectors, computed by a scratch model; the GDScript must reproduce them):** `H(1, 0x1234, 7, 0) = 735845735` (viewer 1, `ref_key` 0x1234, `lie_key` 7 from `start_run` 7, `epoch` 0); `sub` tag 1 = 2012313603, tag 2 = 860677879 (`mod 1001` = 61), tag 3 = 2970622220 (566), tag 4 = 1008397857 (467), tag 5 = 3021564105 (`mod 1000` = 105), tag 6 = 1256265666, tag 7 = 4252627420, tag 8 = 2605191723 (tags 6 and 7 added from the QA independent recomputation), tag 9 = 1981835580 (`mod 1001` = 725, `T_rotor` = 16.7 s), tag 10 = 2916972829 (`mod 1000` = 829, `p_rotor` = 5.209 rad); `cycle` 0 = 2870072146 (`mod 2001` = 1828, `T_0 = 5828 ms`), `cycle` 1 = 137788599 (1740, 5740 ms), `cycle` 2 = 1819539049 (1735, 5735 ms). Also `mix(0) = 0`, `mix(1) = 824515495`. **Added 2026-10-27 (design review), recomputed by an independent scratch model that first reproduced every vector above unchanged:** tag 11 = 3023762598 (`mod 2001` = 1473, `T_s` = 5473 ms); the masked-overflow vector `lie_key(start_run = 2^32 + 7)` = 7 so `H` = 735845735; the forced non-zero vector `lie_key(start_run = 0)` = `lie_key(2^32)` = 4294967295 so `H(1, 0x1234, 4294967295, 0)` = 1114458501; the rim vectors `H(1, 0xFFFF0001, 1, 0)` = 4079351472 (`cycle` 0 = 716281272, `mod 2001` = 1311, 5311 ms), `epoch` 1 = 4005588813 (`cycle` 0 = 381151264, 784, 4784 ms), `epoch` 2 = 136756076 (`cycle` 0 = 1022581, 70, 4070 ms); the outline vector `H(1, 0xFFFF0002, 3, 0)` = 2134790335 (seat 2; `cycle` 0 `mod 2001` = 857, 4857 ms). All are in the committed constant file `tests/fixtures/perception_filter/hash_vectors.json` (AC-PF-30).

### D.15 Audio onset and level

The audio onset and level formula is defined as:

`t_sound_on = 0` (the first ACTIVE frame), invariant `t_sound_on <= t_glide_visible`, `t_glide_visible = LEAK_EPS_DEG / rate`

`amp_snd_i(t) = amp_i(t)` with `breath01 := 1` while ACTIVE (D.5; on a needle class `amp_snd = amp`, those classes do not breathe); in RETURN, the D.5 RETURN rule applied to `amp_snd_end` (the unbreathed value latched at removal) in place of `amp_end`, so the sound falls on the same `R_AMP` follower as the gleam and does not drop by the breath factor at removal *(Amended 2026-10-27 — re-review, delegated ruling R8)*

`LEVEL_pm_i(t) = amp_snd_i(t)` (an int 0 to 1000 per mille of `AMP_MAX_PM`) if SOUND is in `leak_modes`, else no request. ON carries `(LEVEL_pm_i(0), T_s, SOUND_BREATH_FLOOR_PM)` and sets `last_sent := LEVEL_pm_i(0)`; a LEVEL request is sent when `abs(LEVEL_pm_i(t) - last_sent) >= AUDIO_LEVEL_STEP_PM` and sets `last_sent := LEVEL_pm_i(t)`; `LEVEL_pm = 0` is a level, not an OFF; OFF is sent once, at CLEAR, and never earlier. There is no sum over lies and no division, so no zero-denominator case exists (G5): a lie whose amplitude reaches 0 before CLEAR (an early debunk) sends LEVEL 0 and keeps its voice requested until CLEAR *(Amended 2026-10-27 — design review)* *(Amended 2026-10-27 — re-review, delegated ruling R8)*

**Variables:**

| Variable | Type | Range | Source | Description |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| `t_glide_visible` | float s | 0.125 to 0.83 s (0.25 deg / 2.0 to 0.3 deg/s; 0.5 s at the unscaled rate floor 0.5) | D.2, D.5 | time at which the needle first departs by `LEAK_EPS_DEG` (0.25 deg / 1.19 to 2.0 deg/s) |
| `amp` | int pm | D.5 | D.5 | the leak amplitude |
| `AUDIO_LEVEL_STEP_PM` | int pm | 20 `[PLACEHOLDER]`, range 10 to 50 | Filter | request-traffic limiter |
| `amp_snd`, `LEVEL_pm` *(Amended 2026-10-27 — re-review, delegated ruling R8)* | int pm | 0 to 1000 | D.5 | the SOUND amplitude, the lie's `amp` with `breath01` removed; per mille of `AMP_MAX_PM`, the Filter's own discrepancy envelope. It is the value Audio reads as `leak_amplitude_pm` and converts to dB (Audio D.15); the Filter states no dB, no consequence-class reference and no ceiling or combined cap (all Audio's, on the `hal` bus) |
| `last_sent` *(Amended 2026-10-27 — re-review)* | int pm | 0 to 1000 | derived, presentation-local | the last level sent to Audio for this lie: set by ON, reset to none on `hard_start` (the restore re-ON below) |
| `SOUND_BREATH_FLOOR_PM` *(Amended 2026-10-27 — design review)* *(Amended 2026-10-27 — re-review)* | int pm | 500 `[PLACEHOLDER]`, range 300 to 800 | Filter | the trough of the sound's breath envelope as a fraction of its peak, carried on the ON request together with `T_s` (drawn per lie from tag 11 in 4000 to 6000 ms): the one breath authority for the lie sound; Audio's own period and depth constants are fallbacks |
| `SOUND_LEAD_MAX_MS` | int ms | 100 `[PLACEHOLDER]`, range 0 to 200 | Filter / Audio | the latest the sound may begin after the first ACTIVE frame |

**Output Range:** the request goes ON on the first ACTIVE frame, so sound leads the first visible displacement by `t_glide_visible - 0` = 0.125 to 0.83 s; HR-5 holds as **requested no later than the first visible frame** (the ON is stamped on the first ACTIVE frame, ahead of the glide by `t_glide_visible`); the audible start is Audio's, bounded by `SOUND_LEAD_MAX_MS` and measured on the real audio thread under the AC-AA-6 spike, not on the Filter's stub (AC-PF-103) *(Amended 2026-10-27 — re-review)*. **Example:** `rate` 1.19: `t_glide_visible = 0.25 / 1.19 = 0.21 s`; `rate` 2.0: 0.125 s. The class ids are `FA_LIE_SIG_GAUGE` and `FA_LIE_SIG_SPRITE` (PF-20); a lie whose SOUND mode is dropped has no request at all. A muted or unavailable audio system leaves the lie two modes. **Level contract** *(Amended 2026-10-27 — design review)* *(Amended 2026-10-27 — re-review, delegated ruling R8)*: the request is the raw per-lie `amp_snd`. One lying instrument at `amp` 1000 requests LEVEL 1000, and four lying instruments at `amp` 1000 request 1000 each (four voices; the combined cap is Audio's on the `hal` bus, over the voices that survive `R_hal`); at the stage 0 to 1 floor onset the request is 400, at stage 2 it is 325 and at the stage-3 floor it is 250 (user ruling D74, the floors themselves) *(Amended 2026-10-27 — user rulings)*. **Worked conversion at Audio's registered defaults (cited, Audio-owned)**: `L_peak = -42 + 24 x LEVEL / 1000`, so LEVEL 1000 is -18 dBr (`HAL_CEIL_DB`, below the witness nominal by `HAL_BUS_REL_DB`), 400 is -32.4 dBr, 250 is -36 dBr; the sound breath at `SOUND_BREATH_FLOOR_PM` 500 takes 6.02 dB off the peak, so the lone stage-3 floor lie breathes between -36 and -42.0 dBr, above `HAL_ACTIVE_MIN_DB` -48 (it stays above it down to `SOUND_BREATH_FLOOR_PM` 300, -46.5 dBr). The audible minimum and the combined cap are Audio's to confirm. Audio keeps distance attenuation, ducking and the assets (PF-20).

**Restore, pause and hitch on the audio side** *(E-PF-21, E-PF-24, E-PF-26)* *(Amended 2026-10-27 — re-review)*: (1) a restore with a lie ACTIVE re-issues the lie's ON on the first RUNNING frame (a restore tears every `hal` voice down, so the Filter treats it as a fresh onset for audio): `last_sent` is reset and the ON carries the level of the first frame's `amp_snd` and the lie's restore-exact `(T_s, floor)` (the hash key is the saved `start_run`); a RETURN restored shows no lie (E-PF-5) and so issues no ON; (2) pause: no LEVEL, ON or OFF request is issued while PAUSED, the `hal` voices pause with the sim clock (the pause policy of the voices, mute or hold, is Audio's), and on resume the first frame re-sends a LEVEL only if the step rule requires it; (3) hitch: `amp_snd` is computed from the `dt`-clamped follower state (`DT_SLEW_MAX_S`, E-PF-26), so a 0.5 s frame produces at most one LEVEL request and the level never jumps by more than the RETURN rate allows.

### D.16 SIM-to-pixel latency

The SIM-to-pixel latency formula (registry: `sim_to_pixel_latency`) is defined as:

`L_max_ms = SANITY_DRIFT_DIVISOR * 1000 / TICK_RATE + frame_ms`

**Variables:**

| Variable | Type | Range | Source | Description |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| `SANITY_DRIFT_DIVISOR` | int | 12 (cited) | Sanity | ticks per sanity run |
| `TICK_RATE` | int | 60 (cited) | Kernel | sim ticks per second |
| `frame_ms` | float ms | 6.9 (144 Hz) to 33.3 (30 fps) | display | one frame |

**Output Range:** 200 ms plus one frame. **Example:** at 60 Hz `200 + 16.7 = 216.7 ms`; at 144 Hz `206.9 ms`. It applies to **stage steps and entry appearance and removal** only; lies, ramps and breaths then evolve on their own clocks. Accepted (PF-35) and stated in Game Feel.

### D.17 Rotor cadence of a SPRITE_SWAP lie

The rotor cadence formula is defined as:

`f_rotor(tau) = ROTOR_BASE_FRAC * F_run * (1 + ROTOR_DRIFT_FRAC * sin(2 * pi * tau / T_rotor + p_rotor))` (the steady lie cadence)

Onset *(Amended 2026-10-27 — re-review)*: `f_shown(tau) = F_honest + (f_rotor(tau) - F_honest) * smoothstep(0, ROTOR_WINDUP_S, tau - SWAP_LEAD_S)`: the gleam leads, exactly as for a static pair (`disc := nu(lie_tau)` while ACTIVE, D.5 and D.6: it rises over `SWAP_LEAD_S` and holds), and the cadence starts to wind up only at `tau = SWAP_LEAD_S`

Constraint: `abs(f_rotor(tau) - F_run) >= 0.05 * F_run` at all `tau` (ACTIVE)

`nu_rot = LEAK_D_FULL_DEG * min(1, abs(f_shown - F_honest) / (ROTOR_FULL_FRAC * F_run))`

Return *(Amended 2026-10-27 — re-review)*: `disc := nu_rot` with `nu_end := nu_rot at removal` (D.6's `nu(lie_tau)` when the removal precedes the wind-up); the cadence moves toward `F_honest` at the constant rate `df/dt = rm_ret * GLIDE_RETURN_RATE * ROTOR_FULL_FRAC * F_run / LEAK_D_FULL_DEG`; while `abs(f_shown - F_honest) >= ROTOR_FULL_FRAC * F_run` (`nu_rot` saturated) the discrepancy and the gleam hold at full; when `abs(f_shown - F_honest) <= ROTOR_SNAP_FRAC * F_run` the rotor snaps to the honest cadence (a frame cut) and the gleam continues on `nu` from `LEAK_D_FULL_DEG * ROTOR_SNAP_FRAC / ROTOR_FULL_FRAC`, falling at `rm_ret * GLIDE_RETURN_RATE` (D.6)

**Variables:**

| Variable | Type | Range | Source | Description |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| `F_run` | float frames/s | > 0 | the machine's own running cadence (Instruments sheet, provisional) | the honest cadence when running |
| `F_honest` | frames/s | 0 on a stopped machine | the true state | the cadence the machine should show |
| `ROTOR_BASE_FRAC` | float | 0.75 `[PLACEHOLDER]`, range 0.6 to 0.9 | Filter | base of the lie, never the honest rate |
| `ROTOR_DRIFT_FRAC` | float | 0.15 `[PLACEHOLDER]`, range 0.05 to 0.30 | Filter | modulation depth |
| `T_rotor`, `p_rotor` | s, rad | 8 to 20, 0 to 2 pi | D.14 tags 9 and 10: `T_rotor = 8 + 12 x (h9 mod 1001) / 1000` s; `p_rotor = 2 pi x (h10 mod 1000) / 1000` | modulation period and phase |
| `ROTOR_FULL_FRAC` | float | 0.4 `[PLACEHOLDER]`, range 0.2 to 0.6 | Filter | cadence error that counts as a full discrepancy |
| `ROTOR_WINDUP_S` *(Amended 2026-10-27 — re-review)* | float s | 3 `[PLACEHOLDER]`, range 1 to 6 | Filter | the cadence wind-up after the gleam lead (smoothstep from the honest cadence to `f_rotor`) |
| `ROTOR_SNAP_FRAC` *(Amended 2026-10-27 — re-review)* | float | 0.05 `[PLACEHOLDER]`, range 0.02 to 0.15, and below `ROTOR_FULL_FRAC` | Filter | the minimum cadence, as a fraction of `F_run`, at which the returning rotor snaps to the honest cadence (a slower rotor reads as stopped) |
| `f_shown`, `df/dt` *(Amended 2026-10-27 — re-review)* | float frames/s | `f_shown` between `F_honest` and `f_rotor`; `df/dt` 0.06 `F_run` per s at `rm_ret` 1.0, 0.0498 at 0.83 | derived | the cadence actually drawn and the constant return rate |

**Output Range:** `f_rotor` in `[0.6375, 0.8625] x F_run` at defaults (lint `ROTOR_BASE_FRAC x (1 + ROTOR_DRIFT_FRAC) <= 0.95`); it never settles to the honest running rate. **Example** *(Amended 2026-10-27 — re-review)*: a stopped machine (`F_honest` 0): the gleam rises over `SWAP_LEAD_S` 3 s with the rotor still; at `tau` 3 s the cadence winds up over `ROTOR_WINDUP_S` 3 s to about 0.75 `F_run`, so no first frame shows a running rotor with full gleam. On removal at 0.75 `F_run` the cadence winds down at `3.0 x 0.4 / 20 = 0.06 F_run` per second (0.0498 at `rm_ret` 0.83): `nu_rot` stays saturated at 20 until 0.4 `F_run` (5.83 s), then falls at 3 deg/s; the rotor snaps to stopped at 0.05 `F_run` at 11.67 s, where `nu` is 2.5 deg, and the gleam continues for `(2.5 - 0.25) / 3 = 0.75 s` more, so CLEAR is at about 12.4 s (about 15.0 s at `rm_ret` 0.83). A static-pair frame (cracked or whole, lit or unlit) uses D.6's `nu(t)` instead.

## Edge Cases

Every case states the exact outcome and the reason. Ids `E-PF-n` are cited by the rules and formulas (E-PF-3 by PF-14 and S3, E-PF-5 by PF-12 and PF-33, E-PF-46 by D.14). "Falls back to truth" means PF-44: the affected surface draws the honest path and a counter moves; a Filter fault never reaches the simulation or a TRUTH node.

### Lie start, hold and end

- **If a lie is accepted but its re-check fails or it is debunked while PENDING** *(E-PF-1)*: the entry never appears in `sanity.perception`, so nothing is drawn and `perceived` stays on the honest path (no pre-onset hint, SN-16). The Filter has no PENDING state of its own; this is why a debunk during the 2 to 5 s delay leaves no trace at all.
- **If a debunk (`field_check`) lands at ACTIVE `lie_tau` 0.1 s** *(E-PF-2)* *(Amended 2026-10-27 — design review)*: the entry leaves with `disc_end` about 0.1 deg, already under `LEAK_EPS_DEG`, so the unified RETURN follower of D.5 has no discrepancy tail (`amp_cand` is 0) and `amp_end` 400 falls at `R_AMP` 266.7 pm/s to exactly 0 in `LEAK_FADE_S` 1.5 s; the needle is already within `LEAK_EPS_DEG` of the truth, but the record stays RETURN, with `lie_id` held and the gleam fading, and CLEAR (the later of the discrepancy, the amplitude and the wander fade) arrives at `WANDER_BLEND_S` 3.0 s, when `motion_mode` returns to HONEST without an event. The earlier text left RETURN while the violet fade and the sound were still running, which put a gleam on an honest record. The first-frame bound of D.1 (at most 0.1 deg) means a lie that ends this early was never visibly displaced, but it did carry its gleam and its sound request, so it was never an unleaked lie.
- **If a new lie starts on a ref whose previous lie is still in RETURN** *(E-PF-3)*: the new lie **starts from its own formula** (discrepancy against the true value is 0 at `lie_tau` 0); the displayed value does **not** continue from the return's value, so an ACTIVE lie's displayed value never depends on presentation-local return state and PF-11 and PF-33 hold. The single slew state (D.3) closes the residual gap between the in-flight return value and the new lie's target at a rate of at most `GLIDE_RATE_MAX_DEG` (8.33 pm/s at 240 deg): no snap, and after a restore the displayed value equals the formula from the first frame. The gleam steps up on the frame the new entry appears (from the RETURN value to `floor_eff + (AMP_MAX_PM - floor_eff) x g(disc)`, a rise of up to about 400 per mille) *(Amended 2026-10-27 — user rulings)*. That step is accepted: it is a rise (the safe direction, never an unleaked moment), the new lie must carry its full leak from frame one (SN-15 L-L, SNV-13), and it cannot make a flash pair, because the other half of a pair would be the preceding return decline, which is below 0.10 per second by the D.6 coupling (D.11). Sanity's `LIE_GAP_S` normally prevents the case; it is defensive.
- **If EXPIRED, INVALIDATED and DEBUNKED each remove an entry at the same `disc_end` (48 deg)** *(E-PF-4)*: the `displayed_pm(t)` and `amp(t)` series are bit-identical across the three; no verdict flash, relief cue or sound sting. The Filter sees only that the entry left (PF-12), so the end type cannot become an oracle (SN-16, SNV-11, AC-SN-153).
- **If a save is loaded, or LOADING happens, while a lie is in RETURN** *(E-PF-5)*: the return track is presentation-local and is dropped; the entry had already left `sanity.perception`, so nothing is ACTIVE, and the first RUNNING frame (behind the black hold) shows the true value. This is not a clean-frame oracle (no lie is active to hide). The residual tell, "loading cleared the tail", is accepted (OQ-PF-9 answered: ended entries are not retained, loading clears the tail).
- **If `sanity.perception` holds two entries with the same `subject_ref` for the viewer** *(E-PF-6)* *(Amended 2026-10-27 — design review)*: only the lowest `start_run` draws, ties broken by the lowest position in the entry order of `sanity.perception` (requested to be ascending ledger `slot` order, OQ-PF-38); the other contributes no pixels and no sound; counter `lie_collisions`; no leak is lost because the winner carries its own (PF-46). Sanity's pre-draw gate enforces at most one live lie per (seat, subject_ref) (SN-16; registry `sanity.ledger`), so this tie-break is a defensive fallback.
- **If the true value moves through a FALSE_OK or FALSE_FAIL target** *(E-PF-7)* *(Amended 2026-10-27 — design review)*: the aim distance reaches 0 (the true value is inside the aim point's zone interval) and the displayed needle meets the truth; `amp` stays at its ACTIVE value (never below `floor_eff`, 400 at stages 0 to 1) until Sanity's re-check removes the entry within one run (0.2 s), after which the RETURN follower of D.5 applies. The needle meets the truth for at most that window; the gleam and the sound stay on throughout *(Amended 2026-10-27 — re-review)* (the amplitude does not step when the aim distance reaches 0: it falls only through the `R_AMP_ACT` follower).
- **If the true value steps by 500 pm in one Graph step (`apply_damage`, a repair) while a lie is ACTIVE** *(E-PF-8)* *(Amended 2026-10-27 — design review)*: GLIDE carries its offset with the truth (the target steps) and FALSE_* keeps the zone; the displayed value follows through the slew at `R_act` 8.33 pm/s and does not snap or settle (SNV-11, SNV-23), **except where the `DISC_CAP_PM` clamp binds**: the slew is held within 400 pm of the truth, so after a step or ramp larger than the cap the needle moves faster than `R_act` once, to the cap, and stays there (D.3; a bounded exception, OQ-PF-40). The discrepancy and the leak grow for as long as the slew lags, at worst about 48 s; when the step moves the truth **toward** a GLIDE needle and `disc` collapses by up to 400 pm in one frame, the gleam amplitude falls only at `R_AMP_ACT` (266.7 pm/s, 0.0776 of luminance per second), so the step is not visible as a gleam event and tells the player nothing about where the truth moved *(Amended 2026-10-27 — re-review)*; a step in the **true** needle is honest and is not the Filter's (Instruments apply their settle only when `motion_mode` is HONEST, which an ACTIVE lie is not). A truth ramp of 100 pm/s pins the discrepancy at -400 pm after 5.5 s (AC-PF-114).
- **If the subject of a lie is destroyed mid-glide (`true_value` no longer resolves)** *(E-PF-9)*: the entry leaves (INVALIDATED); the ref draws nothing (the instrument is gone), there is no return animation, and the per-lie state is freed the same frame. A Filter datum that throws or goes missing falls back to truth (PF-44).
- **If the truth sits at or near a dial stop in the departure direction, so the live clamp leaves less than `D_VIS_MIN_DEG`** *(E-PF-10)* *(Amended 2026-10-27 — design review)*: `D_eff` is a per-lie constant and is not reduced; the target is clamped live to `[W_M, 1000 - W_M]` (D.3), so displayed-minus-true is smaller than usual for as long as the truth stays there. The lie is still drawn with its gleam, its sound and its **wander, the needle's hue-independent motion mode, which the clamp never removes** (the needle never pins at a stop: a pinned needle rests); `lie_short_reach` counts once per lie. A needle lie therefore always has GLEAM plus a motion mode. It is a weak lie, not an unleaked one, and Sanity's pre-draw gate should refuse a subject with no room (OQ-PF-38).
- **If the calibration record is missing or `needle_len_px` is below the 14.3 art-px eligibility floor (`NEEDLE_LEN_MIN_PX` default 16)** *(E-PF-11)* *(Amended 2026-10-27 — design review)*: a missing record draws nothing for the ref and counts `lie_unrenderable` (a build failure for any kind listed in `lie_targets.json`, PF-6 vi). A short needle cannot give a needle glide (`28.6 / 14 = 2.04 > GLIDE_RATE_MAX_DEG`) and a gleam with a sound has no hue-independent motion mode, so **the surface is not lied on** (the honest path, `lie_unrenderable`) unless the same ref has a visible digit mechanism whose TICK, or STALE with the gleam breath, is then its motion mode (PF-15 table); a build lint excludes such a gauge from `lie_targets.json` (requested of Sanity's data, OQ-PF-38). The earlier text kept "the gleam and the readout mode", which for a gauge with no numeral meant a gleam and a sound only.
- **If a SPRITE_SWAP lie targets a machine whose sheet lacks the opposite honest frame** *(E-PF-12)* *(Amended 2026-10-27 — design review)*: no swap is drawn (the Filter never invents or blends a frame) and, because a gleam on an unchanged sprite is a violet mark on an honest surface with no motion mode, **no gleam and no sound are drawn either**: the ref takes the honest path, counter `lie_unrenderable`, log `SPRITE_FRAME_MISSING`. The content gate (SN-16 plus Instruments and Derelict Authoring) is the first defence; the lie is lost whole and never half-drawn.
- **If a lie's onset lands during an Instruments settle in progress** *(E-PF-62)* *(Amended 2026-10-27 — design review)*: Instruments clears its settle and tremor triple on the first non-HONEST frame and the drawn needle moves to `displayed_pm` by at most the settle's current deviation (In-World Instruments IW:243, checkpoint ruling C14). That deviation is bounded by the honest step that started the settle, which is one LS-20 quantum (for example 20 permille of range for a store) except after a Graph step such as `apply_damage`, where it can be larger; an honest needle at rest has none, and the first lie is drawn on a fault-free subject at rest (SN-16). The one-frame move is accepted: it ends a settle that was already running rather than starting an uncaused move, the leak (gleam at `floor_eff`, the sound request) is on from the same frame, and any tremor in progress is blended out by `tremor_weight_pm` over `TREMOR_BLEND_S`. The Filter adds no additive tail (it brushes PF-17, OQ-IW-6).

### Stage, ramp and QA flags

- **If the stage drops while an envelope is mid-ramp** *(E-PF-13)*: the envelope reverses from its current value with its rate re-latched (D.7), so the return takes `STAGE_RAMP_S` (8 s) from any value (from 0.5 at 0.0625 per second, from 0.375 at 0.047 per second); no restart at 0, no relief cue; ACTIVE lies run on (a stage change never ends a lie, SN-16). The hash `epoch` of the effect increments only when its envelope is 0, so a re-draw is invisible.
- **If the stage flaps around a threshold** *(E-PF-14)*: the hysteresis (`STAGE_HYST` 2000 mSU) and the dwell (`STAGE_DWELL_RUNS` 25, 5 s) keep reversals at least 5 s apart; the envelope integrates the net direction continuously, so between flips it moves by at most 5/8 of the current step (5 s of an 8 s transition), and no opposing pair can fall inside 1 s (D.11).
- **If `force_stage` jumps the stage from 0 to 3, or sets any stage** *(E-PF-15)*: `hard_start`: every envelope is set to its target on the first rendered frame with no ramp (rim and bleed at their stage-3 gains), every ACTIVE lie is drawn at the formula's value for the current `lie_tau`, and counter `first_frame_filtered` moves. QA flags exist only in debug builds (SN-28); two flips inside 1 s are outside the flash model and outside release.
- **If `force_level` changes the sanity level with a lie ACTIVE** *(E-PF-16)*: as E-PF-15; the lie's presence, kind and leak are unchanged (the entry is Sanity's), only the stage-bound envelopes jump; the picture is never presented clean for one frame (PF-33).
- **If the stage-3 pipe sheet lacks the `pipe_shared_msk` edge channel (or is not authored)** *(E-PF-17)*: at the shipped default (`AMBIENT_FRINGES_ENABLED` false) the bleed is off everywhere and this case does not arise *(Amended 2026-10-27 — user rulings)*; with the opt-in flag on, the hue bleed is disabled for that sheet and the Debug Overlay lists it as `bleed_unavailable`; stage 3 still leaks through the gleam and the whole-sprite lies; no error (PF-24, cut first).
- **If a stage-0 seat (above `STAGE_ENTER[1]`) receives an entry after a recovery, or an entry targets a surface beyond its stage's reach (a glyph at stage 1)** *(E-PF-18)*: the Filter draws what is listed and never decides whether (Sanity owns eligibility, PF-22); the Debug Overlay shows the kind and the stage side by side so the mismatch is visible; the lie still carries its full leak.
- **If the stage rises or falls with no lie ACTIVE** *(E-PF-19)*: only the stage-bound envelopes (rim, bleed, outline) move; no lie is created, ended or announced; a stage change is weather, never an event (PF-21).
- **If the stage-2 rim fringe is ramping when its subject beam is occluded or off** *(E-PF-20)*: the envelope keeps its value; the band is simply not drawn along occluded rim segments (E-PF-54) and there is no pulse on re-emergence. In LIE_BOUND mode (the default) the last reach lie ending while the beam is occluded or off ramps the envelope out over `STAGE_RAMP_S` with nothing drawn, and a reach lie starting then ramps it in from its current value *(Amended 2026-10-27 — user rulings)*

### Save, restore, pause and hitches

- **If a save is restored with a lie ACTIVE** *(E-PF-21)* *(Amended 2026-10-27 — design review)*: the first RUNNING frame draws `displayed_pm` at the formula's value for the restored `lie_tau` (D.3), `amp` at the ACTIVE value for the restored discrepancy, envelopes at their targets, and a full leak from frame one; wander and breath phase may differ (SNV-13); every per-lie constant is identical (the hash key is the saved `start_run` and `epoch` is 0, D.14); the displayed value differs from the pre-save value only by a slew gap that was still closing at the save (E-PF-63); the lie's sound ON is re-issued on that first RUNNING frame with `last_sent` reset (D.15) *(Amended 2026-10-27 — re-review)*.
- **If a save lands while the slew is closing a gap (after a truth step or a lie hand-off)** *(E-PF-63)* *(Amended 2026-10-27 — design review)*: the slew is presentation-local and is not saved, so the first RUNNING frame shows the formula's value (`hard_start` sets `slew_pm` to the target, held within `DISC_CAP_PM`): the displayed value differs from the pre-save value by exactly the gap that was still open, at most 400 pm and typically a few pm, while ACTIVE presence, kind, leak modes and every per-lie constant are identical. PF-11, PF-33 and PFV-14 promise restore-exactness of the lie and its constants, and of the displayed value up to that gap (AC-PF-4b).
- **If a save lands while a lie is PENDING** *(E-PF-22)*: nothing is drawn; the entry keeps its `start_run`, `end_run` and delay and activates on schedule (EC-SN-29), after which the Filter treats it as a fresh ACTIVE entry whose `act_run` Sanity reports.
- **If an entry arrives without `active_run` and a restore lands** *(E-PF-23)*: defensive fallback only (Sanity publishes `active_run`, registry `sanity.perception`, so this does not occur in a conforming build): the Filter's latch falls back to `start_run + 10` (the `ONSET_DELAY_RUNS` minimum; never the restore run); the displayed value differs from the pre-save value by at most 3 s x 2 deg/s = 6 deg, behind the black hold; the claim of PF-11 and PF-33 holds exactly whenever `active_run` is present.
- **If the game is PAUSED** *(E-PF-24)*: the tick, `tick_alpha` and `present_time` are frozen, so every lie clock, ramp, breath, wander and digit tick freezes; the paused frame still contains every ACTIVE leak (hue and edge presence do not depend on motion), and a screenshot is a stable still; on resume nothing jumps and nothing flashes; no audio request is issued while PAUSED and the `hal` voices follow Audio's pause policy (D.15) *(Amended 2026-10-27 — re-review)*.
- **If the game is PAUSED during a RETURN** *(E-PF-25)*: the follower and the leak decay freeze mid-value; on resume they continue from the same value at the same rate (the amplitude never restarts).
- **If a render hitch makes the frame delta 0.5 s** *(E-PF-26)*: `dt` is clamped to `DT_SLEW_MAX_S` 0.1 s for the slew and the ramps, so displayed motion is capped at `R_act x 0.1`; the sim-time `lie_tau` advances by the true 0.5 s and the next frames catch up at the bounded rate; no visible jump and no flash.

### Zoom, geometry and the camera

- **If the zoom ladder changes step while a lie is ACTIVE** *(E-PF-27)*: the zoom is a cut (PIPE C6); `displayed_pm` is unchanged (it is in dial space); fringe and gleam widths are re-derived as `n x k'` on the first frame of the new rung and never interpolated; no leak mode is removed.
- **If the field is the largest (zoom 1x on 2160p, 3840x2160 art px, 36 times the default area)** *(E-PF-28)*: every on-screen lie is still drawn with every mode; level-of-detail may reduce amplitude detail (breath sampling rate only; the wander amplitude and frequency are never reduced, D.4), never remove a mode (a culled leak is an unleaked lie, PF-34); the draw count stays bounded by `LIES_MAX`, not the field (D.12).
- **If the window moves to a monitor of a different size so `k_auto` changes** *(E-PF-29)*: the ladder top moves (`k_auto + 2`), widths re-derive as in E-PF-27, and the smallest-region flash bound is re-evaluated at the new field (the flash scan covers the largest field).
- **If a lying surface is on screen but its host is unlit** *(E-PF-30)*: no gleam is drawn (the gleam rides the host's lit draw, PF-16), no prompt appears (IA-13) and the lie clock still runs; the Filter makes no `lit(ref)` query. An unlit gleam would be a location oracle and a phantom in the dark.
- **If a lying surface is off screen** *(E-PF-31)*: nothing is drawn and no shader work is done; because the trajectory is a function of sim time it reappears at the correct point of its glide, with no onset animation (PF-34).

### Hallucination interplay and the co-op hooks

- **If `perceived(ref, P)` is called with a PlayerId that is not the local seat** *(E-PF-32)*: the honest record returns (`displayed` equals the true value, `leak_amplitude_pm` 0, `motion_mode` HONEST, `lie_id` 0); counter `remote_queries`; never an error (PF-10). Answering from replicated data would let a client compute another's lies.
- **If the viewer is themself `tell_on`** *(E-PF-33)*: the victim tell of **other** seats is rendered normally (absence would be a self-sign, PF-28); nothing about the viewer's own body changes.
- **If `harm_eligible` sets mid-stride (VS-B)** *(E-PF-34)*: on the next rendered frame every other bound seat's body is substituted by a horror rig at the true position and driven by the true velocity (hard cut, no morph, no glimpse); the true beam stays anchored to the true chest point and the true voice plays (PF-29).
- **If `harm_eligible` clears (immediate, no dwell on exit)** *(E-PF-35)*: the rig is replaced by the crewmate body on the next rendered frame, again a hard cut; the monsterised rig did not draw the held tool's body, while the tool's true emission VFX stayed (OQ-SN-26 recommendation).
- **If `tell_on(B)` and `harm_eligible(A)` both hold** *(E-PF-36)*: one fringe (the rig's contour with `leak` above 0) is drawn, never two (SNV-17).
- **If a substituted or phantom rig is rendered with `leak` 0** *(E-PF-37)*: the build lint fails (HE-40, HE:1189); at runtime the Filter raises it to the minimum visible leak and counts `leak_repaired`; a rig with no violet contour would be an unleaked lie.
- **If two viewers share one machine (VS-B)** *(E-PF-38)*: seat A with a lie on gauge G sees D.3's value, seat B sees honest; the hash includes `viewer`, so their wander and breath sequences differ even for the same `lie_key`.
- **If a lie is ACTIVE on a gauge while an endogenous breach alarm fires** *(E-PF-39)*: the lie neither shows nor suppresses the warning mark, the alarm sweep or any tell (anchors); `W_MIN_WARNING_S` (10 s, Graph) and "alarm class never first" are Sanity's and the Graph's gates; the Filter's draw is unaffected (LS-13).
- **If a lamp-housing glow sprite lies** *(E-PF-40)*: only the appearance frame swaps; the real lamp light, the floor pool and `lux` are unchanged and TRUTH (AC-SN-122); a housing lit over a cold floor is the whole lie.

### Degenerate strategies and settings

- **If the player zooms to 1x to survey the ship for liars** *(E-PF-41)*: lies on screen and lit show every mode at their normal strength; nothing extra is revealed (no extra leak, no count), and the number of lies is bounded by `LIES_MAX`; zooming in cannot hide a leak either (widths scale with `k`).
- **If the player mutes audio, or has no audio device** *(E-PF-42)*: the lie keeps the gleam and the glide (two modes); the player's mute is not a Filter event, and the sound request is still emitted (Audio decides playback).
- **If the player turns on reduced motion mid-lie** *(E-PF-43)* *(Amended 2026-10-27 — design review)*: `rm` is latched per lie at activation and at `hard_start`, so an in-flight lie keeps its time axis and new lies use the scaled one (`D_eff` is identical either way, so no lie is smaller); the wander amplitude and frequency are never scaled, the digit tick is never lowered below `LIE_TICK_MS`, and the breath stays (HR-7). The return uses `rm_ret = max(rm, RM_RETURN_FLOOR)`, so the assertion `rm_ret x GLIDE_RETURN_RATE >= rm x GLIDE_RATE_MAX_DEG` holds and the reduced-motion return takes at most about 1.2 times the normal one.
- **If the player runs an OS colour filter or is hue-blind** *(E-PF-44)*: no global remap or daltonization is applied (HR-10); the glide and the breathing carry the lie without hue; a hue-removed tester still finds the liar by motion and by sound (AC-SN-154).
- **If the player stares at the pause screen's frame to find the liar** *(E-PF-45)*: the paused frame contains the same leaks as the live frame and nothing more; a still needle shows no motion, but the gleam, the edge and the housing frames remain, so pausing neither hides nor reveals a lie.

### Numeric, content and build-time cases

- **If a `start_run`, `ref_key` or `viewer` exceeds 32 bits** *(E-PF-46)* *(Amended 2026-10-27 — design review)*: it is masked to 32 bits before mixing (D.14); the lie key is forced non-zero (`start_run` 0 or 2^32 maps to 0xFFFFFFFF); a collision only changes presentation phase, never a displayed value or a leak.
- **If the dead zones leave no legal breath period** *(E-PF-47)*: the draw clamps deterministically to the nearer zone boundary inside the 4000 to 6000 ms band; a zone wider than the band is a boot assertion failure (a configuration error).
- **If a node has no `PerceptionTag`, or a CORRUPTIBLE tag's `lie_surface_id` is absent from `lie_targets.json`** *(E-PF-48)*: an untagged node is TRUTH and counts `tag_defaults_applied` once per node per session (never an error); the missing id fails the build and at runtime the node is treated as TRUTH (PF-4, PF-6).
- **If an entry arrives with empty `leak_modes`, or with `leak_modes` that lacks the GLEAM bit** *(E-PF-49)*: a debug build asserts; a release build draws GLEAM and GLIDE for an empty entry, **adds GLEAM** to any entry that arrives without it (keeping the modes it did carry), and counts `leak_repaired` once per lie, so neither an unleaked lie nor a palette-silent lie is ever drawn (PF-15).
- **If the stage-3 worst case would exceed `D_FX_ADDED_MAX` draws (neither the instance-parameter path nor the MultiMesh overlay is available, path 3 of D.12)** *(E-PF-50)* *(Amended 2026-10-27 — design review)*: the lies that do not fit are not drawn at all (the oldest survive: lowest `start_run`, then entry order); counter `filter_draws_dropped` counts those lies; every lie that is drawn keeps GLEAM **and** its motion mode, so the invariant of PF-15 holds on the fallback and is linted (AC-PF-108). The earlier claim that "the glide and the sound need no draw" was false for the static-pair classes, whose motion mode is the gleam breath, and the sound is not a motion mode. Surviving draws per stage: D.12.
- **If per-instance shader parameters are unavailable or capped on a tested GPU or backend** *(E-PF-51)* *(Amended 2026-10-27 — design review)*: the gleam transport falls to the MultiMesh overlay (D.12 path 2, spike S-1c) and, if that fails its batching, ordering or light-mask test, to path 3 and then to the recorded reduced lie set (PF-42); `self_modulate` is **not** a fallback (it cannot draw an edge-only gleam and would take the host's only modulate channel); spike S-1a decides before the rules depend on it `[UNV]`.
- **If the container material slot is null** *(E-PF-52)*: the Filter reports `filter_route = PER_ELEMENT` and container `NOT_USED`; `filter_pass_disabled` shows no red (X13); `NO_MASK` is reserved for a route that expects a mask and has none.
- **If a pipe sheet pixel is both silhouette edge and emission or chevron** *(E-PF-53)*: emission wins (the bleed is multiplied by `1 - emission`); chevron shape, pattern, speed and direction are never touched (PF-24).
- **If the rim band's beam is occluded by a wall 3 tiles away** *(E-PF-54)*: the band stops at the occluder cut and never follows the shadow edge; no violet appears on the far side of the wall at any ladder step (a free wall-hack otherwise); the one-frame pose lag of Lighting's beam is a registration failure, so the Filter reads the same-frame published pose (PF-23, PF-35).
- **If every leak parameter of a material is at its rest value** *(E-PF-55)*: the output is bit-identical to the unmodified material (pass-through exactness, PF-43); a TRUTH_PX node carries the shared material at rest only (PF-6 ii).
- **If `rate_floor` exceeds `GLIDE_RATE_MAX_DEG` (a needle shorter than about 14.3 px) or the truth lies outside the clamp range `[W_M, 1000 - W_M]` (the old negative `room_deg`)** *(E-PF-56)* *(Amended 2026-10-27 — design review)*: the first makes the needle ineligible (E-PF-11); the second clamps the target live (E-PF-10): the lie keeps its gleam, its sound and its wander, and `lie_short_reach` moves.
- **If the slew is pinned at `DISC_CAP_PM` when the lie ends** *(E-PF-64)* *(Amended 2026-10-27 — design review)*: RETURN starts from the pinned value; the follower closes the gap at `rm_ret x GLIDE_RETURN_RATE` (a 400 pm gap is 96 deg, at most 32 s at `rm` 1.0), `amp_end` is 1000 and the series is the 48-deg series of D.5 stretched by the longer tail; the cap applies to the follower as well, so a truth that moves away cannot widen the gap past 400 pm.
- **If `N_r` changes mid-run (a lying readout starts or ends in the region, or the lying-to-honest switch lands at CLEAR)** *(E-PF-65)* *(Amended 2026-10-27 — design review)*: `T_tick` re-evaluates at the next tick and the region gap governor defers any lying tick due less than 334 ms after the previous one to the earliest slot 334 ms later (never queued twice), so no closed second in the region holds more than three Filter transitions across the change; the lying-to-honest switch at CLEAR lands on the next HUD grid boundary and is the HUD arbiter's to admit or skip (AC-PF-75b, AC-PF-75c) *(Amended 2026-10-27 — re-review)*.
- **If a configuration violates a build assertion (`STAGE_RAMP_S` below its floor, `GLIDE_RETURN_RATE` below `GLIDE_RATE_MAX_DEG`, `LEAK_D_FULL_DEG` at or below `LEAK_EPS_DEG`, the never-rest amplitude bound, the flash bound on amplitude falls, D.5/D.6)** *(E-PF-57)*: the boot check fails with the assertion's name (a data error, not a runtime state); a tuning knob can therefore never silently leave its derived safe range.
- **If two fringes are visible at once (the rim band and a crewmate outline)** *(E-PF-58)*: they use different hashed breath sequences (different `(subject or rim, seat)` keys), so they do not beat in phase; neither is locked to `tell_beat`, the alarm sweep or the voice-activity cue (PF-30, D.8).
- **If `CLASSES_ENABLED` clears class (b) mid-session** *(E-PF-59)*: the entries leave `sanity.perception` and every lie takes the D.6 return path (SN-16 INVALIDATED), never a pop.
- **If a client joins mid-session or a seat changes owner (VS-B)** *(E-PF-60)*: the tell and outline envelopes of the joining viewer start at their targets (`hard_start`), because a fade-in from clean would be a free oracle for that viewer; the joining seat's own `sanity.perception` is OWNER_PRIVATE and arrives by the owner's own restore path.
- **If the Debug Overlay is on** *(E-PF-61)*: it reads `true_value` and `perceived` for the same ref and the Filter's counters; the Filter's draw path is unchanged (the overlay is a DIAGNOSTIC subscriber, never an accessibility feature, absent from release builds), and it can show deliberate corruption beside real desync because `state_hash` excludes the perceived channel.

## Dependencies

Status vocabulary: **designed** (a GDD on disk with header Status "Designed"); **in-flight** (a draft on disk, header Status "In Design"); **undesigned-provisional** (no GDD on disk; this GDD states a request and re-checks it when the neighbour lands). Glob of `design/gdd/` at the time of writing: `command-simulation-layer.md`, `pixel-perfect-render-pipeline-camera.md`, `2d-lighting-and-occlusion.md` and `sanity-and-hallucination-rules.md` exist and are designed (as are the other neighbours the Interactions section lists); `adaptive-audio-and-tells.md` exists and is now designed (it was a draft, header `In Design`, when this GDD was written; it honours PF-20 and answers IDX:41) *(Amended 2026-10-27 — design review)*; `chart-table-web-map-and-menus.md` exists (header `In Design` when first globbed, now `Designed`; it names this GDD, cites PF:223 (PF-5 row 9) and CT-2, and demands the rule be repeated); no file existed then for HUD & Suit Layer, In-World Instruments & Inspect View, Debug Overlay (True Values), Accessibility Settings or Co-op Session & Replication (refreshed 2026-10-27: `adaptive-audio-and-tells.md`, `in-world-instruments-and-inspect-view.md`, `hud-and-suit-layer.md` and `debug-overlay-true-values.md` are now designed; Accessibility Settings and Co-op Session & Replication still have no file); no ADR exists (`docs/architecture/` holds only templates).

| System | Direction | Hard or soft | Interface | Status |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| Command & Simulation Layer | the Filter depends on it | **Hard** | `read_view` of SIM and `PERCEPTION_OUT` blocks; `tick_alpha`; the tick count; kernel state (PAUSED, LOADING, RUNNING); subscriber class `PRESENTATION`; `state_hash` exclusion; no hook, block or mutator | designed |
| Pixel-Perfect Render Pipeline & Camera | the Filter depends on it | **Hard** | the world layer, `k`, `k_auto`, pass A/B flag, `present_time`, the container slot assignment, the flash invariants (PR-13), `D_BUDGET` accounting | designed |
| 2D Lighting & Occlusion | the Filter depends on it; Lighting also depends on the Filter's decoy list (VS-A) | **Hard** | mask-bit table, beam-edge mask sprites and beam pose, HullSurface and Emissive include block, the decoy list hand-off, `GLOW_HDR_THRESHOLD`, LO-22's region definition | designed (this run) |
| Sanity & Hallucination Rules | the Filter depends on it | **Hard** | `sanity.perception` (`OWNER_PRIVATE`), `sanity_view(player_id)`, `READING_LIE_STAGE`, `SCANNER_LIE_STAGE`, `lie_targets.json`, `lure_bands.json`, `force_stage`/`force_level` flags | designed (this run) |
| Life-Support Graph | the Filter depends on it | **Hard** (edge **not in the index**; owed) | `true_value(kind, id)`; the consequence-channel closed list (LS-14); the falsifiability table (LS-22) | designed |
| Suit & Equipment | the Filter depends on it | **Hard** (edge **not in the index**; owed) | `true_value(key, player_id)` for five keys; body cues and the chest point left untouched (SE-25, SE-27, SE-29) | designed |
| Player Controller & Input / Ship Body & Interior Physics | the Filter depends on them | Soft (reads only) | per-facing chest point; hand point; true positions (PC-5, PC-11, BDY-30) | designed |
| Horror Entities | the Filter depends on it | Soft (VS) | `horror.view`; the rig presenter and its `leak` input (HE-40) | designed |
| Failure & Trail System | bidirectional | Soft for F&T, **hard** for the Filter | the truth tag; the per-trail-element mask; `D_FT_MAX` | designed |
| Emitter State & Light-Exposure Query | the Filter is bounded by it | Soft | the closed list; EM-29 lint on fringes and decoys | designed |
| Attention System | the Filter is bounded by it | Soft | AT-17: the tell is a truth channel; the anchor list includes the tell cues | designed |
| Interaction System | the Filter is bounded by it | Soft | IA-13, IA-38, AC-IA-54, AC-IA-85 | designed |
| Ship Section & Layout / Modular Ship Assembly / Web Map & Transit / Derelict Authoring (Handmade Kits) / Boarding & Salvage | the Filter is bounded by them | Soft | the truth asks (PF-5 rows 11 to 15) | Layout, Assembly, Web Map, Derelict Authoring, Boarding & Salvage: designed |
| Save/Load & Versioned Format | the Filter is bounded by it | Soft (routed request) | SL-24, SL H4, SL OQ-11, AC-SL-124: the first RUNNING frame is filtered | designed |
| Item & Module Database | the Filter reads data it routes | Soft | the Sanity-owned `lure_bands.json`; defs carry no perceived-state field | designed |
| Chart Table, Web Map UI & Menus | the Filter is bounded by it | Soft (negative requirement) | layer 3 is never wrapped; PF-5 row 9 TRUTH (chart table, menus); CT-2 | designed (in-flight when this GDD was written) |
| **In-World Instruments & Inspect View** | depends on the Filter | **Hard** | `perceived`, tag calibration (PF-45, answered by IW-8 and IW-9), the leak-material parameters | designed (`in-world-instruments-and-inspect-view.md`; PF-45 answered by IW-8, OQ-PF-29) |
| **HUD & Suit Layer** | depends on the Filter | **Hard** | `perceived(SUIT_KEY)`; the frame is fed nothing; the gleam on HUD glass is drawn by the HUD from `leak_amplitude_pm` and `leak_modes` (OQ-PF-11 closed) | designed (`hud-and-suit-layer.md`) |
| **Debug Overlay (True Values)** | depends on the Filter | **Hard** | `perceived` and `true_value` for the same ref; counters and their severities, `t_return_max` per lie and the calibration per ref on the dev channel (PF-41) | designed (`debug-overlay-true-values.md`) |
| **Adaptive Audio & Tells** | depends on the Filter | **Hard** for the lie family (Audio's reading; a lie without its sound mode leaves HR-5 unmet), soft otherwise | `FILTER_AUDIO_CLASSES` ids and the request channel; `perceived().leak_modes` | designed (quotes this GDD's PF-20 and answers IDX:41) |
| **Accessibility Settings** (Alpha) | depends on the Filter | Soft | published scalars (PF-39) | undesigned-provisional |
| **Co-op Session & Replication** (VS-B) | depends on the Filter | Soft | the replicated `SHARED_SUMMARY` per seat | undesigned-provisional |

**Edges the index lacks** (owed to the next index pass, raised by the upstream GDDs): Perception Filter -> Life-Support Graph (hard; LS-19, LS:864), Perception Filter -> Suit & Equipment (hard; SE-29), Perception Filter -> Player Controller / Ship Body (soft reads; PC row 16, BDY-30).

**Bidirectionality check** (each dependency GDD's own statement of the edge): Kernel row 11 and Pipeline row 5 name the Filter; Lighting names it as a Hard downstream with a decoy list; Sanity names it as Hard downstream with "Data in: nothing"; Graph, Suit, F&T, Emitter, Attention, Horror, Controller, Interaction, Layout, Assembly, Web Map, Body, Save/Load and Boarding carry a row or an ASK naming it (quoted in the Interactions tables). Derelict Authoring (Interactions row "Sanity and Perception Filter" and its Dependencies row; ask: never fabricate or erase the residual light, LY OQ-13) and the Item & Module Database (row "Sanity / Perception Filter / In-World Instruments": a def carries no perceived-state field) also name the Filter; both asks are answered by PF-5 rows 13 and 15 and the I-A Item DB row.

**Soft-cycle note.** The index records a soft cycle Sanity -> Filter ("writes only CanvasItem/Material/Light2D properties"); it resolves by one-way ownership: Sanity writes only perception state, the Filter draws it, nothing flows back (SN-23, PF-3). A hard cycle would require the Filter to feed Sanity; it feeds nothing.

## Tuning Knobs

*Reading the table.* Every Filter-owned number below is a `[PLACEHOLDER]` (invented in this document, final nowhere) with a safe range and a proposed registered name; the playtest dials are marked **dial**. **★** marks a registry candidate: a cross-system fact other GDDs or the Debug Overlay will read, to be written by the registry-merge stage from this final text. A name marked **cited** is owned by another GDD and is never re-valued here; its source of truth is named. A name marked **named here** has its value from the art bible but is not a registry constant. **Derived** knobs have a floor that a boot assertion computes (the last table); a value outside the safe range fails the build with the assertion's name (E-PF-57). Accessibility Settings (Alpha, undesigned) may expose only three scalars, all derived from rows below: `reduced_motion_glide_pm` (= `RM_GLIDE_SCALE` x 1000), `breath_depth_pm` (= 1000 - `BREATH_FLOOR_PM`) and `lie_tick_min_ms` (= `LIE_TICK_MS`); no sanity-specific setting may remove a leak (PF-39).

### Filter-owned knobs

| Knob | Default | Unit | Safe range | Too high | Too low | Interacts with |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| **Glide and lie shape** | | | | | | |
| `GLIDE_REACH_FRAC` | 0.6 | fraction of life | 0.4 to 0.8 (at 0.8 the arrival assertion needs `rm_min` 0.89) *(Amended 2026-10-27 — re-review)* | the departure arrives near expiry and the lie is held only briefly | the needle arrives early and sits in a long static hold | D.2 build assertions `rm_min x D_DRAW_MIN_DEG >= D_VIS_MIN_DEG` and `GLIDE_REACH_FRAC / rm_min <= RM_ARRIVE_FRAC_MAX`; `lies_partial` |
| `D_DRAW_MIN_DEG` | 24 | deg | 24 to 40 *(Amended 2026-10-27 — design review)* | most lies hit the 40 %-of-arc cap | lies barely visible | `D_MAX_FRAC_ARC`, `D_VIS_MIN_DEG`; guard `rm_min x D_DRAW_MIN_DEG >= D_VIS_MIN_DEG` |
| `D_MAX_FRAC_ARC` | 0.4 | fraction of arc | 0.25 to 0.5 | a false reading crosses several zones; heavy lies | a FALSE_OK may stay inside the degraded zone (the lie fails to hide) | zone map (Instruments), D.3 |
| `D_VIS_MIN_DEG` | 12 | deg | 8 to 12 *(Amended 2026-10-27 — design review)* | a lie needs a bigger or faster glide to be drawn | a tiny departure; weak motion mode | the D.2 reach assertion and the `rm_min` guard |
| `NEEDLE_LEN_MIN_PX` | 16 | art px | 14.3 to 32 (owner: In-World Instruments, provisional; eligibility is `needle_len_px >= 28.6 / GLIDE_RATE_MAX_DEG` = 14.3, QA C-1) | fewer gauges eligible for a needle glide | below 14.3 the drift cannot reach 0.5 px/s inside 2 deg/s, so the surface is not lied on (E-PF-11) unless the ref has a visible digit mechanism, whose TICK or STALE is then its motion mode *(Amended 2026-10-27 — re-review)* | `rate_floor = max(0.5, 28.6 / L)`; recommended needle 16 to 24 |

### Tuning notes

The three feel dials are `STAGE_RAMP_S` (weather, not event), `GLIDE_RETURN_RATE` (the tail after a paid scan) and `AMP_FLOOR_PM` (how loud a lie is on frame one; it moves with `LEAK_FADE_S`, which it must not outrun, and it is the lever of the P1/P4 tension of OQ-PF-37, ruled 2026-10-27 as the stage-indexed floor 400 / 325 / 250 of D.5, so the dial now has two ends, `AMP_FLOOR_PM` and `AMP_FLOOR_S3_PM`) *(Amended 2026-10-27 — user rulings)*. Playtest each from the middle of its range; none may be tuned until the dial-calibration records of Instruments exist (every angle and pixel figure assumes `arc_deg` 240 and a 24-px needle, both provisional). `WANDER_AMP_PX` and `WANDER_F_MIN` move together (the D.4 assertion); raising one lets the other fall. The accessibility scalars never raise a knob past a safe range: reduced motion lowers `rm` to no less than 0.7 *(Amended 2026-10-27 — re-review)* and raises `LIE_TICK_MS` to at most 600, and never touches the wander.

## Visual/Audio Requirements

*REQUIRED section (Visual/Audio category; art-director consulted, proposal merged 2026-10-26).* Sanity's table SNV-1 to SNV-31 is the first writer of WHAT is seen and heard, for WHOM, and that every lie leaks; this section does not restate those rows, it cites them by id and adds what the Filter owns: pixel geometry at every zoom rung, ramp and breath shapes, the single leak-material family and its variants, the grade of everything the Filter emits, the art-bible refreshes owed, and the art-director rulings routed here. Row ids are `PFV-n`. Scope tags: **MVP-live**, **MVP-conditional** (cut candidates, PF-23 and PF-24), **VS-A**, **VS-B** (designed to full depth, rendered at MVP by stubs only). Grades are the PF-4 grades: **PX** (bit-identical to the simulation's drawing), **ST** (state and shape never moved; colour-only tint allowed), **LEAK** (truth by construction: it is the tell that the lie is a lie), **C** (corruptible module body).

**The Filter emits nothing of grade PX.** PX is a property of what the Filter must leave exactly as the simulation drew it (trails, beams, the true reveal radius, character sprites, the HUD frame). Everything the Filter draws is LEAK, C, or (VS-A) ST. Any asset or shader that claims PX is a defect in this section. The write set, stated positively (PF-2): `CanvasItem` and `Material` properties, particle members and audio requests; no real-light member (LO-25 (ii)), no Camera, Viewport, SubViewport or SubViewportContainer property, no simulation state.

The "Grade" column is the grade of what the Filter EMITS for that event.

### V.2 Motion and animation constraints

| ID | Constraint | Source |
|---|---|---|
| PFV-21 | Exactly three motion signatures exist and only one belongs to a lie. **Settle**: damped spring, ease-out 250 to 400 ms, overshoot at most 5 %, then still. **Tremor**: +-1 art px (+-2 art px at k = 1, so it stays at least 2 screen px; Instruments' `tremor_offset`), irregular, 6 to 10 Hz, around a TRUE value, rests when stress ends. **Glide**: 0.5 to 2 deg/s on a dial arc, micro-wander below 0.5 Hz, NEVER rests, carries the gleam. A lie never uses settle or tremor; an honest gauge never glides. The returned `motion_mode` is the only switch: Instruments apply settle and tremor only when it is HONEST. The leak's own breath (PFV-18) is a modulation of the gleam, not a fourth gait, and is the motion mode of the static-pair classes (PF-15 table); a tremor already running at a lie's onset blends out through `tremor_weight_pm` instead of stopping dead *(Amended 2026-10-27 — design review)*. | ART 7.3; SNV-23; PF-17 |
| PFV-22 | A lie never snaps (onset, hold or return). A snap is an unsignalled uncaused change and borrows neither signature. The only hard cuts the Filter produces are frame swaps of two honest state frames (PFV-9) and the class-(c) body substitution (PFV-13); both are the native cut vocabulary of an HD-pixel-art game and both carry a leak. | SNV-7, SNV-11, SNV-6 |
| PFV-23 | Stage onset and offset are amplitude-monotone over `STAGE_RAMP_S` 8 s `[PLACEHOLDER]` (6.5 to 20), never a pulse, never a step, never an overshoot. A ramp longer than one breath guarantees the onset cannot be read as the first breath. | PF-21; SNV-27; D.7 |
| PFV-24 | Tick interval at least `LIE_TICK_MS` (floor 334 ms; 1000 / 333 = 3.003 transitions per second would exceed `FLASH_MAX_PER_S` 3). Several lying readouts inside one region form a combined flash budget with a shared scheduler (D.9, V.5). | SNV-8, SNV-24; PF-18; AC-SN-149 |
| PFV-25 | No camera motion, zoom, rotation or offset from any lie or stage (camera framing is a never-shift anchor, PF-36). No HUD shake, blur, chromatic aberration, scale change or luminance change above 3 Hz (HR-13). A needle moves inside a STILL frame; that stillness makes the drift readable. | PIPE C1, C9; ART 7.3; SNV-31 |
| PFV-26 | Phantom and class-(c) rigs use ART 5.4 construction: segment rigs, stepped at a fixed low rate (12 fps, every 5th tick at 60 Hz, 15 deg quantisation, ART 8.10 "Horror rig rotation"). The Filter does not animate a real horror; for a phantom or a class-(c) substitution it supplies `(pose_stream, type_id, leak)` to the presenter (HE-40). | SNV-28; HE-40; PF-27, PF-29 |
| PFV-27 | A lie that is on screen is drawn whenever its surface is lit; a lie running while the player is elsewhere reappears at the correct point of its trajectory (a pure function of the run count since activation). No special case for "seat away". | PF-34; OQ-SN-17 |

### V.3 Palette and semantic colour rules the Filter must obey

| Rule | Value or constraint | Source |
|---|---|---|
| PFV-28 Violet colour | Every leak uses the FRINGE violet `#B07BE6` (hue 270 to 330 deg, saturation above 25 %). Never the core violet `#8A3FD1` (about 2.9:1 on Ground, fails; the fringe is about 5.3:1). | ART 4.4; HR-5 |
| PFV-29 Violet placement | At an EDGE only: glass edge, needle leading edge, cone rim, pipe silhouette edge, sprite contour. Never a fill, plane, wash or full-sprite tint. | ART 4.4; PF-16 |
| PFV-30 Flesh | `#D9A58C` / `#B5705C`, stage 3 only, only on surfaces (grade ST, colour-only) and on phantom and lure content (VS-A). The MVP ships no flesh surface tint. Never on UI, on a real thing's sprite, or over a trail. | ART 4.4; PF-4, PF-22 |
| PFV-31 Drift order | Violet before flesh. No flesh at stage 1 or 2. | ART 4.4 |
| PFV-32 Warm shift bound (VS-A) | Any apparent-temperature warm shift holds hue at or above 36 deg on sampled Hull Grey and lit Graphite; never pulled toward orange, never made by desaturating. Applies only to non-anchor content and never over beam pixels `#FFE2A8`. | ART 4.4; LO-25 CD-2; AC-LO-36 |
| PFV-33 Luminance bound | Every Filter-emitted pixel stays at or below `LEAK_MAX_CH` 0.85 per channel and 0.9 x `GLOW_HDR_THRESHOLD` (1.3, so at most 1.17) in the world pass: leak colours are authored with max channel at or below 1.0 and no emissive multiplier above 1.0. A leak must not bloom: glow is processed before tonemapping since 4.6 (`breaking-changes.md`), and a blooming fringe would read as a light source (Principle 3). | LO-14; PF-16 |
| PFV-34 No Alarm Red, no "safe" colour | A lie, a debunk and a recovery never use Alarm Red, the warning mark, the alarm sweep or a green or blue all-clear. A lie neither shows nor suppresses the warning mark. | ART 4.1, 4.2; SN-15 L-A; LS-13 |
| PFV-35 Loop hue kept | A lying gauge or sprite keeps the loop's own hue and fitting silhouette: the lie is wrong in VALUE, not in VOCABULARY. The stage-3 bleed adds violet at the edge and never replaces the loop hue. | ART 4.2, 3.4 |
| PFV-36 No global colour operation | No tint, vignette, desaturation, grade, remap, daltonization, blur, aberration, pixelation, scan-line or glitch at any stage. Colour-vision support is the tri-modal redundancy. | PIPE PR-10; HR-10; PF-38 |
| PFV-37 Deeper rings | A deep ring never leaks violet to a sane player (iridescence excludes 270 to 330 deg), so violet always means the player's own mind. The Filter is never the reason a real object shows violet to another viewer, except the VS-B outline fringe of a `tell_on` subject. | ART 4.1, 4.3 |

#### V.3.1 Luminance-ladder collisions and what carries each with hue removed (ART 4.5 addition owed)

| Colour | L* (approx.) | Collides with | Carried by (hue removed) |
|---|---|---|---|
| Violet fringe `#B07BE6` | about 61 | Hull Grey (about 61), Fuel Ember (about 61) | Edge placement (a tangent band at the cone rim, a glass-edge gleam, a 2-px outline) against Hull Grey's raised seam or rib and Fuel's flanged pipe; above all the BREATHING (4 to 6 s) and the glide, which neither colour has |
| Violet core `#8A3FD1` | about 43 | Alarm Red (about 47), lit Graphite (about 43) | not used for leaks |
| Flesh light `#D9A58C` | about 72 | Chemlight `#86B896` (about 70) | flesh has no cone and no source sprite; Chemlight is an omnidirectional pool with a source sprite and is untouched by sanity |
| Flesh deep `#B5705C` | about 54 | between Hull Grey and Alarm Red | surface or phantom placement, never a sweep |

Hue-removed tests (binding for acceptance, AC-PF-29a MVP-live, 29b the rim, 29c the crewmate *(Amended 2026-10-27 — design review)*): with hue removed, a tester finds (a) the lying instrument by its glide and never-rest, (b) the lying rim by the breathing tangent band, (c) a below-threshold crewmate by the breathing outline plus the P1 proximity cue (HR-5, HR-7, AC-SN-154). Each leak mode must also be sufficient with audio removed.

The full union table (PF-5) belongs to Detailed Rules; this table is only what the Filter EMITS. The default for anything else under layer 0 is TRUTH (PF-4).

| ID | Visual emitted | Grade | Notes |

| PFV-46 | Inspect card reading | C | same instrument as the wall gauge (HR-14); the card frame is an anchor |

| PFV-52 | Suit readings through `perceived(SUIT_KEY)` | C, honest in the MVP | body cues, chest point and overlay visibility untouched (SE "must repeat") |

**Never emitted, never touched** (named so no asset claims otherwise): trail VFX (mist, drip, frost, scorch, debris, puddle, hairline); `acoustic` classes at descriptor anchors; Suit body cues; lamp brightness (`served_pm`), the floor pool and `lux`; real light reach, edge, colour and shadow; the `AIRFLOW` draught and `SEAL_STRAIN`; Attention outputs (`web_strand_*`, `vfx_tell_*`, band slide, filaments, tell audio); real-horror marks and footprints; the crewmate beam, character sprites and hand tools; camera framing; the HUD frame and every menu, pause, settings and chat surface; walls, door leaves, seams, sockets, cap plate and bead; seam-lane PIPE and DUCT valve and CABLE breaker sprites (until Graph ASK G8 and F&T ASK FT1 publish a witness; the wrapped-sprite registry must be empty of their ids, AC-MSA-126); pipe emission, chevrons, pattern, diameter and fitting silhouette (PF-5 row 17).

### V.5 Flash and bound statement (art-director reading of PF-32)

Both bounds are always cited as a pair: PR-13 (`FLASH_MAX_PER_S` 3 per rolling 1 s, per light, per region of a ninth of the displayed picture at the current field, and on the frame mean; a flash is a pair of opposing luminance changes of at least 10 %) AND HR-13 (no HUD element changes luminance faster than 3 Hz).

- **Breath**: a period of 4 to 6 s is 0.17 to 0.25 Hz; a 600 pm swing over half a period of 2 to 3 s is at most 0.3 per second. Even if a region were wholly the fringe, that is far below 3 per second.
- **Stage ramp**: monotone over at least 6.5 s and containing no reversal, hence no flash pair.
- **Gleam**: the amplitude follows the discrepancy, which changes at the glide rate; it is monotone except at the micro-wander (below 0.5 Hz). A static-pair gleam breathes like the fringe (period 4 to 6 s, swing 0.6, so at most 0.47 per second, D.11) *(Amended 2026-10-27 — design review)*.
- **Digit tick**: each tick is a small-area luminance change; at the 334 ms floor a single lying readout is at 2.99 changes per second, at the bound. Several lying readouts in one region (`LIES_MAX` 4 at stage 3) could exceed it. Requirement (D.9): lying-readout tick phases are staggered so that the SUM of transitions in any rolling 1 s, within any region and within the HUD, is at most 3; a tick is a digit-glyph substitution, never an inversion of a plate.
- The Filter's own ramps do not enter Lighting's governor; the Filter self-governs by construction and is verified by a 60 s flash scan at stage 3 at the LARGEST field (1x zoom on 2160p).

### V.6 Pixel geometry (worked examples)

#### V.6.1 Fringe widths at every ladder rung

All fringes, gleams and bands are defined in art px of the 640x360 world grid; on screen they are `w x k` px at integer zoom `k`. `k_auto = floor(min(W/640, H/360))`; the ladder is `k` in [1, `k_auto` + 2].

| Display | `k_auto` | Ladder | 2 art px (outline) at k | gleam (`GLEAM_EDGE_PX` 2 art px at k 1, else 1) at k *(Amended 2026-10-27 — re-review)* | `RIM_FRINGE_PX` 2 at k |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| 1920x1080 | 3 | 1 to 5 | 2 / 4 / 6 / 8 / 10 px | 2 / 2 / 3 / 4 / 5 px | 2 / 4 / 6 / 8 / 10 px |
| 3840x2160 | 6 | 1 to 8 | 2 / 4 / ... / 16 px | 2 / 2 / 3 / ... / 8 px | 2 to 16 px |

Worked example: at 1080p, rung k = 5 (two rungs zoomed in), the outline fringe is exactly 10 screen px with no sub-art-pixel softness; at k = 1 (zoomed out to a field nine times the default area) it is 2 screen px. The default rung is k = 3 (6 screen px). Checks at every rung: the width is an exact integer multiple of `k`; the band grid aligns to the art-pixel grid; no feathering. Under render pass B the leak material takes `k` as a uniform (pass-agnostic spec, PF-31).

#### V.6.2 Rules

- The leak is drawn inside the world render at world resolution, never as a post-process on the upscaled container (SNV-29, PF-25). Line-drawn marks lose their antialias feather in 4.7, so any mark that is a drawn line must be widened to a whole art pixel or authored as a sprite or mask (`breaking-changes.md`, 4.6 to 4.7).
- Character and horror cells keep transparent clearance outside the contour for the 2-px outline. ART 8.10 "Fringe" asks for a 4-px cell margin as the IDEAL while its engine constraint and Sanity say at least 2 px; this GDD adopts 2 px as the hard minimum and 4 px as the target, and the art-director owns the reconciliation in ART 8.2 (art-bible refresh 8).
- The outline fringe is `show_behind_parent`-class: it never covers a sprite pixel, including the contour.

#### V.6.3 Needle legibility rule (the most important pixel finding)

The bible requires the lying needle to be "always a pixel from where it was a second ago" (ART 7.3, HR-8). In an integer-pixel needle that holds by rotation only if the tip moves at least 1 art px per second: tip speed `v = L x omega x pi / 180`, with `L` the pivot-to-tip length in art px and `omega` in deg/s.

| Needle length L (art px) | Tip speed at 0.5 deg/s | at 1 deg/s | at 2 deg/s | Slowest omega giving 0.5 px/s | Slowest omega giving 1 px/s |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| 12 | 0.10 px/s | 0.21 | 0.42 | 2.4 deg/s (out of range) | 4.8 deg/s (out of range) |
| 16 | 0.14 | 0.28 | 0.56 | 1.8 deg/s | 3.6 deg/s (out of range) |
| 24 | 0.21 | 0.42 | 0.84 | 1.2 deg/s | 2.4 deg/s (out of range) |
| 32 | 0.28 | 0.56 | 1.12 | 0.9 deg/s | 1.8 deg/s |
| 57 | 0.50 | 1.00 | 1.99 | 0.5 deg/s | 1.0 deg/s |

For L = 16 at 1 deg/s, `16 x 0.01745 = 0.28 px/s` (one pixel step about every 3.6 s). A glide inside the bible's 0.5 to 2 deg/s therefore moves a small in-world needle one pixel only every 1 to 10 s, and "never rests" is not literally satisfiable by rotation alone (CONFLICT C-1 of X-4; PF-17). Requirements: (1) the per-lie glide rate is chosen so tip speed is at least 0.5 art px/s where the range allows (`omega_min = max(0.5, 28.6 / L)` deg/s; 1.19 at L = 24); a needle under 14.3 px is not lied on (E-PF-11), unless the same ref has a visible digit mechanism whose TICK or STALE (with the gleam breath) is then its motion mode (SN-15 L-L) *(Amended 2026-10-27 — re-review)*. (2) Lie-capable in-world gauges ship a needle of at least 16 art px (recommend 24). (3) The never-rest mechanism is the micro-wander (0.40 to 0.45 Hz, amplitude `WANDER_AMP_PX` 2.2 at the tip *(Amended 2026-10-27 — design review)*, so a pixel flips at least every `NEVER_REST_MAX_S` 1.0 s at every needle angle, D.4) plus the gleam's slow modulation, not net rotation alone. (4) The ART 7.3 and HR-8 sentence is restated as the micro-wander requirement (art-bible refresh 6).

#### V.6.4 Own-cone rim occlusion rule

The four beam-edge masks (ART 8.10) are fixed shapes. Drawn at the nominal cone rim they would continue through a wall the beam does not pass, painting violet in the dark beyond the occluder and revealing reach (a free wall-hack and a false extension). Requirement: the rim band is drawn only along rim segments that lie in lit, unoccluded space (the end arc and the side edges up to the first occluder); where the beam is cut by an occluder the band stops at the cut and never follows the shadow edge. How the clip is realised is a technical-artist and lighting-spike item `[UNV]` (ADR candidate 8). Verification sample: a beam facing a wall 3 tiles away shows no violet on the far side of the wall at any rung (AC-PF-61).

### V.7 Audio

As art-director the mood and the existence of the channel are stated here; content, mix, ducking, bus and triggers are Adaptive Audio's, and the id namespace `FILTER_AUDIO_CLASSES` with the request semantics is PF-20's (R5).

- **Mood: doubt, not horror** (ART 2.9): wrong, warm where it should not be, fringed, over-reaching. No heartbeat, no tempo change, no sting at a stage boundary or a debunk.
- **The sound mode is one leak channel of HR-5**: it exists for every lying reading and is requested no later than the first visible frame (D.15: the request is ON on the first ACTIVE frame, ahead of `t_glide_visible` 0.125 to 0.83 s; the audible start is Audio's, measured under AC-AA-6) *(Amended 2026-10-27 — re-review)*. Each mode (gleam, glide, sound) is sufficient alone, which is why a hue-removed AND an audio-removed test must each still find the liar. Adaptive Audio & Tells (designed, quotes PF-20) states that "audio can lie only through the hallucination family the Filter requests" and that every member of that family carries an audible leak: the Filter acknowledges that constraint, and no `FILTER_AUDIO_CLASSES` member may be specified without an audible leak (AC-PF-98).
- **Level and breath** (PF-20, D.15) *(Amended 2026-10-27 — re-review, delegated ruling R8)*: the request is the raw per-lie `amp_snd` (0 to 1000 per mille); the dB mapping, the ceiling under the witness nominal, the floor and any combined cap are Audio's; the ON carries the one breath envelope `(T_s, floor)`, so the sound breathes once and is never a constant tone.
- **Ids** (PF-20): `FA_LIE_SIG_GAUGE` and `FA_LIE_SIG_SPRITE` at MVP; `FA_PHANTOM_MOVE` and `FA_PHANTOM_PRESENCE` at VS-A. Lint-disjoint from the Graph witness `acoustic` classes, Attention's tell family and Horror's sub-tell.
- **Silent by requirement**: the outline fringe (ART 5.5), a stage transition, a recovery, a debunk, an expiry (beyond the ending lie's own LEVEL and OFF request).
- **Never altered, faked or added**: consequence-channel sounds (`acoustic` classes at descriptor anchors), the human voice and chat, the P1 proximity cue (physically real, plays on every client), tell audio. No phantom sound reuses a witness class, a tell sound or Horror's sub-tell.
- Phantom-movement audio (VS-A) is positional and per viewer. The art constraint is only that it cannot be louder than the real thing it imitates and that a phantom is never heard without being seeable when lit (no audio-only phantom in the MVP). Positional playback mechanics (the `area_mask` default changed from 1 to 0 in 4.7, `breaking-changes.md`) are Adaptive Audio's.

### V.8 Art-director rulings carried in this section

- **A-1. SPRITE_SWAP return (OQ-PF-3).** The bible has no dissolve or crossfade vocabulary and HD pixel art forbids blended in-between sprites; a half-cracked housing is a machine state that does not exist; a frame cut is the native vocabulary of machine state change, but an UNCAUSED cut with no leak is exactly an unleaked lie. Ruling: two mechanisms by lie type, one rule. (1) Continuous-variable frames (rotor cadence on a stopped machine): the cadence returns on the glide path to the honest cadence and the frame cut occurs at zero discrepancy, so the return is invisible (D.17). (2) Static-pair frames (housing cracked or whole, lit or unlit pair): cut back to the honest frame, and the gleam decays on the glide-return schedule (D.6) so the leak outlives the false frame, never the reverse. A false frame is never shown with the gleam already gone. Identical for EXPIRED, INVALIDATED and DEBUNKED; onset is the mirror image (the gleam rises first over `SWAP_LEAD_S` 3 s, then the cut). Trade-off accepted: the afterglow after a cut tells the player "something just changed back" (a mild oracle that a lie ended), identical for all end types so it never says why, and preferred to an unleaked false frame. Needs creative-director confirmation (OQ-PF-3).
- **A-2. Victim tell rendered always, including to a `tell_on` viewer.** ART 5.5's "A player never sees their own fringe" is read as "never on their own body", not "never as a viewer" (PF-28).
- **A-3. One shader source for all leaks.** Confirmed provisionally (PF-43): the five-source ceiling (LO-13) is full, so every leak is a variant of one parametrised material; reopens if the LO-13 ratification fails (OQ-PF-5).
- **A-4. Own-cone rim fringe stays**, MVP-conditional, **lie-bound by default (user ruling X11, OQ-PF-1)** *(Amended 2026-10-27 — user rulings)*: the diegetic self-sign at stage 2 and the "look at edges" lesson, drawn only while a stage-2 reach lie is ACTIVE; the AMBIENT (standing) mode would behave as a de facto stage indicator (tension with IA-39) and is a playtest-gated opt-in.
- **A-5. Hue bleed ships cuttable and default-off** (OQ-PF-2, user ruling X11/D76) *(Amended 2026-10-27 — user rulings)*: build the instance-uniform edge channel only if `pipe_shared_msk` is authored anyway; `AMBIENT_FRINGES_ENABLED` is false at the shipped default.
- **A-6. Fringe luminance floor**: `BREATH_FLOOR_PM` 400 beside `DECOY_LEAK_ALPHA_PM` 300 so every violet edge in the game shares one visibility floor (the one deliberate exception is the stage-3 gleam floor of 250, user ruling D74, routed to Lighting and the art-director) *(Amended 2026-10-27 — user rulings)*. Cut order if the Filter slips: hue bleed, then the own-cone rim fringe, then SPRITE_SWAP return polish; never below two leak modes, never the tag lints.
