The stage-3 pipe-edge bleed row is a default-off opt-in (D76). Section 4.5 luminance ladder and the visibility-floor note: the gleam floor falls 400, 325, 250 per mille with stage (D74).

### V.10 Risks and art-director flags

- **Needle legibility**: CONFLICT C-1 (X-4); a wander amplitude below 1 art px is invisible.
- **Rim fringe through occluders**: an unclipped band paints violet beyond walls (V.6.4); `[UNV]` spike.
- **Gleam unit seam** (CONFLICT C-3, OQ-PF-11): *closed 2026-10-27 from the HUD side* (OQ-HS-16): the HUD draws the gleam on its own glass (HUD px, stroke at least 2 px, HR-12) from `leak_amplitude_pm` and `leak_modes`; the Filter owns no HUD node.
- **Digit-tick flash sum**: the floor equals the bound for one readout; staggering and a per-region cap are required (V.5).
- **State-token and category gaps in ART 8.5** block `/asset-spec` names; the names below are provisional (OQ-SN-28).
- **Violet L* 61 collides with Hull Grey and Fuel Ember in achromatopsia**: carried by edge placement and breathing only; the hue-removed test is mandatory at every rung, because a 2-px band at k = 1 on a Steam Deck is 2 screen px and may not resolve (OQ-PF-4, PIPE OQ 4).
- **Shader ceiling 5/5** (LO-13) leaves no slack; leak variants must stay configurations.
- **Default TRUTH means a forgotten corruptible node never lies** (the lie vanishes, the truth stays intact): the coverage lint against `lie_targets.json` (AC-PF-6) is the guard.
- **Engine unknowns, all `[UNV]` and not the basis of any number here**: per-instance shader uniforms and their caps; `LIGHT_MODE_UNSHADED` batching with leak overlays; per-element alpha-edge sampling at the rim; occluder-aware clipping of the rim band; container `TEXTURE` access. The engine reference has no `scripting.md` or `core.md`, so GDScript integer semantics (the presentation hash) are `[UNV]`; the hash masks every step explicitly (D.14).

ART 8.5 has no shader category. Proposed token `shd` (shader or material resource); the names are provisional and the 8.5 addition is owed by the art-director (OQ-SN-28).

Counts as the fringe among the five `ShaderMaterial` sources of LO-13. |

"Cracked" is a `mach_*` state frame, never a `trail_*` hairline. |
| Lamp-housing glow frame pair | `mach_lamp_<name>_lit` / `_unlit` | In-World Instruments | Appearance only; must not read as a lantern pool. |
| Pipe silhouette-edge channel | `pipe_shared_msk.png` edge channel | environment art | Needed for the stage-3 bleed (PFV-12); cut with it if absent. |
| Character and horror cell clearance | all `char_*` and `horror_*` cells | character / horror art | At least 2 px transparent outside the contour (target 4 px); horror cells included because class (c) reuses them. |
| Horror rig presenter inputs | `horror_t0x` segment rigs (reuse) | Horror art | `leak` is the only input the Filter sets on a real-horror-derived rig; the velocity driver for class (c); no new sheets; the flesh tint is a shader, not baked. |
| Junk-sprite outline slice (VS-A lure) | `_msk` outline slice on junk defs | Boarding / Item DB art | No `ovl_` asset for sanity (ART 5.3). |
| Dust-in-beam particles | existing (ART 5.5, Attention's Tell) | F&T / VFX | Sanity depends on them as a falsifier; not Filter-owned. |
| Audio assets | not specified here | Adaptive Audio & Tells | Ids in `FILTER_AUDIO_CLASSES`; disjoint from witness, tell and sub-tell classes. |

No `ovl_` asset, no screen-space sprite, no HUD sprite and no sanity icon is implied by this system. `vfx_tell_*` and `web_strand_*` are Attention's reserved family; no Filter asset may carry either prefix (AC-PF-31).

📌 **Asset Spec** — Visual/Audio requirements are defined. After the art bible is approved, run `/asset-spec system:perception-filter` to produce per-asset visual descriptions, dimensions and generation prompts from this section.

## Game Feel

*REQUIRED section (Game Feel category).* The Filter takes no player input and owns no verb, so "feel" here is the kinesthetic quality of **looking**: how a wrong reading arrives, how long it takes to be noticed, how it leaves, and how it never announces itself. The targets below are numbers (ticks, frames, px, ms) so that Instruments, HUD, Audio and QA can build to them; every number the Filter invents is a `[PLACEHOLDER]` with a range in Tuning Knobs and none may be tuned before the dial-calibration records of Instruments exist (every angle and pixel figure assumes `arc_deg` 240 and a 24-px needle, both provisional). Frame counts assume 60 frames per second unless stated.

### Feel Reference

**Target**: the instrument you have trusted for an hour that is, on a second look, a hair off. Borrow the quality of the unreliable-but-diegetic instrument in *Alien: Isolation* (the motion tracker is a tool the player leans on and the game then makes untrustworthy by degrees, without ever telling them) and the dread of *Beyond the Aquila Rift* and *Pandorum* (the room is subtly not what it was). The lie is **weather, not event**: a stage change arrives over 8 s like a change in the air, a lie departs from the true value at a pace the eye can follow but does not demand, and a lie ends the way it began, quietly.

**Anti-reference**: the sanity effects of *Amnesia: The Dark Descent* (screen warp, vignette, blur, heartbeat, a meter filling) and every "glitch" overlay. Those are announcements; this system forbids every one of them (PF-38, PFV-36). The Filter must also not feel like a scripted jump-scare delivery: nothing it draws is a sting.

### Input Responsiveness

The Filter has no input of its own. What it has is **trigger-to-pixel latency** for the events the player or the simulation causes. The budgets are maxima; the chain is: Sanity run (every `SANITY_DRIFT_DIVISOR` 12 ticks, 200 ms at `TICK_RATE` 60) writes `sanity.perception` -> the notification handler only marks the surface dirty (PF-35) -> the render callback, after the Avatar Presenter and before Lighting reads the decoy list in the same frame, calls `perceived` and writes materials -> pixels.

| Trigger | Max trigger-to-pixel latency | Frame budget (60 fps) | Notes |
|---|---|---|---|
| A lie becomes ACTIVE, an entry is added or removed, a stage steps (SIM side) | `L_max_ms` = 200 ms + one frame (216.7 ms at 60 Hz, 206.9 at 144 Hz, 233.3 at 30 fps; D.16) | 13 frames | Accepted (PF-35); applies to stage steps and entry appearance and removal only; lies, ramps and breaths then evolve on their own clocks |
| First ACTIVE frame of a lie: gleam at `floor_eff` (400 at stages 0 to 1, 250 at stage 3) and the sound request *(Amended 2026-10-27 — user rulings)* | 0 frames after the frame the entry is first seen | 0 | The leak is born with the lie (SN-15 L-L, SNV-13); the sound request is ON on the first ACTIVE frame (D.15) |
| Audible start of the lie's sound on the Audio side | `SOUND_LEAD_MAX_MS` 100 ms after the first ACTIVE frame | 6 frames | Adaptive Audio's mix; HR-5 needs the sound by the glide (`t_glide_visible` 0.125 to 0.83 s) |
| Zoom ladder step (player input, Pipeline) | one frame; the new rung's gleam, rim and outline widths are `n x k'` on the first frame of the rung | 1 frame | A cut (PIPE C6); no interpolated width on any frame; `displayed_pm` unchanged |
| Pause and resume | 1 frame to freeze; resume continues from the frozen phase | 1 frame | A paused frame contains every active leak (PFV-15) |
| Load or `force_stage` / `force_level` | the first RUNNING frame is already filtered | 0 frames of clean picture | No fade-in from clean (PF-33) |
| Accessibility toggle (reduced motion) mid-lie | takes effect on the next lie; the in-flight lie keeps its latched rate | next lie | `rm` is latched at activation and at `hard_start` (E-PF-43) |
| Honest gauge (no entry) | no added latency; `perceived` returns the true reading in the same frame | 0 | The cheap honest path (PF-7); an honest gauge never lags its settle |

### Animation Feel Targets

For a presentation system "startup / active / recovery" map to **onset / hold / return**. Frames at 60 fps.

| Animation | Startup (onset) | Active (hold) | Recovery (return) | Feel goal | Notes |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| Needle lie (GLIDE) | departure visible after `t_glide_visible` = `LEAK_EPS_DEG` / rate = 0.125 to 0.83 s (8 to 50 frames); gleam at `floor_eff` (400 at stage 1) from frame 0 *(Amended 2026-10-27 — user rulings)* | 40 to 120 s (`LIE_LIFE_S`) at the false region with wander `WANDER_AMP_PX` 2.2 at 0.40 to 0.45 Hz *(Amended 2026-10-27 — design review)*; a pixel flips at least every `NEVER_REST_MAX_S` 1.0 s (60 frames) | `disc_end / GLIDE_RETURN_RATE` (3.0 deg/s): 48 deg returns in 16 s (960 frames); gleam to 0 in about 15.9 s | Slow, unhurried, never still; the eye is drawn by the gleam before the motion, and the motion before the thought | D.2, D.3, D.4, D.6; the reach is `GLIDE_REACH_FRAC` 0.6 of the life at most |
| Needle glide pace | tip speed 0.28 px/s at L = 16 and 1 deg/s; 0.84 px/s at L = 24 and 2 deg/s | wander supplies the pixel flip | | A stair of single-pixel steps, never a slide | V.6.3; rate chosen by D.2 so tip speed is at least 0.5 px/s where the range allows |
| Lying digit readout | ticks every `LIE_TICK_MS` 400 ms (24 frames); `N_r` readouts in a region share a staggered scheduler (`T_tick` = 400 / 668 / 1002 / 1336 ms for `N_r` 1 to 4, stagger at least 334 ms) | continuous ticking or a stale hold | the lying numeral keeps stepping on the scheduler to `round(slew_value / lsb)` while the needle returns, and switches to the honest grid at CLEAR, on the next HUD grid boundary; no "tick stops" signature, no tween (PF-G9, PF-18, D.9) *(Amended 2026-10-27 — re-review)* | A mechanical counter that is a little too busy or a little too still | D.9; floor 334 ms (20 frames); at most 3 transitions per rolling second |
| State glyph, housing glow, machine frame (static pair) | the gleam rises first over `SWAP_LEAD_S` 3 s (180 frames), then the frame cuts (1 frame) | held | cut back to the honest frame (1 frame) with the gleam decaying afterwards over `(LEAK_D_FULL_DEG - LEAK_EPS_DEG) / GLIDE_RETURN_RATE` (6.6 s) | A machine state change with a tell on either side of it | PF-19, D.6; a false frame is never shown without its gleam |
| Rotor on a stopped machine (continuous frame) *(Amended 2026-10-27 — re-review)* | the gleam rises first over `SWAP_LEAD_S` 3 s, then the cadence winds up over `ROTOR_WINDUP_S` 3 s to `ROTOR_BASE_FRAC` 0.75 x `F_run`, drifting by `ROTOR_DRIFT_FRAC` 0.15 | held | the cadence winds down at a constant 0.06 `F_run` per s and snaps to stopped at 0.05 `F_run`; the gleam outlives the snap | A bearing that never quite settles | D.17; always at least 5 % off `F_run` |
| Stage ramp (rim, bleed) | `STAGE_RAMP_S` 8 s (480 frames), amplitude-monotone; every transition (the stage-2 target 0.6, the stage-3 step 0.6 to 1.0, any reversal) takes exactly 8 s | steady | the same curve reversed | Weather: noticed in retrospect, never at the moment | D.7; floor derived (3.91 s flash minimum, 6.5 s breath margin) |
| Breath (rim, outline) | n/a | period 4000 to 6000 ms (240 to 360 frames) per cycle, floor `BREATH_FLOOR_PM` 400 | n/a | Slow tide of a fringe that is always faintly present | D.8; never locked to `tell_beat`, the alarm sweep or voice activity |
| Class-(c) substitution (VS-B stub) | 1 frame hard cut on the tick the bit sets | held while `harm_eligible` | 1 frame hard cut on the tick it clears | An instant wrongness with no transition | PF-29; true position, velocity, beam and voice |
| Class-(c) victim outline (VS-B stub) | `TELL_RAMP_S` 8 s | breathing 4 to 6 s | `TELL_RAMP_S` | A subject slowly outlined, then slowly not | PF-28 |

### Impact Moments

The Filter has **no impact moments, by design**: the whole system lives on the sane side of "doubt, not horror" (ART 2.9), and an impact is an announcement. The rows below are the standard punctuation types, each answered, followed by the only two hard cuts the Filter produces.

| Impact type | Duration | Effect description | Configurable? |
|---|---|---|---|
| Hit-stop (freeze frames) | none | Forbidden: nothing the Filter draws freezes the simulation or the picture (PF-36, PF-38) | No |
| Screen shake / camera impact | none | Forbidden: camera position, zoom, rotation and offset are never-shift anchors; no lie produces a jolt (the camera jolt of AC-HE-74 is Horror's and the Pipeline's, not the Filter's) | No |
| Controller rumble | none | The Filter writes no haptic request; consequence rumble belongs to the Graph's consequence channel and is never altered | No |
| Time-scale slowdown | none | Forbidden; PAUSED freezes the Filter's clocks with the simulation (S1) | No |
| Stage boundary, onset, debunk, recovery | none | Nothing at the boundary tick; no verdict flash, no relief cue, no stinger (PFV-1, PFV-4, PFV-6) | No |
| SPRITE_SWAP frame cut | 1 frame | A hard cut between two honest frames, preceded by the gleam's rise (onset) or followed by its decay (return) | `SWAP_LEAD_S` 2 to 6 s |
| Class-(c) body substitution cut (VS-B stub) | 1 frame | The horror rig replaces the crewmate sprite on the tick `harm_eligible` changes; the beam, chest point and voice are untouched | No (SIM-gated) |

### Weight and Responsiveness Profile

- **Weight**: light and quiet. A lie must have no weight at all in the world's physics (no collision, no light, no Attention, no sound that belongs to the consequence channel) and a great deal of weight in the player's trust. Nothing slams; the heaviest thing the Filter does is hold an 8 s ramp.
- **Player control**: the player has no verb against the filter, but retains complete **information control**: every lie is falsifiable by a cheaper, truer channel (a paid scan, a hiss, a draught, a second instrument, a teammate's voice). The feel of control is the feel of looking twice.
- **Snap quality**: smooth and analog for every lie variable (glide, ramp, breath, return), crisp and binary only for the two honest-frame cuts and the class-(c) substitution, each of which carries a leak. Nothing snaps without a cause.
- **Acceleration model**: simulation feel. Every envelope starts from zero and ramps (`STAGE_RAMP_S`, `TELL_RAMP_S`, `LEAK_FADE_S` 1.5 s, the glide's slew limited by `GLIDE_RATE_MAX_DEG` 2.0); nothing starts instantly except the very first ACTIVE frame's gleam floor, which is deliberate (the leak is born with the lie), and `hard_start` after a load.
- **Failure texture**: when the player misses a lie and acts on it, the cost lands through the world, never through the Filter: a true hiss, a real draught, a trail, a real consequence (P1). The Filter never makes a failure feel like a trick: the lie was falsifiable all along, the leak was on screen the whole time, and the reason for the failure is readable ("I believed the gauge and not the pipe"). A debunk is never celebrated; a lie that ends is never announced.

### Playtest dials

The three dials that most change the feel (Tuning notes): `STAGE_RAMP_S` (8 s default, 6.5 to 20), `GLIDE_RETURN_RATE` (3.0 deg/s default, 2.0 to 8.0), `AMP_FLOOR_PM` (400 default, 250 to 600; with `AMP_FLOOR_S3_PM` 250 at stage 3, user ruling D74). Playtest each from the middle of its range; none may move before the Instruments calibration records exist. Secondary: `BREATH_FLOOR_PM` 400, `LIE_TICK_MS` 400, `WANDER_AMP_PX` 2.2 (moves with `WANDER_F_MIN` 0.40) *(Amended 2026-10-27 — design review)*.

### Feel Acceptance Criteria

*(Amended 2026-10-27 — design review)* The nine sentiment checkboxes that stood here ("a tester notices ... within a few minutes", "no playtester describes ... as a filter", "reads as a counter that is too busy") were not measurable and have moved into the playtest exit criteria below, each with a sample size and a threshold; the measured counterparts remain AC-PF-3 to AC-PF-116. The exit criteria are run by the creative-director's playtest plan, not by CI, and a story does not close on them.

| # | Playtest exit criterion | Sample | Pass |
|---|---|---|---|
| PX-1 | A tester not told that a lie is running notices a wrong gauge unprompted, by the glass edge, the glide or the digit, and says which of the gauge and the room to believe | 8 testers, one 20-minute session at stage 1 | at least 6 of 8 within 5 minutes (V1, V2) |
| PX-2 | Unprompted words for the effect: none of "filter", "overlay", "effect", "glitch", "screen" (they talk about the instrument or the room) | the same 8 | 0 of 8 (V3) |
| PX-3 | A stage change is not noticed at the moment it happens | 8 testers, 3 stage changes each | at most 1 of 8 names the moment (V6) |
| PX-4 | No tester reports that a lie "ended" or "was cleared" as an event | the same 8 | 0 of 8 |
| PX-5 | A needle held in a false region is never perceived as "stuck" or frozen; an honest needle never looks like it glides | the same 8, 5 minutes of a held lie | at most 1 of 8 reports "stuck" |
| PX-6 | With the lie's hue removed, and with its audio removed, the liar is still found | the AC-PF-29a protocol | AC-PF-29a |
| PX-7 | The digit tick reads as a counter, never a flicker; no eye strain or flash is reported from a lying readout, a fringe or a ramp | 8 testers, 10 minutes at stage 3 | 0 of 8 report a flash or strain (the automated bound is AC-PF-12, AC-PF-11a) |
| PX-8 | Zooming through every rung leaves the gleam, rim and outline one crisp mark (no soft or doubled edge) and the picture never jumps; the 2-px band at `k` 1 on Steam Deck class hardware is visible | 8 testers; 5 on the Deck-class machine (OQ-PF-4) | 0 of 8 report softness; at least 4 of 5 see the band |
| PX-9 | Loading or resuming a game never shows a clean-then-corrupt flash | 8 testers, 3 loads each | 0 of 8 |
| PX-10 | The lie sound is not muted or turned down within the first 30 minutes (V8) | 8 testers | at most 1 of 8 |

## UI Requirements

**The Perception Filter has no UI of its own and owns no HUD, instrument, card, prompt, menu or settings node.** It is a data and material supplier: it returns what a surface should display for the local peer, and it draws marks on nodes that other systems own. The surfaces below are owned by **In-World Instruments & Inspect View**, **HUD & Suit Layer**, **Debug Overlay (True Values)**, **Accessibility Settings** and **Adaptive Audio & Tells**, all undesigned or in flight at the time of writing; Instruments, HUD, Debug Overlay and Adaptive Audio have since been designed and answer their rows (OQ-PF-29), and the Accessibility Settings row stays a *provisional* request. No sanity meter, bar, ring, number, icon or colour exists on any surface, at any stage (PF-38); no UX Flag is raised because no screen, widget or HUD element is added by this system.

| Information | Display location (owner) | Update frequency | Condition | Source in this GDD |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| The displayed value of a wall gauge needle, digit readout, state glyph, lamp-housing glow or machine frame | The world prop or the inspect card (In-World Instruments) | Every rendered frame, from the memoised `perceived(ref, viewer)` call (one evaluation per `(ref, viewer, frame)`) | Always; the honest path returns the true reading with `leak_amplitude_pm` 0 and `motion_mode` HONEST | PF-7, PF-8, PF-9 |
| The inspect card's reading | The inspect card (In-World Instruments, provisional) | Same call and same ref as the wall gauge | The card lies exactly when its gauge lies, from stage 1 (HR-14, IA-38); the card frame is an anchor and is never touched | PF-18 |
| The leak on a lying reading (gleam, glide, tick or stale-hold, sound) | On the reading's own glass and needle edge (world: Filter material variant on the Instruments node; HUD layer: a HUD-px mark the HUD draws on its own glass from `leak_amplitude_pm` and `leak_modes`) | Every frame | Whenever the surface is on screen and lit | PF-15, PF-16, PF-34; the HUD-px seam is closed (OQ-PF-11) |
| Suit readings (the five keys `suit.o2`, `suit.reserve`, `suit.integrity`, `suit.warmth`, `suit.charge`) and the scanner | HUD & Suit Layer (provisional) | Every frame | The HUD asks `perceived(SUIT_KEY(k), viewer)` and never calls Suit's `true_value`; all keys are honest at MVP (`READING_LIE_STAGE` never); the HUD frame is fed nothing | PF-40 |
| True versus perceived for one ref, with `lie_id`, `lie_kind`, `leak_modes`, `start_run`, `end_run`, `motion_mode` and the Filter counters (`lies_drawn`, `leak_modes_dropped`, `leak_repaired`, `lies_partial`, `lie_short_reach`, `lie_unrenderable`, `lie_collisions`, `remote_queries`, `filter_route`, `filter_pass_disabled`, `first_frame_filtered`, `tag_defaults_applied`, `filter_draws_dropped`, `lies_zone_missed`, `render_cpu_us`) | Debug Overlay (DIAGNOSTIC subscriber; absent from release builds; never an accessibility feature) | On demand, per frame while open | Debug builds only; `filter_route` reads `PER_ELEMENT` with the container `NOT_USED`, never a red `NO_MASK` | PF-25, PF-41 |
| Three accessibility scalars: `reduced_motion_glide_pm` (700 to 1000, *(Amended 2026-10-27 — re-review)*), `breath_depth_pm` (300 to 750), `lie_tick_min_ms` (334 to 1000; reduced motion may raise it to 600) | Accessibility Settings (Alpha; provisional) | On change; read by the Filter at lie activation and at `hard_start` | Each stays within its safe range; no setting removes a leak mode, and no global hue remap or colour pass exists | PF-39 |
| Audio request `(viewer, ref or position, intent, class)` with the class in `FILTER_AUDIO_CLASSES` | Adaptive Audio & Tells (designed) | Re-sent only when the raw level `amp_snd` (0 to 1000 per mille, unit defined by the Filter, converted by Audio) moves by `AUDIO_LEVEL_STEP_PM` 20; ON carries `(LEVEL, T_s, floor)`; OFF once at CLEAR *(Amended 2026-10-27 — re-review, delegated ruling R8)* | A lie whose SOUND mode is present; a muted audio system leaves the lie two modes | PF-20, D.15 |
| The per-viewer decoy list `{decoy_id, kind, anchor, shape, tint_leak, start, end}` | 2D Lighting & Occlusion (decoy overlays on mask bit 3) | Every frame, before Lighting reads it | VS-A; the MVP publishes the contract and an empty-list stub | PF-26 |
| The tell/outline trigger fact for the P1 proximity cue | Adaptive Audio & Tells plays the cue on every client | On `tell_on` set or clear | VS-B; the Filter supplies only the trigger fact and attributes nothing to the web | PF-28 |

**What other UI must never do with Filter data.** It must never show the true value beside a lying reading (outside the Debug Overlay), never show a lie count, a "lies spotted" statistic, a stage name, a verdict, or a sanity-correlated icon, colour or number (PF-38); never apply settle or tremor to a reading whose `motion_mode` is not HONEST; never cache a `Perceived` record (a presentation-local scalar derived from an honest displayed value and cleared on any non-HONEST frame, cull, cut and restore is not a cache; Instruments C14); and never address the Filter by `peer_id` (PlayerId only, PF-10).

**Accessibility notes** (the settings screen itself is Accessibility Settings'): the three leak modes are each sufficient alone (HR-5), so a colour-blind, hue-removed or muted player still has two; reduced motion slows the glide and never removes it or the wander; reduce-flashing is a separate toggle that changes nothing the Filter emits because its ramps are flash-free by construction; text size, contrast and key rebinding are not touched by this system.

## Acceptance Criteria

*Authored from the systems-designer and technical-artist drafts, then reviewed by the qa-lead (2026-10-27); every qa-lead rewrite and gap closure is applied below. Config: `rigor: full`, `workflow: full`, so Logic and Integration evidence is BLOCKING and Visual and UI evidence is BLOCKING and retained in `production/qa/evidence/perception-filter/` (project defaults, `testing.strict` unset).*

**Format.** **AC-PF-n (tag) [scope]**: GIVEN ... WHEN ... THEN ... `[rule or formula ids]`. A lettered id (4a, 4b) is a sub-criterion sharing the id; a failing sub-check names itself.

**Tags.** **(auto)**: an automated test that must pass and runs headless-safe in the CI lane (`godot --headless ... GdUnitCmdTool.gd`); **(render)**: an automated capture test that needs a rendering-capable runner (offscreen or GPU), never `--headless`, reported `SKIPPED-NO-RENDERER` and never PASS in the headless lane, still BLOCKING for its story (OQ-PF-26); **every (render) AC begins with the precondition PRE-R** *(Amended 2026-10-27 — design review)*: the baseline (Filter-off) capture must contain more than one distinct colour and the renderer must report more than 0 draws, otherwise the result is SKIPPED and never PASS (headless runs use the dummy rasterizer, two blank captures compare as bit-identical and the draw count reads 0, so about 17 BLOCKING render ACs would otherwise pass vacuously); a story cannot close on SKIPPED; **(lint)**: a build-time or static check; **(review)**: a named reviewer inspects and signs off; **(manual)**: a tester runs a scripted session and retains evidence; **(spike)**: a time-boxed engine experiment whose result decides a rule stated conditionally.

**Scope markers.** **[MVP-live]** built and tested at MVP; **[MVP-stub]** runs against a stub (the VS-A or VS-B owner builds the real one); **[MVP-conditional]** the criterion belongs to a cut candidate (PF-23 rim fringe, PF-24 hue bleed): if the feature is cut, the criterion becomes its **absence test** (no rim node in any scene, no non-rest `edge_bleed_pm`) and that test is the story's evidence; since 2026-10-27 *(Amended 2026-10-27 — user rulings)* the default branch of the rim criteria is the LIE_BOUND one and the default branch of the bleed criteria is the flag-off absence test, while the AMBIENT and flag-on branches run only when that opt-in is enabled; **[blocked: OQ-n]** cannot be signed off until the open question closes.

**Rules for every test.** Fixtures are constant files or factory functions; no test calls the global RNG; PIPE criteria are cited by the heading "Perception Filter hook" and bullet order (slot/material scan, truth-channel bit-identical, checkerboard, `NO_MASK`, write lint, flash scan), never by line number. **Homes**: Logic `tests/unit/perception_filter/`; Integration and capture `tests/integration/perception_filter/`; Visual and UI evidence `production/qa/evidence/perception-filter/<ac-id>-<k>-<display>.png` with lead sign-off.

**Reviewers named.** art-director (57b, 29a, 44, 74b, 11b, 23 card frame); lead-programmer (80, 51); accessibility-specialist (80, 29a protocol, 41 confirmation).

**Test doubles, scenarios and fixtures** *(Amended 2026-10-27 — design review)*. About twenty ACs run against neighbours that are not built; each uses one of these named doubles (home `tests/doubles/perception_filter/`), never an ad hoc stub.

| Double | Stands in for | Behaviour | Used by |
|---|---|---|---|
| `StubSanityPerception` | Sanity (`sanity.perception`, `sanity_view`, `force_stage`, `force_level`) | feeds scripted entries `{subject_ref, kind, params, start_run, active_run, end_run, leak_modes, first_lie}` and removes them on a script | most Logic ACs |
| `StubGraphTruth`, `StubSuitTruth` | Graph and Suit `true_value` | scripted true-value series: steps, ramps, holds | AC-PF-47, 81, 114, 4b |
| `StubInstruments` | In-World Instruments (needle, card, digit mechanism) | calls `perceived`, applies settle and tremor only when HONEST, honours `tremor_weight_pm`, owns a calibration record per fixture | AC-PF-23, 85, 101, 109, 46 |
| `StubHud` | HUD & Suit Layer | asks `SUIT_KEY` refs, draws the gleam in HUD px from `leak_amplitude_pm` | AC-PF-39, 57a, 96 |
| `StubAudio` | Adaptive Audio & Tells | records every request `(viewer, ref or position, intent, class, level, envelope)` and plays nothing | AC-PF-20, 98, 103, 115 |
| `StubOverlay` | Debug Overlay | reads `true_value` and `perceived` for one ref and the counters | AC-PF-38, 40 |
| `StubLightingPose` | Lighting beam pose and edge masks | publishes a beam pose per frame | AC-PF-61a |
| `StubAccessibility` | Accessibility Settings | exposes the three scalars | AC-PF-80, 107 |
| `StubCoop`, `StubHorrorRig` | Co-op Session; Horror presenter | `SHARED_SUMMARY` per seat; records `(pose_stream, type_id, leak)` | AC-PF-33 to 35, 65, 105 |

Committed scenario files (JSON, `tests/fixtures/perception_filter/scenarios/`): **SCN-PF-1** `stage3_four_lies_10min.json` (stage 3, four lies, rim on, a card open, a stage step at 5:00, a save and restore at 7:30, a breach at 9:00; used by AC-PF-5b, 43b); **SCN-PF-2** `all_variants_first_use_10min.json` (enters stage 3 and triggers every leak variant; AC-PF-77); **SCN-PF-3** `each_stage_10min.json` (stages 0 to 3, 150 s each; AC-PF-18). Constant fixtures (same directory): `hash_vectors.json` (every D.14 vector), `hash_avalanche_pairs.json` (the AC-PF-72 pairs, generated once by a documented fixed-seed script and checked in), `calibration_fixtures.json` (`arc_deg` 240, `needle_len_px` 24, OK and alarm zone intervals, `lsb`, `value_lo`, `value_hi`), `lie_targets_fixture.json`, `stage3_fixture.save`. A render capture pins a frozen tick, `stage3_fixture.save` and fixed-step frames.

### A. Query, record and scope (Logic; Home `test_query_*.gd`)

- **AC-PF-1 (auto) [MVP-live]** *(Amended 2026-10-27 — design review)*: GIVEN no `sanity.perception` entry for a ref WHEN `perceived(ref, viewer)` is called 10 000 times THEN every call returns `displayed_pm` equal to the true reading, `displayed_value` equal to `true_value`, `leak_amplitude_pm` 0, `motion_mode` HONEST, `tremor_weight_pm` 1000, `lie_id` 0, `frame_override` NONE, and every call returns the **same preallocated slot object** (an identity check) while the Filter's own counter `record_constructions` stays 0; no `Performance.OBJECT_COUNT` or `MEMORY_STATIC` claim is made (allocation accounting is `[UNV]`). `[PF-7, PF-8]`
- **AC-PF-2 (auto) [MVP-live]**: GIVEN an ACTIVE local lie WHEN `perceived(ref, P_remote)` is called with a PlayerId that is not the local seat THEN the honest record returns, `remote_queries` increments and no error is raised; addressing is by PlayerId, never `peer_id`. `[PF-10, E-PF-32]`
- **AC-PF-3 (auto) [MVP-live]**: GIVEN one ACTIVE entry and a fixed true value WHEN the history-free parts (`lie_tau`, `target_pm`, `amp`, `leak_modes`) are sampled at 10 chosen `(T_c, tick_alpha)` pairs THEN they are identical at `present_time` rates of 30, 60 and 144 fps; AND WHEN `displayed_pm` is sampled at identical `(T_c, tick_alpha, present_time)` after `hard_start` THEN it is identical across the three rates. The path-dependent slew is tested only in AC-PF-47 and AC-PF-86. `[PF-11, D.1, D.3]`
- **AC-PF-4a (auto) [MVP-live]** *(Amended 2026-10-27 — re-review)*: GIVEN an ACTIVE lie and an unchanged true value WHEN the game is saved at `lie_tau` 33 s and restored THEN the first RUNNING frame's `slew_pm` (wander excluded) equals the pre-save `slew_pm` within 1 pm, and each `displayed_pm` stays within `W_M` (21.9 pm at defaults) of its own `slew_pm` (the wander phase and the breath phase are free to differ, so `displayed_pm` itself is not compared), using the entry's published activation run. `[PF-11, PF-33, D.3, E-PF-21, E-PF-23]`
- **AC-PF-4b (auto) [MVP-live]** *(Amended 2026-10-27 — design review)*: GIVEN an ACTIVE lie with a slew gap of 150 pm still closing (a true step of 150 pm at `lie_tau` 20 s) WHEN the game is saved 2 s later and restored THEN the first RUNNING frame's `displayed_pm` equals the formula's target within 1 pm (the open gap is dropped), differs from the pre-save value by the gap that was still open (at most `DISC_CAP_PM`), and `lie_kind`, `leak_modes`, `lie_id` and every per-lie constant (`rate_nom`, `D_eff`, `s`, `f_w`, `phi_w`, tick mode, `T_s`) are identical to their pre-save values. `[PF-11, PF-33, D.3, E-PF-63]`
- **AC-PF-47 (auto) [MVP-live]**: GIVEN a lie ACTIVE and a step of 500 pm in the true value WHEN frames are sampled THEN the displayed value moves at no more than `R_act` (8.33 pm/s at 240 deg), never snaps, and reconverges to the formula within `gap / R_act`; AND GIVEN a restore at any point THEN the first frame equals the formula (within the `DISC_CAP_PM` clamp, AC-PF-114). `[D.3, E-PF-8]`
- **AC-PF-114 (auto) [MVP-live]** *(Amended 2026-10-27 — design review)*: GIVEN a GLIDE lie with offset +100 pm and the true value ramping +100 pm/s for 10 s WHEN frames are sampled THEN the displayed value moves at no more than `R_act` until displayed-minus-true reaches -400 pm (5.5 s +- 0.2 s), is then pinned at `DISC_CAP_PM` (never beyond -400 pm plus the wander), follows the truth continuously (no per-frame step above 100 pm/s x dt + `R_act` x dt), and the leak stays at or above `floor_eff`; GIVEN a true step of 600 pm THEN the displayed value ends within `DISC_CAP_PM` of the truth on the next frame and then follows the slew; GIVEN `u_true` 950, `s` +1, `delta` 100 pm and a +100 repair that pins the truth at 1000 THEN the target never leaves `[W_M, 1000 - W_M]`, the needle never pins on a stop, the wander runs at full amplitude, `lie_short_reach` increments once, and GLEAM, SOUND and the wander are all present. `[D.3, E-PF-8, E-PF-10]`
- **AC-PF-116 (auto) [MVP-live]** *(Amended 2026-10-27 — design review)*: GIVEN FALSE_OK with the real zone interval `[800, 1000]` (`W_M` and `m_z` 21.9) and `u_true` 650 WHEN the lie arrives THEN the held band lies inside the zone and `lies_zone_missed` stays 0; GIVEN `u_true` 350 THEN the band misses the zone and `lies_zone_missed` is 1; GIVEN `u_true` 900 (already inside) THEN `u_aim` equals `u_true` and the needle does not move toward a point outside the zone; GIVEN the real zone intervals of every FALSE_OK and FALSE_FAIL surface in `calibration_fixtures.json` THEN `lies_zone_missed` is 0 whenever the distance to the zone edge plus `m_z` is at most `DISC_CAP_PM`. `[PF-13, D.3]`
- **AC-PF-59 (auto) [MVP-live]** *(Amended 2026-10-27 — design review)*: GIVEN two entries with the same `subject_ref` and different `start_run` WHEN drawn THEN only the lower `start_run` contributes pixels and sound and `lie_collisions` is 1; GIVEN equal `start_run` THEN the entry earlier in the `sanity.perception` lies list wins; the outcome is identical across 10 reorderings of unrelated entries. `[PF-46, E-PF-6]`
- **AC-PF-64 (auto) [MVP-live]** *(Amended 2026-10-27 — design review)*: GIVEN a held ACTIVE lie with `abs(disc) >= D_VIS_MIN_DEG` WHEN the `Perceived` record is serialised THEN it has exactly the ten PF-8 fields within their ranges (`displayed_pm` int 0..1000, `leak_amplitude_pm` int 0..1000, `leak_modes` a bitmask over {1, 2, 4}, `tremor_weight_pm` 0 after `TREMOR_BLEND_S`, `lie_id` non-zero), no field is named `true_*`, and the record holds no reference to the Graph or Suit value object or to `u_true`; and for a remote viewer the same ref returns the honest record. (The earlier clause "no field equals the true reading" was unsound: a wandering needle can pass through the truth.) `[PF-8, PF-10]`
- **AC-PF-71 (auto) [MVP-live]**: GIVEN an injected fault (a ref with no tag, a missing calibration record, an unavailable `true_value`, a thrown exception inside a wrap, a lie entry for a destroyed subject) WHEN the surface is drawn THEN it draws the honest path, the matching counter increments, no fault reaches the simulation or a TRUTH node, and for the destroyed subject the ref draws nothing, plays no return animation and its per-lie state is freed the same frame. `[PF-44, E-PF-9, E-PF-11]`
- **AC-PF-78 (auto) [MVP-live]**: GIVEN a lie accepted by Sanity and PENDING for the whole `ONSET_DELAY_RUNS` WHEN frames, audio requests and counters are compared with a no-lie run THEN they are identical (no hint); a debunk during PENDING leaves no trace; a save during PENDING restores and the lie activates on its original schedule with `lie_tau` 0 at activation. `[PF-12, E-PF-1, E-PF-22]`
- **AC-PF-100 (auto) [MVP-live]**: GIVEN two `perceived` calls for one `(ref, viewer, frame)` THEN one evaluation runs (memo hit counter 1); GIVEN an honest ref whose true value steps THEN the honest memo is invalidated by the notification and the next frame shows the new value (an honest gauge never lags its settle); GIVEN the instrument scene destroyed and re-instantiated THEN `ref_key` and the hash draws are unchanged. `[PF-7, PF-9, PF-37]`
- **AC-PF-101 (lint + auto stub) [MVP-stub]**: GIVEN every CORRUPTIBLE dial or readout tag THEN it carries `arc_deg`, `needle_len_px`, zone intervals and `lsb`, its needle is a separate sprite of at least 16 art px, a `_msk` slice and both honest state frames on one sheet page exist; a missing record fails the build for any kind in `lie_targets.json` and increments `lie_unrenderable` at runtime. A stub Instruments applies settle and tremor only when `motion_mode` is HONEST and returns to them at CLEAR. `[PF-7, PF-45]`

### B. Tag contract, write set and truth (Logic lints in `lint_*.gd`; captures Integration)

- **AC-PF-5a (lint)**: GIVEN the built scenes and materials WHEN linted THEN no TRUTH_PX node's material carries a leak parameter default other than rest, and a TRUTH_ST node differs only in `edge_bleed_pm`, on a sheet with the edge channel set and the emission channel clear. **AC-PF-5b (auto)**: GIVEN a scripted 10-minute stage-3 session with four lies WHEN every TRUTH node's leak parameters are read every frame THEN all are at rest (TRUTH_ST: only `edge_bleed_pm`, only on a permitted sheet). `[PF-4, PF-6, PF-24]`
- **AC-PF-6 (lint) [MVP-live]**: GIVEN `lie_targets.json` WHEN the coverage lint runs THEN every module kind listed has at least one tagged CORRUPTIBLE surface in the MVP content, and every CORRUPTIBLE tag carries a `lie_surface_id` present in `lie_targets.json`. `[PF-4, PF-6 (i), (vi)]`
- **AC-PF-7 (lint) [MVP-live]**: GIVEN the wrapped-sprite registry WHEN linted THEN it contains no seam-lane PIPE or DUCT valve id and no CABLE breaker id until Graph ASK G8 and F&T ASK FT1 publish a witness (AC-MSA-126). `[PF-5 row 12]`
- **AC-PF-8 (lint) [MVP-live]** *(Amended 2026-10-27 — re-review)* (adds PF-6 (vii): zero `set_shader_parameter` calls on a material referenced by more than one node): GIVEN the Filter source WHEN the write-set lint runs THEN it has zero writes to any `Camera2D`, `Viewport`, `SubViewport` or `SubViewportContainer` property (the pipeline root's assignment of the Filter material excepted), zero writes to any real-light member, imports no simulation module, writes no block, registers no hook or mutator, calls no `register_subject` and submits no command (AC-IA-85, the PIPE hook-block write lint, LO-25 (ii)). `[PF-1, PF-2, PF-3, PF-6 (iv), (v)]`
- **AC-PF-9 (render) [MVP-live]**: GIVEN stage 3 with a lie ACTIVE WHEN captures are taken with the Filter on and off THEN every TRUTH_PX element (trails and their emissive layers, the beam and the floor pool, character sprites and hand tools, the HUD frame, consequence VFX, Attention outputs) is bit-identical, enumerated from the node group `truth_px` generated from the tags, with debanding dither disabled by the debug flag `--filter-test-freeze-dither` (build assertion: the flag is absent from release). `[PF-4, PF-5; PIPE hook block bullet 2]`

- **AC-PF-51 (lint) [MVP-live]**: GIVEN the source tree WHEN the caller lint runs THEN the only non-diagnostic call site of Graph `true_value(kind, id)` and of Suit `true_value(key, player_id)` is the Filter's wrap module; the Debug Overlay is the only other caller; HUD and Instruments modules hold zero `true_value` call sites and call `perceived`; the Filter holds no reference to `sanity.state` or `sanity.ledger`. `[PF-3, LS-19, LS-22, SE-29, SN-23]`

- **AC-PF-57a (auto) [MVP-live]** *(Amended 2026-10-27 — design review)*: GIVEN the scene tree at stages 0 to 3 WHEN scanned THEN every node the Filter created is of a registered leak type (leak material host, MultiMesh overlay, rim node, outline node), and a **write spy** on the HUD tree records zero Filter-sourced writes to any `CanvasItem` colour, alpha, visibility or material (the earlier node-name scan against a banned-word list was unsound: it passes any node that is simply named differently). **AC-PF-57b (review, art-director)** *(Amended 2026-10-27 — re-review)*: GIVEN the retained screenshots at stages 0 to 3 THEN the art-director ticks each of the twelve numbered PF-38 Never-list items (1 to 12) in `production/qa/evidence/perception-filter/never-list-checklist.md` (no meter, bar, ring, number, icon or colour; no vignette, tunnel, blur, aberration, shake, scan-line, glitch, desaturation or grade; no verdict, banner or relief cue; no Alarm Red or "safe" colour in a lie; no self-tell on the body; no consequence-channel addition; no Attention visual; no phantom in the dark); retained. `[PF-38, PF-22]`

### C. Lie trajectory, leak, end and lifecycle (Logic; formulas D.1 to D.6, D.17)

- **AC-PF-45 (auto) [MVP-live]**: GIVEN an entry that becomes ACTIVE WHEN the first ACTIVE frame renders at 30 and 60 fps THEN the displayed-minus-true discrepancy is below `LEAK_EPS_DEG` (at most 0.1 deg), the wander offset is 0, `tremor_weight_pm` is 1000 and then falls linearly to 0 over `TREMOR_BLEND_S`, and the leak is at least `floor_eff` (400 at stages 0 to 1, 325 at stage 2, 250 at stage 3) *(Amended 2026-10-27 — user rulings)*; and GIVEN a restore with the activation run later than the current tick count THEN `lie_tau` clamps at 0. `[D.1, D.4]`
- **AC-PF-15 (auto) [MVP-live]**: GIVEN a lie sampled from onset at 144 fps WHEN the drift component is differenced THEN its angular speed never exceeds `GLIDE_RATE_MAX_DEG` and the per-frame change never exceeds `rate x dt` (no snap); the wander is excluded and tested separately. `[PF-13, D.3]`
- **AC-PF-52 (auto) [MVP-live]** *(Amended 2026-10-27 — design review)*: GIVEN 1000 draws from the committed hash fixture (a constant file of `(viewer, ref_key, start_run)` triples, never the global RNG), `L_s` in {40, 80, 120}, `needle_len_px` in {16, 24, 32}, `rm` in {1.0, 0.85, 0.7} *(Amended 2026-10-27 — re-review)* WHEN D.2 runs THEN `rate_nom` is in `[rate_floor, GLIDE_RATE_MAX_DEG]`, `rate` equals `max(0.3, rm x rate_nom)` within 1e-9, **`D_eff` is identical for the three `rm` values** (reduced motion is not a difficulty slider), `D_eff >= D_VIS_MIN_DEG - 1e-6` (expected minimum 24.0 deg for GLIDE; 12.0 appears only at the `rm_min x D_DRAW_MIN_DEG` guard boundary), `D_eff <= GLIDE_REACH_FRAC x rate_nom x L_s + 1e-6`, and `t_arrive(rm) / t_arrive(1.0)` equals `1 / rm` within 1e-6 wherever the rate floor does not bind, and at `rm` 0.7 `t_arrive <= 0.9 x L_s` for every drawn lie, so a FALSE_* lie that is reach-bound arrives at 0.857 `L_s` and is held in its zone for at least `ZONE_DWELL_MIN_S` (4.0 s, 5.7 s at the 40 s life) before expiry *(Amended 2026-10-27 — re-review)*; AND GIVEN `needle_len_px` 14 THEN the surface is not lied on (E-PF-11), `lie_unrenderable` increments and no gleam is drawn. `[PF-13, PF-17, D.2, E-PF-11, E-PF-56]`
- **AC-PF-82 (auto) [MVP-live]** *(Amended 2026-10-27 — design review)*: GIVEN `H(1, 0x1234, 7, 0)`, `arc_deg` 240 and `needle_len_px` 24 WHEN D.2 runs for GLIDE with `L_s` 40, 80, 120 THEN (`rate_nom`, `D_eff`, `t_arrive`) = (2.0, 48, 24.0), (1.349, 64.75, 48.0), (1.192, 64.75, 54.3) within 0.01, with `lies_partial` incrementing for the first row only; for FALSE_OK and FALSE_FAIL THEN (2.0, 48, 24.0), (2.0, 96, 48.0), (1.333, 96, 72.0); under `rm` 0.7 the six `t_arrive` are 34.3, 68.6, 77.6, 34.3, 68.6 and 102.9 s (+- 0.1) with the same `D_eff`, each at most 0.9 `L_s`, with the displayed interval held in the zone for at least 4.0 s before expiry on the three FALSE rows (the `L_s` 40 s row 5.7 s) *(Amended 2026-10-27 — re-review)*; WHEN the truth sits at a dial stop THEN the target is clamped live, `D_eff` is unchanged, `lie_short_reach` increments once, and the gleam, the sound and the wander all remain (E-PF-10). `[PF-13, D.2, E-PF-10, E-PF-56]`
- **AC-PF-81 (auto) [MVP-live]**: GIVEN `arc_deg` 240, `rate` 1.19, `D_eff` 24 deg, side +1, kind GLIDE, `u_true` 700 WHEN sampled THEN `target_pm` = 749.6 +- 0.5 at `lie_tau` 10 s and 800 from 20.2 s; WHEN `u_true` steps to 650 at 25 s THEN the target is 750 and the displayed value reaches it after 6.0 +- 0.1 s with no per-frame move above `R_act x dt`; GIVEN FALSE_OK with the zone interval `[800, 1000]` (`m_z` 21.9) and `D_eff` 400 pm THEN `u_true` 650 holds 821.9, `u_true` 400 stops at 800 (the cap; a weak lie that Sanity's gate should have refused) and `u_true` 350 stops at 750 and counts `lies_zone_missed`; when `u_true` enters the zone the aim equals `u_true` and the target has no step above 1 pm per tick *(Amended 2026-10-27 — design review)*; and the leak stays at or above `floor_eff` while the needle meets the truth; GIVEN a GLIDE lie at `disc` 96 deg and a truth step toward the needle that collapses `disc` to 0 THEN `amp` falls from 1000 at no more than 266.7 pm/s and is 400 after 2.25 s +- one frame, with no single-frame change above `R_AMP_ACT x dt` *(Amended 2026-10-27 — re-review)*. `[PF-13, D.3, D.5, E-PF-7, E-PF-8]`
- **AC-PF-86 (auto) [MVP-live]**: GIVEN a 0.5 s render hitch WHEN the slew and ramps advance THEN `dt` is clamped to `DT_SLEW_MAX_S` 0.1, no frame's displayed move exceeds `R_act x 0.1` (0.83 pm at 240 deg) plus one frame of wander, `lie_tau` advances by the true 0.5 s and no flash pair is produced. `[D.3, E-PF-26]`
- **AC-PF-46 (auto) [MVP-live]** *(Amended 2026-10-27 — design review)*: GIVEN a held lie at `needle_len_px` 24 sampled for 120 s at 60 fps, at 20 values of the target's fractional pixel on each axis, at **needle angles 0 to 90 degrees in 15-degree steps**, and at `WANDER_F_MIN` and `WANDER_F_MAX` WHEN the quantised tip position is logged THEN it changes at least every `NEVER_REST_MAX_S` 1.0 s at every angle (analytic worst case 0.959 s at 45 degrees and 0.791 s at 0 and 90); GIVEN the first ACTIVE frame (onset, drift disabled, 24 wander phases) THEN the longest rest is at most 1.25 s (measured worst case 1.15 s) and never above `ONSET_REST_MAX_S` 2.0 s; the same holds under reduced motion (the wander is not scaled); and an honest tremor in progress at onset fades through `tremor_weight_pm` from 1000 to 0 over `TREMOR_BLEND_S` and never restarts. `[PF-17, D.4]`
- **AC-PF-85 (auto) [MVP-live]**: GIVEN a lie ACTIVE THEN `motion_mode` is GLIDE, TICK, STALE or BREATH (never HONEST) and a stub Instruments applies no settle spring and no *new* tremor (a tremor already running at onset fades through `tremor_weight_pm` from 1000 to 0 over `TREMOR_BLEND_S`, so it is not zero on frame one) *(Amended 2026-10-27 — design review)*; GIVEN an honest gauge THEN `motion_mode` is HONEST and there is no glide; the wander frequency is within [0.40, 0.45] Hz, disjoint from 6 to 10 Hz, and `abs(w_px) <= WANDER_AMP_PX`. `[PF-17, D.4]`
- **AC-PF-53 (auto) [MVP-live]**: GIVEN a lie ending with a wander offset of 2 art px WHEN the entry leaves THEN the displayed value has no step larger than one frame's slew plus one frame's wander motion (the wander fades out over `WANDER_BLEND_S`). `[D.4, D.6]`

### D. Stage, fringes, geometry and flash (Capture and Visual evidence)

- **AC-PF-13 (auto + render for the pixel half) [MVP-live]**: GIVEN the first RUNNING frame after LOADING at stage 2 with an ACTIVE reach lie (a housing or glyph lie, so the LIE_BOUND rim is on) WHEN presented THEN the rim fringe is at steady amplitude, `r_RIM` = 0.600 +- 0.001 on the first RUNNING frame (a stage-2 restore with no reach lie shows no rim, `r_RIM` = 0.000) *(Amended 2026-10-27 — user rulings)* *(Amended 2026-10-27 — re-review)*, `r_FLOOR` = 0.500 +- 0.001 (`floor_eff` 325 +- 1), the lie draws full leak (`amp` at or above 325), `perceived` was answerable before that frame and no frame was presented unfiltered (AC-SL-124). `[PF-33]`

### E. Readouts, audio and accessibility

- **AC-PF-22 (auto) [MVP-live]**: GIVEN a lying readout sampled for 1000 ticks WHEN transitions are logged THEN the interval is at least `LIE_TICK_MS` and the rate is at most 3 per second (AC-SN-149); the 334 ms floor is asserted. Under the ruled counting unit `ceil(n_transitions / 2)` (registry `flash_count`) 3 transitions per second count as 2 flash events; the 334 ms floor is Sanity's AC-SN-149 transition cap and is not loosened (user ruling X9/D91) *(Amended 2026-10-27 — user rulings)*. `[PF-18, D.9]`
- **AC-PF-75 (auto) [MVP-live]**: GIVEN `N_r` = 1 to 4 lying readouts in one ninth of the picture WHEN ticks are logged for 60 s THEN total transitions in any rolling 1 s are at most `FLASH_MAX_PER_S` and `T_tick` equals {400, 668, 1002, 1336} ms with consecutive transitions at least 334 ms apart. `[PF-18, D.9]`

- **AC-PF-75c (auto) [MVP-live]** *(Amended 2026-10-27 — re-review)*: GIVEN reduce-flashing ON (STRICT) and `N_r` = 3 lying readouts plus one honest numeral in the same region WHEN transitions are logged for 60 s THEN lying ticks are never deferred or dropped by the arbiter (334 ms apart, 2.994 per second), the sum of lying and honest transitions in any rolling second is at most 3, every honest swap that the window cannot hold is skipped to its next grid boundary by the HUD arbiter (skip-only, never delayed), and the Filter's own governor logs no honest-swap deferral; GIVEN WEIGHTED mode THEN honest digit swaps are exempt and appear at every grid boundary. `[PF-18, D.9, E-PF-65, hud_flash_budget]`
- **AC-PF-102 (auto) [MVP-live]**: GIVEN kind GLIDE on a readout THEN the digit changes on every scheduled tick (`q_k != q_{k-1}`); GIVEN FALSE_OK or FALSE_FAIL THEN the digit holds the false target while the true value changes; honest numerals change once per true change and never tween; with `N_r` 3 the transitions are 334 ms apart; GIVEN a lie ending THEN the numeral keeps stepping on the scheduler to `round(slew_value / lsb)` during RETURN and switches to the honest grid only at CLEAR, never jumping to the honest value; a STALE numeral equals the displayed needle value at its last tick, holds from arrival (never the zone centre while the needle is short of it) and stays inside the range, with the `lsb` step flipping sign at a range edge *(Amended 2026-10-27 — design review)*. `[PF-18, D.9]`

- **AC-PF-76 (spike)** *(Amended 2026-10-27 — design review)*: **S-1a** (yes/no, editor check, no reference hardware, before the gleam stories): the per-instance shader-parameter API exists on `CanvasItem` in 4.7.2; if not, path 1 is withdrawn at once. **S-1b** (batching): `set_instance_shader_parameter` on a lying instrument keeps 24 lying instruments within +1 batch of the baseline on both reference machines `[blocked: reference hardware]` and on each backend of the matrix (D3D12, Vulkan, Compatibility) *(Amended 2026-10-27 — re-review)*. **S-1c** (overlay) *(Amended 2026-10-27 — re-review)*: a `MultiMeshInstance2D` gleam overlay is tested against hosts at several y-sort depths, on two or more atlas pages and with mixed light masks; it draws all gleams in at most `G_MM` draws (one per distinct depth band, atlas page and light mask, at most 6) for 1 to 24 lying surfaces, keeps the gleam lit with its host, samples the host's `_msk` slice through the packed custom data (PF-42), and orders correctly against an occluding sprite in front of the host. S-1 is an **early gating spike** (before any gleam story) with a recorded contingency, the reduced lie set (PF-42). Time-box 2 working days; deliverable an ADR "leak transport: instance parameter vs MultiMesh vs not lied on" (ADR candidate 9); until then the budget in AC-PF-43 is conditional. `[PF-42, UNV]`
- **AC-PF-108 (auto + lint) [MVP-live]** *(Amended 2026-10-27 — design review)*: GIVEN path 2 and then path 3 forced by fixture THEN at most `D_FX_ADDED_MAX` 10 draws are added, **every ACTIVE lie that is drawn still has GLEAM plus a hue-independent motion mode** (PF-15 table), `filter_draws_dropped` counts the lies not drawn (path 3: 1 of 2 at stage 2 and 1 of 4 at stage 3, the oldest surviving), no lie is drawn with `leak_modes & GLEAM == 0` or without its motion mode, and the lint adds a fallback branch to `lint_leak_palette.gd` that fails the build for any code path that draws a lie while its gleam transport is unavailable; `self_modulate` appears in no Filter code path. `[PF-42, PF-15, E-PF-50, E-PF-51]`
- **AC-PF-77 (auto) [MVP-live]** *(Amended 2026-10-27 — design review)*: GIVEN the Filter's material registry keeps a counter `variant_first_use_after_loading` WHEN LOADING completes THEN the LOADING log lists every leak variant instantiated once off-screen under the black hold, and in SCN-PF-2 (a scripted 10-minute run that enters stage 3 and triggers every leak variant for the first time) the counter stays 0; the frame-time check `max(3 x median, median + 8 ms)` is retained as an advisory measurement. `[PF-43]`
- **AC-PF-111 (auto) [MVP-live]** *(Amended 2026-10-27 — design review)* *(Amended 2026-10-27 — re-review)*: GIVEN a LINEAR sight-glass with `travel_px` 40 WHEN D.2 to D.6 run in per-mille space THEN `r_floor_pm_s` = 12.5 +- 0.01, the rate ceiling is 50 and the return rate 75 pm/s, `D_DRAW_MIN` = 100 pm, `D_vis_pm` = 100 pm, `W_M` = 55.0 pm with the target clamped to [55, 945], `LEAK_EPS` = 1.04 pm and `LEAK_D_FULL` = 83.3 pm, the wander moves the meniscus row by `WANDER_AMP_PX` 2.2 px (worst dwell 0.79 s at 0.40 Hz), the D.6 return assertion holds (75 >= 50), the linear visible-in-time condition gives 11.4 s <= 24 s at `rm` 0.7, the worked vector of PF-45 gives `rate_nom` 16.67 pm/s, `D_eff` 400 pm and `t_arrive` 24.0 s (34.3 s at `rm` 0.7); the same at `travel_px` 50 gives 10, 40 and 60 pm/s, `D_vis_pm` 80 and `W_M` 44.0 pm; a surface under `TRAVEL_MIN_PX` 40 (for example 36) is not lied on unless its ref has a visible digit mechanism (Instruments' constants are cited, never re-valued). `[PF-45]`
- **AC-PF-112 (lint + auto) [MVP-live]** *(Amended 2026-10-27 — design review)*: GIVEN every row of `lie_targets_fixture.json` and every `subject_ref` form in the PF-9 join table WHEN resolved THEN each row resolves to exactly one `PerceptionRef` and each ref maps back to exactly one row (a bijection on (module `stable_id`, surface kind, quantity) *(Amended 2026-10-27 — re-review)*, vector: two compartment gauges in one room, `temp` and `pressure`, resolve to two refs and two ledger keys), no `ref_key` is at or above `0xFFFF0000`, and a WORN row fails until a ref type exists. `[PF-9, OQ-PF-38]`
- **AC-PF-113 (auto) [MVP-live]** *(Amended 2026-10-27 — design review)*: GIVEN 80 preallocated `Perceived` slots WHEN each is written in turn with a unique pattern THEN no other slot changes (no two refs alias), a lying ref and an honest ref never share a slot, the honest path returns the same slot every call, and `record_constructions` is 0 over a 10 000-frame run. `[PF-7, D.13]`
- **AC-PF-115 (auto) [MVP-live]** *(Amended 2026-10-27 — design review)* *(Amended 2026-10-27 — re-review, delegated ruling R8)*: GIVEN 1 to 4 lying instruments at `amp` 1000 WHEN the ON and LEVEL requests are logged THEN each lie's request equals its own `amp_snd` (1000 for each of the four; no request exceeds 1000, none is scaled by another lie and the Filter applies no sum cap, the combined cap being Audio's), ON carries `(LEVEL, T_s, SOUND_BREATH_FLOOR_PM)` with `T_s` in [4000, 6000] ms (5473 ms for the vector lie), a static-pair lie's LEVEL carries no `breath01` factor (vector: gleam `amp` at its breath trough 400 while LEVEL is 1000), and an off-screen or unlit lie still issues its request (no visibility gating). `[PF-20, D.15]`

### Gaps / untestable as written

These cannot be signed off by wording alone; each is carried as an Open Question with an owner.

- **AC-PF-43b and AC-PF-76**: the two reference machines (min-spec and 2160p) are not named anywhere (OQ-PF-25); until the technical-director names them both are `[blocked: reference hardware]`.
- **AC-PF-26 and AC-PF-95**: *(closed 2026-10-27)* the alarm sweep is the registered `BEACON_SWEEP_TICKS` (OQ-PF-13), so the dead-zone code now has a live input for the sweep.
- **AC-PF-27**: `BREATH_LOCK_CEIL` 0.30 is a `[PLACEHOLDER]` pending the systems-designer's ratification (OQ-PF-24); the criterion cannot be signed off with a number nobody owns.
- **AC-PF-56 and AC-PF-93**: blocked until Lighting accepts the extra default-off block in HullSurface and Emissive and the LO-13 ratification ADR resolves (OQ-PF-5).
- **AC-PF-23 and AC-PF-101**: In-World Instruments is designed but its wall-gauge and card nodes are not built; the criteria run against `StubInstruments` until they are *(Amended 2026-10-27 — design review)*.
- **All `(render)` criteria**: depend on a rendering-capable CI lane that the project test setup does not yet have (OQ-PF-26); in the headless lane they report `SKIPPED-NO-RENDERER`, and PRE-R turns a blank or draw-less baseline into SKIPPED, never PASS *(Amended 2026-10-27 — design review)*.
- **AC-PF-1 and AC-PF-113**: GDScript allocation accounting is `[UNV]` (the engine reference has no `scripting.md` or `core.md`); both are rewritten as identity and construction-counter checks that need no allocation accounting *(Amended 2026-10-27 — design review)*.
- **AC-PF-29a to 29c**: the per-tester threshold (7 of 8 testers at 7/10 or better, control 9/10) and the 8-tester minimum are the QA proposal, to be ratified by the creative-director *(Amended 2026-10-27 — design review)*.
- **Rim (PF-23) and hue bleed (PF-24)**: if either is cut, the story needs the absence variant as its evidence (the conditional criteria above state it).

## Open Questions

Numbering is authoritative for this document; the body cites `OQ-PF-n` by these ids. Rows 1 to 3 are the user-decision items (nobody could answer them in the autonomous run, so each carried the logged recommendation that was in force until the user ruled). *(Amended 2026-10-27 — user rulings)* OQ-PF-1, -2, -12, -31 and -37 were resolved by the user's rulings of 2026-10-27 (design-review decisions); OQ-PF-25 (the reference machines) stays OPEN under X14. **Owner** is `user`, an agent role, or a GDD (the neighbour that must answer when it is designed); **Target** is `playtest`, `next GDD`, `ADR`, or `spike`. Upstream ids carried here follow X-7: OQ-SN-21, -22, -23, -26, -28 and -29 are carried; OQ-SN-17, -24 and -25 are answered in the body (PF-34, PF-14 with D.6, PF-28). The X-3 stale-token sweep (20 routed refresh rows) is a routing list, not a question: this GDD edits none of those documents (file ownership), and the owners listed in X-3 apply the refreshes. Sweep result on this GDD: `Light2D` appears only in the negative or in quotations of stale clauses (PF-2 edge note); `pipe flow` only in quotations of LO-14; no `stage 4`, `213x120` or `perception.state` in live rule text.
